

========== ./src/io/checkpoint.cpp ==========
// src/io/checkpoint.cpp
//
// Implementation of ModelWriter and ModelReader for `.l3` binary serialization.

#include "log3/io/checkpoint.h"

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>

#include "log3/core/bit_ops.h"
#include "log3/core/scope_guard.h"

namespace l3 {
namespace io {

namespace {

constexpr char kMagicString[16] = "LOG3_MODEL_V01\0";
constexpr u32 kCurrentVersion = 1;
constexpr u32 kEndianTag = 0x01020304u;

struct Header {
  char magic[16];
  u32 version;
  u32 endian_tag;
  u64 tensor_count;
  u64 meta_offset;
  u64 meta_size;
  u8 reserved[16];
};

static_assert(sizeof(Header) == 64, "Header must be exactly 64 bytes");
static_assert(sizeof(TensorMeta) == 224, "TensorMeta must be 224 bytes");

// Bounded strlen that never reads past `max_cap` bytes if a corrupted file
// omits a null terminator inside `TensorMeta::name`.
usize safe_name_len(const char* s, usize max_cap) noexcept {
  usize n = 0;
  while (n < max_cap && s[n] != '\0') {
    ++n;
  }
  return n;
}

}  // namespace

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ModelWriter
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Status ModelWriter::add_tensor(const char* name, const Tensor& tensor) noexcept {
  if (name == nullptr || name[0] == '\0' || !tensor.is_valid()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "ModelWriter::add_tensor: invalid name or Tensor");
  }
  const usize name_len = std::strlen(name);
  if (name_len >= sizeof(TensorMeta::name)) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
        "ModelWriter::add_tensor: tensor name '%s' exceeds 127 characters",
        name);
  }
  if (!tensor.is_contiguous()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "ModelWriter::add_tensor: tensor must be contiguous");
  }

  // Reject duplicate tensor names upfront so a checkpoint never contains
  // shadowed entries.
  for (Index i = 0; i < entries_.size(); ++i) {
    if (std::strcmp(entries_[i].name, name) == 0) {
      return Status::errorf(
          StatusCode::kAlreadyExists,
          "ModelWriter::add_tensor: duplicate tensor name '%s'", name);
    }
  }

  WriteEntry entry{};
  std::memcpy(entry.name, name, name_len + 1);
  entry.tensor = tensor.view();
  return entries_.push_back(std::move(entry));
}

Status ModelWriter::save(const char* filepath) const noexcept {
  if (filepath == nullptr || filepath[0] == '\0') {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "ModelWriter::save: invalid filepath");
  }

  std::FILE* fp = std::fopen(filepath, "wb");
  if (fp == nullptr) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kIoError,
        "ModelWriter::save: failed to open file '%s' for writing", filepath);
  }
  L3_DEFER { std::fclose(fp); };

  Header hdr{};
  std::memcpy(hdr.magic, kMagicString, 16);
  hdr.version = kCurrentVersion;
  hdr.endian_tag = kEndianTag;
  hdr.tensor_count = static_cast<u64>(entries_.size());
  hdr.meta_offset = 0;
  hdr.meta_size = 0;
  mem_zero(hdr.reserved, 16);

  if (std::fwrite(&hdr, sizeof(Header), 1, fp) != 1) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kIoError,
                         "ModelWriter::save: write header failed");
  }

  SmallVec<TensorMeta, 256> metas;
  Size current_pos = sizeof(Header);

  for (Index i = 0; i < entries_.size(); ++i) {
    const auto& entry = entries_[i];
    const Tensor& t = entry.tensor;

    // Align every tensor payload to 64 bytes (kDefaultAlignment) so SIMD AVX-512
    // and AVX2 loads on memory-mapped tensors are always cache-line aligned.
    const Size aligned_pos = align_up(current_pos, kDefaultAlignment);
    const Size pad_bytes = aligned_pos - current_pos;
    if (pad_bytes > 0) {
      byte zero_pad[64]{};
      if (std::fwrite(zero_pad, 1, pad_bytes, fp) != pad_bytes) {
        return Status::error(StatusCode::kIoError,
                             "ModelWriter::save: failed writing alignment pad");
      }
      current_pos = aligned_pos;
    }

    TensorMeta meta{};
    std::memcpy(meta.name, entry.name, sizeof(meta.name));
    meta.dtype = t.dtype();
    meta.rank = static_cast<u8>(t.rank());
    for (Index d = 0; d < t.rank(); ++d) {
      meta.dims[d] = t.dim(d);
    }
    meta.quant_scale = t.quant_scale();
    meta.data_offset = current_pos;
    meta.data_bytes = t.nbytes();

    if (t.nbytes() > 0) {
      if (std::fwrite(t.raw_data(), 1, t.nbytes(), fp) != t.nbytes()) {
        return Status::error(StatusCode::kIoError,
                             "ModelWriter::save: failed writing tensor payload");
      }
      current_pos += t.nbytes();
    }

    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(metas.push_back(meta));
  }

  // Align the metadata directory table to `alignof(TensorMeta)` (8 bytes) so
  // reading `TensorMeta` records from the memory-mapped file never triggers
  // misaligned struct access UB when the final tensor payload is not a multiple
  // of 8 bytes.
  const Size aligned_meta_pos = align_up(current_pos, alignof(TensorMeta));
  const Size meta_pad_bytes = aligned_meta_pos - current_pos;
  if (meta_pad_bytes > 0) {
    byte zero_pad[8]{};
    if (std::fwrite(zero_pad, 1, meta_pad_bytes, fp) != meta_pad_bytes) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kIoError,
                           "ModelWriter::save: failed writing meta padding");
    }
    current_pos = aligned_meta_pos;
  }

  const Size meta_offset = current_pos;
  const Size meta_bytes = static_cast<Size>(metas.size()) * sizeof(TensorMeta);

  if (meta_bytes > 0) {
    if (std::fwrite(metas.data(), sizeof(TensorMeta),
                    static_cast<usize>(metas.size()),
                    fp) != static_cast<usize>(metas.size())) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kIoError,
                           "ModelWriter::save: failed writing metadata table");
    }
  }

  hdr.meta_offset = meta_offset;
  hdr.meta_size = meta_bytes;
  if (std::fseek(fp, 0, SEEK_SET) != 0 ||
      std::fwrite(&hdr, sizeof(Header), 1, fp) != 1 || std::fflush(fp) != 0) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kIoError,
                         "ModelWriter::save: rewrite header failed");
  }

  return Status::ok();
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ModelReader
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Result<ModelReader> ModelReader::open(const char* filepath, bool prefetch,
                                      const Allocator& allocator) noexcept {
  MMapFile mf;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(mf, MMapFile::open(filepath, prefetch));

  if (mf.size() < sizeof(Header)) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "ModelReader::open: file too small for log3 header");
  }

  Header hdr{};
  std::memcpy(&hdr, mf.data(), sizeof(Header));
  if (std::memcmp(hdr.magic, kMagicString, 16) != 0) {
    return Status::error(
        StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
        "ModelReader::open: invalid file magic (not an .l3 checkpoint)");
  }
  if (hdr.version != kCurrentVersion || hdr.endian_tag != kEndianTag) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "ModelReader::open: unsupported version or byte order");
  }

  // Overflow-checked validation of tensor_count, meta_size, and meta_offset.
  if (hdr.tensor_count > static_cast<u64>(kMaxIndex)) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                         "ModelReader::open: tensor_count overflow");
  }
  Size expected_meta_bytes = 0;
  if (!checked_mul(static_cast<Size>(hdr.tensor_count), sizeof(TensorMeta),
                   &expected_meta_bytes) ||
      expected_meta_bytes != static_cast<Size>(hdr.meta_size)) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "ModelReader::open: corrupt meta_size in header");
  }
  Size meta_end = 0;
  if (!checked_add(static_cast<Size>(hdr.meta_offset), expected_meta_bytes,
                   &meta_end) ||
      meta_end > mf.size()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                         "ModelReader::open: metadata table exceeds file size");
  }

  // Allocate MMapHolder via default_allocator() so its RefCounted header is
  // always backed by a static-lifetime allocator.
  Ptr<MMapHolder> holder;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(holder,
                      make_ptr_in<MMapHolder>(default_allocator(), std::move(mf)));

  ModelReader reader;
  reader.holder_ = std::move(holder);
  L3_UNUSED(allocator);
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(
      reader.meta_map_.reserve(static_cast<Index>(hdr.tensor_count)));

  const byte* raw_metas =
      reader.holder_->file().data() + static_cast<Size>(hdr.meta_offset);

  for (u64 i = 0; i < hdr.tensor_count; ++i) {
    const byte* record_ptr = raw_metas + static_cast<Size>(i) * sizeof(TensorMeta);
    TensorMeta m{};
    std::memcpy(&m, record_ptr, sizeof(TensorMeta));
    m.name[sizeof(m.name) - 1] = '\0';

    // CRITICAL FIX: Construct the StrView key pointing into the persistent
    // memory-mapped file (`record_ptr`), NOT into a temporary stack variable!
    // The MMapFile stays alive inside `reader.holder_` for the entire lifetime
    // of `ModelReader`.
    const char* mapped_name = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(record_ptr);
    const usize name_len = safe_name_len(mapped_name, sizeof(m.name) - 1);
    const StrView key(mapped_name, static_cast<Index>(name_len));

    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(reader.meta_map_.put(key, m));
  }

  return reader;
}

Result<Tensor> ModelReader::get_tensor(StrView name) const noexcept {
  const TensorMeta* meta = meta_map_.find(name);
  if (meta == nullptr) {
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kNotFound,
                          "ModelReader::get_tensor: tensor '%.*s' not found",
                          static_cast<int>(name.size()), name.data());
  }
  if (!is_valid_dtype(meta->dtype) || meta->rank > kMaxTensorDims) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "ModelReader::get_tensor: corrupt TensorMeta record");
  }

  Shape shape = (meta->rank == 0) ? Shape::scalar() : Shape();
  if (meta->rank > 0) {
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(
        shape, Shape::from_dims(Span<const Index>(meta->dims, meta->rank)));
  }
  const Strides strides = Strides::contiguous_for(shape);

  Ptr<Storage> storage;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(
      storage,
      Storage::from_mmap(holder_, meta->data_offset, meta->data_bytes));

  return Tensor::from_storage(std::move(storage), shape, strides, meta->dtype, 0,
                              meta->quant_scale);
}

}  // namespace io
}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/core/thread_pool.cpp ==========
// src/core/thread_pool.cpp
//
// Implementation of the low-latency data-parallel ThreadPool.
//
// Platform threading primitives:
//   * POSIX (Linux, Android, macOS): pthread_t, pthread_mutex_t, pthread_cond_t
//   * Windows: _beginthreadex / WaitForSingleObject, SRWLOCK, CONDITION_VARIABLE
//
// Using native OS primitives directly keeps the binary free of <thread>,
// <mutex>, and <condition_variable> exception-throwing wrappers.

#include "log3/core/thread_pool.h"

#include <atomic>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/cpu_features.h"

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
#include <process.h>
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <pthread.h>
#include <sched.h>
#endif

namespace l3 {

namespace {

inline constexpr int kMaxPoolThreads = 64;
inline constexpr u32 kSpinIterationsBeforeSleep = 2000;

L3_FORCE_INLINE void cpu_relax() noexcept {
#if L3_ARCH_X64 || L3_ARCH_X86
#if L3_MSVC_ABI
  ::_mm_pause();
#else
  __builtin_ia32_pause();
#endif
#elif L3_ARCH_ARM64
  asm volatile("yield" ::: "memory");
#endif
}

// Per-thread TLS flag to detect nested `parallel_for` calls from inside a
// worker and run them inline without deadlocking.
thread_local bool g_in_parallel_for = false;

}  // namespace

struct ThreadPool::Impl {
  int total_threads = 1;
  int num_workers = 0;  // total_threads - 1

  // Serializes outer callers of parallel_for on the same ThreadPool instance.
  L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic_flag dispatch_lock = ATOMIC_FLAG_INIT;

  // Synchronizes worker sleep/wake when spinning times out.
#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  SRWLOCK mutex = SRWLOCK_INIT;
  CONDITION_VARIABLE cv_work = CONDITION_VARIABLE_INIT;
  HANDLE workers[kMaxPoolThreads]{};
#else
  pthread_mutex_t mutex = PTHREAD_MUTEX_INITIALIZER;
  pthread_cond_t cv_work = PTHREAD_COND_INITIALIZER;
  pthread_t workers[kMaxPoolThreads]{};
#endif

  // Current batch descriptor (written before bumping `generation`).
  L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) Index range_begin = 0;
  Index range_end = 0;
  Index grain_size = 1;
  ParallelRangeFn active_fn{};

  // Cache-line-isolated atomic cursor for dynamic work stealing across chunks.
  L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic<Index> next_chunk{0};

  // Generation counter: bumped by the main thread on each dispatch.
  L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic<u64> generation{0};

  // Number of workers that have NOT yet finished the current generation.
  L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic<int> active_workers{0};

  // Number of workers currently blocked in pthread_cond_wait / SleepConditionVariableSRW.
  L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic<int> sleeping_workers{0};

  std::atomic<bool> stop{false};

  struct WorkerArg {
    Impl* pool;
    int worker_id;  // 1 .. num_workers
  };
  WorkerArg worker_args[kMaxPoolThreads]{};

  // Execute available chunks for the current task until the range is exhausted.
  void run_chunks(Index thread_idx) noexcept {
    const Index end = range_end;
    const Index step = grain_size;
    const ParallelRangeFn fn = active_fn;

    for (;;) {
      const Index start = next_chunk.fetch_add(step, std::memory_order_relaxed);
      if (start >= end) break;
      const Index chunk_end = (end - start < step) ? end : (start + step);
      fn(thread_idx, start, chunk_end);
    }
  }

  void worker_loop(int worker_id) noexcept {
    g_in_parallel_for = true;
    u64 last_gen = 0;

    for (;;) {
      // Phase 1: Hybrid short spin waiting for next generation or stop.
      u64 cur_gen = generation.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
      if (cur_gen == last_gen && !stop.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
        for (u32 spin = 0; spin < kSpinIterationsBeforeSleep; ++spin) {
          cpu_relax();
          cur_gen = generation.load(std::memory_order_acquire);
          if (cur_gen != last_gen || stop.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
            break;
          }
        }
      }

      // Phase 2: Fall back to OS condvar wait if still idle after spin window.
      if (cur_gen == last_gen && !stop.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
        ::AcquireSRWLockExclusive(&mutex);
        sleeping_workers.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        while ((cur_gen = generation.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) ==
                   last_gen &&
               !stop.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
          ::SleepConditionVariableSRW(&cv_work, &mutex, INFINITE, 0);
        }
        sleeping_workers.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        ::ReleaseSRWLockExclusive(&mutex);
#else
        ::pthread_mutex_lock(&mutex);
        sleeping_workers.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        while ((cur_gen = generation.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) ==
                   last_gen &&
               !stop.load(std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
          ::pthread_cond_wait(&cv_work, &mutex);
        }
        sleeping_workers.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
        ::pthread_mutex_unlock(&mutex);
#endif
      }

      if (stop.load(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
        return;
      }

      last_gen = cur_gen;

      // Participate in the current batch.
      run_chunks(static_cast<Index>(worker_id));

      // Signal completion of this worker for the current generation.
      active_workers.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_release);
    }
  }

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  static unsigned __stdcall worker_entry(void* arg) noexcept {
    auto* wa = static_cast<WorkerArg*>(arg);
    wa->pool->worker_loop(wa->worker_id);
    return 0;
  }
#else
  static void* worker_entry(void* arg) noexcept {
    auto* wa = static_cast<WorkerArg*>(arg);
    wa->pool->worker_loop(wa->worker_id);
    return nullptr;
  }
#endif
};

Result<ThreadPool> ThreadPool::create(int num_threads,
                                      const Allocator& allocator) noexcept {
  if (num_threads <= 0) {
    num_threads = cpu_features().physical_cores;
    if (num_threads <= 0) num_threads = 4;
  }
  if (num_threads > kMaxPoolThreads) {
    num_threads = kMaxPoolThreads;
  }

  Impl* impl = create_object<Impl>(allocator);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(impl == nullptr)) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfMemory,
                         "ThreadPool::create: failed to allocate Impl");
  }

  impl->total_threads = num_threads;
  impl->num_workers = num_threads - 1;

#if !L3_OS_WINDOWS
  ::pthread_mutex_init(&impl->mutex, nullptr);
  ::pthread_cond_init(&impl->cv_work, nullptr);
#endif

  int spawned = 0;
  for (int i = 0; i < impl->num_workers; ++i) {
    impl->worker_args[i].pool = impl;
    impl->worker_args[i].worker_id = i + 1;

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
    uintptr_t h = ::_beginthreadex(nullptr, 0, &Impl::worker_entry,
                                   &impl->worker_args[i], 0, nullptr);
    if (h == 0) break;
    impl->workers[i] = reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(h);
#else
    if (::pthread_create(&impl->workers[i], nullptr, &Impl::worker_entry,
                         &impl->worker_args[i]) != 0) {
      break;
    }
#endif
    ++spawned;
  }

  if (spawned < impl->num_workers) {
    impl->num_workers = spawned;
    impl->total_threads = spawned + 1;
  }

  ThreadPool pool;
  pool.impl_ = impl;
  pool.allocator_ = &allocator;
  return pool;
}

void ThreadPool::shutdown() noexcept {
  if (impl_ == nullptr) return;

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  ::AcquireSRWLockExclusive(&impl_->mutex);
  impl_->stop.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
  ::WakeAllConditionVariable(&impl_->cv_work);
  ::ReleaseSRWLockExclusive(&impl_->mutex);

  for (int i = 0; i < impl_->num_workers; ++i) {
    ::WaitForSingleObject(impl_->workers[i], INFINITE);
    ::CloseHandle(impl_->workers[i]);
  }
#else
  ::pthread_mutex_lock(&impl_->mutex);
  impl_->stop.store(true, std::memory_order_release);
  ::pthread_cond_broadcast(&impl_->cv_work);
  ::pthread_mutex_unlock(&impl_->mutex);

  for (int i = 0; i < impl_->num_workers; ++i) {
    ::pthread_join(impl_->workers[i], nullptr);
  }
  ::pthread_cond_destroy(&impl_->cv_work);
  ::pthread_mutex_destroy(&impl_->mutex);
#endif

  const Allocator& alloc =
      allocator_ != nullptr ? *allocator_ : default_allocator();
  destroy_object(alloc, impl_);
  impl_ = nullptr;
  allocator_ = nullptr;
}

int ThreadPool::num_threads() const noexcept {
  return impl_ != nullptr ? impl_->total_threads : 1;
}

void ThreadPool::parallel_for(Index begin, Index end, Index grain_size,
                              ParallelRangeFn fn) noexcept {
  if (end <= begin || !fn.is_valid()) return;

  const Index total_items = end - begin;
  const int nthreads = num_threads();

  if (grain_size <= 0) {
    const Index target_chunks = static_cast<Index>(nthreads) * 4;
    grain_size =
        static_cast<Index>(div_ceil(static_cast<Size>(total_items),
                                    static_cast<Size>(target_chunks)));
    if (grain_size < 1) grain_size = 1;
  }

  // Fast single-thread path if pool has 1 thread, range fits in 1 chunk, or
  // called recursively from inside an active worker.
  if (nthreads <= 1 || total_items <= grain_size || g_in_parallel_for) {
    fn(0, begin, end);
    return;
  }

  // Acquire dispatch lock so concurrent outer calls serialize cleanly.
  while (impl_->dispatch_lock.test_and_set(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
    cpu_relax();
  }

  impl_->range_begin = begin;
  impl_->range_end = end;
  impl_->grain_size = grain_size;
  impl_->active_fn = fn;
  impl_->next_chunk.store(begin, std::memory_order_relaxed);
  impl_->active_workers.store(impl_->num_workers, std::memory_order_relaxed);

  // Publish new generation to wake spinning workers immediately.
  impl_->generation.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_release);

  // If any workers are sleeping on the OS condvar, wake them up.
  if (impl_->sleeping_workers.load(std::memory_order_acquire) > 0) {
#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
    ::AcquireSRWLockExclusive(&impl_->mutex);
    ::WakeAllConditionVariable(&impl_->cv_work);
    ::ReleaseSRWLockExclusive(&impl_->mutex);
#else
    ::pthread_mutex_lock(&impl_->mutex);
    ::pthread_cond_broadcast(&impl_->cv_work);
    ::pthread_mutex_unlock(&impl_->mutex);
#endif
  }

  // Calling thread participates as thread 0.
  const bool prev_tls = g_in_parallel_for;
  g_in_parallel_for = true;
  impl_->run_chunks(0);
  g_in_parallel_for = prev_tls;

  // Wait for all workers to finish their final chunk.
  while (impl_->active_workers.load(std::memory_order_acquire) > 0) {
    cpu_relax();
  }

  impl_->dispatch_lock.clear(std::memory_order_release);
}

ThreadPool& default_thread_pool() noexcept {
  static ThreadPool s_pool = []() {
    auto res = ThreadPool::create(0, default_allocator());
    L3_CHECK_OK(res.status());
    return res.take_value();
  }();
  return s_pool;
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/core/status.cpp ==========

// src/core/status.cpp
//
// Out-of-line implementation of Status and Result failure handling.
//
// Design principles:
//   * Error metadata is independent of the tensor allocator.
//   * Error reporting does not modify memory-tracking statistics.
//   * Allocation failures use an immutable static OOM fallback.
//   * Messages are bounded to prevent excessive diagnostic allocations.
//   * Short messages use inline storage.
//   * Long messages use separately allocated storage.
//
// Compatibility:
//   * C++17
//   * No exceptions
//   * No RTTI

#include "log3/core/status.h"

#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <new>

#include "log3/core/log.h"

namespace l3 {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Internal state
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

struct Status::State {
  StatusCode code;
  u32 length;

  const char* message;
  char inline_message[16];
};

namespace {

constexpr usize kMaxStatusMessageLength = 1023;
constexpr usize kInlineMessageCapacity = 16;

constexpr char kOomMessage[] = "out of memory";

// Immutable fallback. Its message points to static storage.
const Status::State kOomState = {
    StatusCode::kOutOfMemory,
    static_cast<u32>(sizeof(kOomMessage) - 1),
    kOomMessage,
    {}
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Bounded string length
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Reads at most max_length characters.
//
// The caller must still provide a valid null-terminated C string.
// If its length exceeds max_length, the result is truncated.

usize bounded_string_length(
    const char* message,
    usize max_length) noexcept {

  usize length = 0;

  while (length < max_length && message[length] != '\0') {
    ++length;
  }

  return length;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// State allocation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Status::State* allocate_state(
    StatusCode code,
    const char* message,
    usize length) noexcept {

  if (length > kMaxStatusMessageLength) {
    length = kMaxStatusMessageLength;
  }

  // Allocate the State independently of tensor memory tracking.
  void* memory = std::malloc(sizeof(Status::State));

  if (L3_UNLIKELY(memory == nullptr)) {
    return const_cast<Status::State*>(&kOomState);
  }

  auto* state = ::new (memory) Status::State{};

  state->code = code;
  state->length = static_cast<u32>(length);

  // Inline storage includes the terminating null byte.
  if (length < kInlineMessageCapacity) {
    if (length != 0) {
      std::memcpy(
          state->inline_message,
          message,
          length);
    }

    state->inline_message[length] = '\0';
    state->message = state->inline_message;

    return state;
  }

  // Long messages use a separate allocation.
  // length is bounded, so length + 1 cannot overflow.
  auto* heap_message = static_cast<char*>(
      std::malloc(length + 1));

  if (L3_UNLIKELY(heap_message == nullptr)) {
    state->~State();
    std::free(state);

    return const_cast<Status::State*>(&kOomState);
  }

  std::memcpy(heap_message, message, length);
  heap_message[length] = '\0';

  state->message = heap_message;

  return state;
}

}  // namespace

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Destruction
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

void Status::destroy(State* state) noexcept {
  if (state == nullptr || state == &kOomState) {
    return;
  }

  // Free separately allocated messages only.
  if (state->message != state->inline_message) {
    std::free(const_cast<char*>(state->message));
  }

  state->~State();
  std::free(state);
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Status::error()
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Status Status::error(
    StatusCode code,
    const char* message) noexcept {

  if (L3_UNLIKELY(code == StatusCode::kOk)) {
    return Status::ok();
  }

  const char* safe_message =
      message != nullptr ? message : "";

  const usize length = bounded_string_length(
      safe_message,
      kMaxStatusMessageLength);

  Status result;

  result.state_ = allocate_state(
      code,
      safe_message,
      length);

  return result;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Status::errorf()
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Status Status::errorf(
    StatusCode code,
    const char* fmt,
    ...) noexcept {

  if (L3_UNLIKELY(code == StatusCode::kOk)) {
    return Status::ok();
  }

  if (L3_UNLIKELY(fmt == nullptr)) {
    return Status::error(code, "");
  }

  char buffer[kMaxStatusMessageLength + 1];

  std::va_list args;
  va_start(args, fmt);

  const int written = std::vsnprintf(
      buffer,
      sizeof(buffer),
      fmt,
      args);

  va_end(args);

  if (L3_UNLIKELY(written < 0)) {
    return Status::error(
        code,
        "status message formatting failed");
  }

  const usize length =
      static_cast<usize>(written) > kMaxStatusMessageLength
          ? kMaxStatusMessageLength
          : static_cast<usize>(written);

  Status result;

  result.state_ = allocate_state(
      code,
      buffer,
      length);

  return result;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Accessors
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

StatusCode Status::code() const noexcept {
  return state_ == nullptr
      ? StatusCode::kOk
      : state_->code;
}

const char* Status::message() const noexcept {
  return state_ == nullptr
      ? ""
      : state_->message;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Status::clone()
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

Status Status::clone() const noexcept {
  if (state_ == nullptr) {
    return Status::ok();
  }

  if (state_ == &kOomState) {
    Status result;
    result.state_ = const_cast<State*>(&kOomState);
    return result;
  }

  Status result;

  result.state_ = allocate_state(
      state_->code,
      state_->message,
      state_->length);

  return result;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Result<T> access failure
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

namespace detail {

[[noreturn]] void result_access_failed(
    const Status& status) noexcept {

  L3_LOG_FATAL(
      "Result<T>::value() called on error Status [%s]: %s",
      status_code_name(status.code()),
      status.message());

  std::abort();
}

}  // namespace detail

}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/core/assert.cpp ==========
// src/core/assert.cpp
//
// Cold, out-of-line failure handlers for L3_CHECK*, L3_DCHECK*, and L3_PANIC.
//
// Design notes:
//   * Each handler forwards the caller's __FILE__, __LINE__, and __func__
//     directly to log_message(LogLevel::kFatal, ...) so the log line points to
//     the failed check rather than to assert.cpp.
//   * CheckValue formatting uses a small stack buffer and handles bools,
//     signed/unsigned 64-bit integers, floats, and pointers without dynamic
//     allocation.

#include "log3/core/assert.h"

#include <cinttypes>
#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>

namespace l3 {
namespace detail {

namespace {

void format_check_value(CheckValue v, char* buf, usize buf_size) noexcept {
  switch (v.kind) {
    case CheckValue::Kind::kBool:
      std::snprintf(buf, buf_size, "%s", v.u != 0 ? "true" : "false");
      return;
    case CheckValue::Kind::kSigned:
      std::snprintf(buf, buf_size, "%" PRId64, v.i);
      return;
    case CheckValue::Kind::kUnsigned:
      std::snprintf(buf, buf_size, "%" PRIu64, v.u);
      return;
    case CheckValue::Kind::kFloat:
      std::snprintf(buf, buf_size, "%.9g", v.f);
      return;
    case CheckValue::Kind::kPointer:
      if (v.p == nullptr) {
        std::snprintf(buf, buf_size, "nullptr");
      } else {
        std::snprintf(buf, buf_size, "%p", v.p);
      }
      return;
  }
  std::snprintf(buf, buf_size, "?");
}

}  // namespace

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD void check_failed(const char* file, int line,
                                       const char* func,
                                       const char* expr) noexcept {
  log_message(LogLevel::kFatal, file, line, func,
              "Check failed: %s (in %s)", expr, func != nullptr ? func : "?");
  fatal_abort();
}

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD void check_failed_msg(const char* file, int line,
                                           const char* func, const char* expr,
                                           const char* fmt, ...) noexcept {
  char user_msg[512];
  user_msg[0] = '\0';
  if (fmt != nullptr) {
    std::va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    std::vsnprintf(user_msg, sizeof(user_msg), fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
  }
  log_message(LogLevel::kFatal, file, line, func,
              "Check failed: %s — %s (in %s)", expr, user_msg,
              func != nullptr ? func : "?");
  fatal_abort();
}

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD void check_op_failed(const char* file, int line,
                                          const char* func, const char* expr,
                                          CheckValue lhs,
                                          CheckValue rhs) noexcept {
  char lhs_buf[64];
  char rhs_buf[64];
  format_check_value(lhs, lhs_buf, sizeof(lhs_buf));
  format_check_value(rhs, rhs_buf, sizeof(rhs_buf));

  log_message(LogLevel::kFatal, file, line, func,
              "Check failed: %s (%s vs. %s) (in %s)", expr, lhs_buf, rhs_buf,
              func != nullptr ? func : "?");
  fatal_abort();
}

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD void check_status_failed(const char* file, int line,
                                              const char* func,
                                              const char* expr,
                                              const Status& status) noexcept {
  log_message(LogLevel::kFatal, file, line, func,
              "Check OK failed: %s returned [%s]: %s (in %s)", expr,
              status_code_name(status.code()), status.message(),
              func != nullptr ? func : "?");
  fatal_abort();
}

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD void panic_msg(const char* file, int line,
                                    const char* func, const char* fmt,
                                    ...) noexcept {
  char user_msg[512];
  user_msg[0] = '\0';
  if (fmt != nullptr) {
    std::va_list args;
    va_start(args, fmt);
    std::vsnprintf(user_msg, sizeof(user_msg), fmt, args);
    va_end(args);
  }
  log_message(LogLevel::kFatal, file, line, func, "Panic: %s (in %s)",
              user_msg, func != nullptr ? func : "?");
  fatal_abort();
}

}  // namespace detail
}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/core/log.cpp ==========
// src/core/log.cpp
//
// Implementation of leveled logging, built-in sinks (stderr and Android
// logcat), thread-safe dispatch with re-entrancy protection, and fatal_abort().
//
// Design notes:
//   * Zero <mutex>, <chrono>, or <thread> dependencies. Locking uses a
//     cache-line-aligned atomic spinlock, and timestamps/thread IDs use
//     lightweight OS primitives plus thread_local state.
//   * Re-entrant calls from inside a custom sink are detected via a
//     thread-local flag and dropped silently so a buggy sink cannot deadlock.

#include "log3/core/log.h"

#include <atomic>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cstdarg>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <ctime>

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
#include <windows.h>
#endif

#if L3_OS_ANDROID
#include <android/log.h>
#endif

namespace l3 {

namespace detail {

// Starts at kInfo by default.
L3_API std::atomic<u8> g_log_level{static_cast<u8>(LogLevel::kInfo)};

}  // namespace detail

namespace {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Default sink selection
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
constexpr LogSinkFn default_sink_fn() noexcept {
#if L3_OS_ANDROID
  return &log_sink_logcat;
#else
  return &log_sink_stderr;
#endif
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Internal lock and active sink state
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic_flag g_sink_lock = ATOMIC_FLAG_INIT;
LogSink g_active_sink = {default_sink_fn(), nullptr};

// Per-thread re-entrancy guard: true while this thread is executing a sink.
thread_local bool g_in_log_sink = false;

void lock_sink() noexcept {
  while (g_sink_lock.test_and_set(std::memory_order_acquire)) {
#if L3_ARCH_X64 || L3_ARCH_X86
#if L3_MSVC_ABI
    ::_mm_pause();
#else
    __builtin_ia32_pause();
#endif
#elif L3_ARCH_ARM64
    asm volatile("yield" ::: "memory");
#endif
  }
}

void unlock_sink() noexcept {
  g_sink_lock.clear(std::memory_order_release);
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Helpers: thread ID, wall-clock timestamp, and filename basename
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
u32 current_thread_id() noexcept {
  static std::atomic<u32> next_id{1};
  thread_local const u32 tid =
      next_id.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
  return tid;
}

u64 wall_clock_ns() noexcept {
#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  FILETIME ft;
  ::GetSystemTimeAsFileTime(&ft);
  ULARGE_INTEGER uli;
  uli.LowPart = ft.dwLowDateTime;
  uli.HighPart = ft.dwHighDateTime;
  // Convert 100-ns ticks since 1601-01-01 to Unix epoch (1970-01-01).
  constexpr u64 kEpochDiffTicks = 116444736000000000ULL;
  if (uli.QuadPart < kEpochDiffTicks) return 0;
  return (uli.QuadPart - kEpochDiffTicks) * 100ULL;
#else
  struct timespec ts {};
  if (::clock_gettime(CLOCK_REALTIME, &ts) != 0) return 0;
  return static_cast<u64>(ts.tv_sec) * 1000000000ULL +
         static_cast<u64>(ts.tv_nsec);
#endif
}

const char* path_basename(const char* path) noexcept {
  if (path == nullptr) return "?";
  const char* base = path;
  for (const char* p = path; *p != '\0'; ++p) {
    if (*p == '/' || *p == '\\') {
      base = p + 1;
    }
  }
  return base;
}

const char* padded_level_name(LogLevel level) noexcept {
  switch (level) {
    case LogLevel::kTrace: return "TRACE";
    case LogLevel::kDebug: return "DEBUG";
    case LogLevel::kInfo:  return "INFO ";
    case LogLevel::kWarn:  return "WARN ";
    case LogLevel::kError: return "ERROR";
    case LogLevel::kFatal: return "FATAL";
    case LogLevel::kOff:   return "OFF  ";
  }
  return "?    ";
}

}  // namespace

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Built-in sinks
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
void log_sink_stderr(const LogRecord& record, void* /*user_data*/) noexcept {
  // Format UTC/local time of day as [HH:MM:SS.mmm] from timestamp_ns.
  const u64 total_ms = record.timestamp_ns / 1000000ULL;
  const unsigned ms = static_cast<unsigned>(total_ms % 1000ULL);
  const std::time_t secs = static_cast<std::time_t>(total_ms / 1000ULL);

  struct std::tm tm_buf {};
#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  ::localtime_s(&tm_buf, &secs);
#else
  ::localtime_r(&secs, &tm_buf);
#endif

  std::fprintf(stderr,
               "[%02d:%02d:%02d.%03u] [%s] [t%" PRIu32 "] %s:%d: %s\n",
               tm_buf.tm_hour, tm_buf.tm_min, tm_buf.tm_sec, ms,
               padded_level_name(record.level), record.thread_id,
               record.file != nullptr ? record.file : "?", record.line,
               record.message != nullptr ? record.message : "");
  if (record.level >= LogLevel::kError) {
    std::fflush(stderr);
  }
}

void log_sink_logcat(const LogRecord& record, void* /*user_data*/) noexcept {
#if L3_OS_ANDROID
  int prio = ANDROID_LOG_INFO;
  switch (record.level) {
    case LogLevel::kTrace: prio = ANDROID_LOG_VERBOSE; break;
    case LogLevel::kDebug: prio = ANDROID_LOG_DEBUG; break;
    case LogLevel::kInfo:  prio = ANDROID_LOG_INFO; break;
    case LogLevel::kWarn:  prio = ANDROID_LOG_WARN; break;
    case LogLevel::kError: prio = ANDROID_LOG_ERROR; break;
    case LogLevel::kFatal: prio = ANDROID_LOG_FATAL; break;
    case LogLevel::kOff:   return;
  }
  ::__android_log_print(prio, "log3", "[t%" PRIu32 "] %s:%d: %s",
                        record.thread_id,
                        record.file != nullptr ? record.file : "?",
                        record.line,
                        record.message != nullptr ? record.message : "");
#else
  L3_UNUSED(record);
#endif
}

void log_sink_null(const LogRecord& /*record*/, void* /*user_data*/) noexcept {}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Sink and runtime level configuration
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
void set_log_sink(LogSink sink) noexcept {
  if (sink.fn == nullptr) return;
  lock_sink();
  g_active_sink = sink;
  unlock_sink();
}

void reset_log_sink() noexcept {
  lock_sink();
  g_active_sink = {default_sink_fn(), nullptr};
  unlock_sink();
}

void set_log_level(LogLevel level) noexcept {
  detail::g_log_level.store(static_cast<u8>(level), std::memory_order_relaxed);
}

LogLevel log_level() noexcept {
  return static_cast<LogLevel>(
      detail::g_log_level.load(std::memory_order_relaxed));
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Emission
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
void log_write(LogLevel level, const char* file, int line, const char* func,
               const char* message, usize length) noexcept {
  if (level != LogLevel::kFatal && !log_enabled(level)) {
    return;
  }
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(g_in_log_sink)) {
    return;  // Drop recursive log calls from inside a sink.
  }

  LogRecord record{};
  record.level = level;
  record.thread_id = current_thread_id();
  record.timestamp_ns = wall_clock_ns();
  record.file = path_basename(file);
  record.line = line;
  record.func = func != nullptr ? func : "?";
  record.message = message != nullptr ? message : "";
  record.length = length;

  lock_sink();
  g_in_log_sink = true;
  g_active_sink.fn(record, g_active_sink.user_data);
  g_in_log_sink = false;
  unlock_sink();
}

void log_message(LogLevel level, const char* file, int line, const char* func,
                 const char* fmt, ...) noexcept {
  if (level != LogLevel::kFatal && !log_enabled(level)) {
    return;
  }
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(fmt == nullptr)) {
    log_write(level, file, line, func, "", 0);
    return;
  }

  char buf[kLogMaxMessageLength];
  std::va_list args;
  va_start(args, fmt);
  const int written = std::vsnprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), fmt, args);
  va_end(args);

  usize len = 0;
  if (written > 0) {
    len = static_cast<usize>(written) < sizeof(buf)
              ? static_cast<usize>(written)
              : sizeof(buf) - 1;
  }
  log_write(level, file, line, func, buf, len);
}

[[noreturn]] void fatal_abort() noexcept {
  std::fflush(stderr);
#if L3_DEBUG
  L3_DEBUG_BREAK();
#endif
  std::abort();
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/core/cpu_features.cpp ==========
// src/core/cpu_features.cpp
//
// Platform-specific CPU feature detection via cpuid (x86/x64) and auxval / sysctl
// (ARM/ARM64 on Linux, Android, and macOS).

#include "log3/core/cpu_features.h"

#include <cstring>

#if L3_ARCH_X86_FAMILY
#if L3_MSVC_ABI
#include <intrin.h>
#else
#include <cpuid.h>
#endif
#endif

#if L3_OS_LINUX || L3_OS_ANDROID
#include <sys/auxv.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#ifndef HWCAP_ASIMD
#define HWCAP_ASIMD (1 << 3)
#endif
#ifndef HWCAP_FPHP
#define HWCAP_FPHP (1 << 9)
#endif
#ifndef HWCAP_ASIMDDP
#define HWCAP_ASIMDDP (1 << 20)
#endif
#ifdef __aarch64__
#ifndef HWCAP2_I8MM
#define HWCAP2_I8MM (1 << 13)
#endif
#endif
#endif

#if L3_OS_APPLE
#include <sys/sysctl.h>
#endif

namespace l3 {

namespace {

#if L3_ARCH_X86_FAMILY
void detect_x86_features(CpuFeatures& f) noexcept {
  int cpuInfo[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};

  // Get max supported standard leaf
#if L3_MSVC_ABI
  ::__cpuid(cpuInfo, 0);
#else
  __cpuid(0, cpuInfo[0], cpuInfo[1], cpuInfo[2], cpuInfo[3]);
#endif
  const int max_std_leaf = cpuInfo[0];

  if (max_std_leaf >= 1) {
#if L3_MSVC_ABI
    ::__cpuid(cpuInfo, 1);
#else
    __cpuid(1, cpuInfo[0], cpuInfo[1], cpuInfo[2], cpuInfo[3]);
#endif
    const u32 ecx = static_cast<u32>(cpuInfo[2]);
    const u32 edx = static_cast<u32>(cpuInfo[3]);

    f.has_sse2 = (edx & (1u << 26)) != 0;
    f.has_sse41 = (ecx & (1u << 19)) != 0;
    f.has_sse42 = (ecx & (1u << 20)) != 0;
    f.has_f16c = (ecx & (1u << 29)) != 0;
    f.has_fma = (ecx & (1u << 12)) != 0;
    f.has_avx = (ecx & (1u << 28)) != 0;
  }

  if (max_std_leaf >= 7) {
#if L3_MSVC_ABI
    ::__cpuidex(cpuInfo, 7, 0);
#else
    __cpuid_count(7, 0, cpuInfo[0], cpuInfo[1], cpuInfo[2], cpuInfo[3]);
#endif
    const u32 ebx = static_cast<u32>(cpuInfo[1]);
    const u32 ecx = static_cast<u32>(cpuInfo[2]);

    f.has_avx2 = f.has_avx && ((ebx & (1u << 5)) != 0);
    f.has_avx512f = (ebx & (1u << 16)) != 0;
    f.has_avx512dq = (ebx & (1u << 17)) != 0;
    f.has_avx512bw = (ebx & (1u << 30)) != 0;
    f.has_avx512_vnni = f.has_avx512f && ((ecx & (1u << 11)) != 0);
  }
}
#endif

#if L3_ARCH_ARM_FAMILY
void detect_arm_features(CpuFeatures& f) noexcept {
#if L3_OS_LINUX || L3_OS_ANDROID
  const unsigned long hwcap = getauxval(AT_HWCAP);
  f.has_neon = (hwcap & HWCAP_ASIMD) != 0;
  f.has_neon_fp16 = (hwcap & HWCAP_FPHP) != 0;
  f.has_neon_dotprod = (hwcap & HWCAP_ASIMDDP) != 0;

#ifdef __aarch64__
  const unsigned long hwcap2 = getauxval(AT_HWCAP2);
  f.has_neon_i8mm = (hwcap2 & HWCAP2_I8MM) != 0;
#endif

#elif L3_OS_APPLE
  // Apple Silicon always has NEON, FP16, and DotProd (M1+).
  f.has_neon = true;
  f.has_neon_fp16 = true;
  f.has_neon_dotprod = true;
  
  int val = 0;
  usize size = sizeof(val);
  if (sysctlbyname("hw.optional.arm.FEAT_I8MM", &val, &size, nullptr, 0) == 0) {
    f.has_neon_i8mm = (val != 0);
  }
#else
  f.has_neon = L3_HAS_NEON;
#endif
}
#endif

void detect_cache_and_cores(CpuFeatures& f) noexcept {
  f.cache_line_size = kCacheLineSize;

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  const long nprocs = ::sysconf(_SC_NPROCESSORS_ONLN);
  f.logical_cores = nprocs > 0 ? static_cast<int>(nprocs) : 4;
  f.physical_cores = f.logical_cores;
#elif L3_OS_APPLE
  int cpu_count = 1;
  usize size = sizeof(cpu_count);
  if (sysctlbyname("hw.logicalcpu", &cpu_count, &size, nullptr, 0) == 0) {
    f.logical_cores = cpu_count;
  }
  if (sysctlbyname("hw.physicalcpu", &cpu_count, &size, nullptr, 0) == 0) {
    f.physical_cores = cpu_count;
  }
#else
  // Linux / Android fallback
  f.logical_cores = 4;
  f.physical_cores = 4;
#endif
}

}  // namespace

const CpuFeatures& cpu_features() noexcept {
  static CpuFeatures s_features = []() {
    CpuFeatures f{};
    std::memset(&f, 0, sizeof(CpuFeatures));
    f.cache_line_size = kCacheLineSize;

#if L3_ARCH_X86_FAMILY
    detect_x86_features(f);
#elif L3_ARCH_ARM_FAMILY
    detect_arm_features(f);
#endif

    detect_cache_and_cores(f);
    return f;
  }();

  return s_features;
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/core/mmap.cpp ==========
// src/core/mmap.cpp
//
// Cross-platform read-only memory-mapped file implementation.

#include "log3/core/mmap.h"

#include <cerrno>
#include <cstring>

#include "log3/core/memory.h"

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
#include <windows.h>
#else
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/mman.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

namespace l3 {

MMapFile::MMapFile(MMapFile&& other) noexcept
    : data_(other.data_),
      size_(other.size_),
      os_file_handle_(other.os_file_handle_),
      os_map_handle_(other.os_map_handle_) {
  other.data_ = nullptr;
  other.size_ = 0;
  other.os_file_handle_ = -1;
  other.os_map_handle_ = nullptr;
}

MMapFile& MMapFile::operator=(MMapFile&& other) noexcept {
  if (this != &other) {
    close();
    data_ = other.data_;
    size_ = other.size_;
    os_file_handle_ = other.os_file_handle_;
    os_map_handle_ = other.os_map_handle_;

    other.data_ = nullptr;
    other.size_ = 0;
    other.os_file_handle_ = -1;
    other.os_map_handle_ = nullptr;
  }
  return *this;
}

Result<MMapFile> MMapFile::open(const char* path, bool prefetch) noexcept {
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(path == nullptr || path[0] == '\0')) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "MMapFile::open: path is null or empty");
  }

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  HANDLE file_h = ::CreateFileA(
      path, GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING,
      FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL | FILE_FLAG_SEQUENTIAL_SCAN, nullptr);
  if (file_h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) {
    const DWORD err = ::GetLastError();
    if (err == ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND || err == ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND) {
      return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kNotFound,
                            "MMapFile::open: file not found: '%s'", path);
    }
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kIoError,
                          "MMapFile::open: CreateFileA failed for '%s' (err=%lu)",
                          path, static_cast<unsigned long>(err));
  }

  LARGE_INTEGER file_size{};
  if (!::GetFileSizeEx(file_h, &file_size) || file_size.QuadPart <= 0) {
    ::CloseHandle(file_h);
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                          "MMapFile::open: file is empty or unreadable: '%s'",
                          path);
  }

  HANDLE map_h =
      ::CreateFileMappingW(file_h, nullptr, PAGE_READONLY, 0, 0, nullptr);
  if (map_h == nullptr) {
    const DWORD err = ::GetLastError();
    ::CloseHandle(file_h);
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kIoError,
        "MMapFile::open: CreateFileMapping failed for '%s' (err=%lu)", path,
        static_cast<unsigned long>(err));
  }

  const void* view = ::MapViewOfFile(map_h, FILE_MAP_READ, 0, 0, 0);
  if (view == nullptr) {
    const DWORD err = ::GetLastError();
    ::CloseHandle(map_h);
    ::CloseHandle(file_h);
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kIoError,
        "MMapFile::open: MapViewOfFile failed for '%s' (err=%lu)", path,
        static_cast<unsigned long>(err));
  }

  MMapFile out;
  out.data_ = static_cast<const byte*>(view);
  out.size_ = static_cast<Size>(file_size.QuadPart);
  out.os_file_handle_ = reinterpret_cast<isize>(file_h);
  out.os_map_handle_ = static_cast<void*>(map_h);

  if (prefetch) {
    out.advise(MMapAdvice::kWillNeed).ignore();
  }
  return out;

#else
  int flags = O_RDONLY;
#ifdef O_CLOEXEC
  flags |= O_CLOEXEC;
#endif
  const int fd = ::open(path, flags);
  if (fd < 0) {
    const int err = errno;
    if (err == ENOENT) {
      return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kNotFound,
                            "MMapFile::open: file not found: '%s'", path);
    }
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kIoError,
                          "MMapFile::open: open('%s') failed: %s", path,
                          std::strerror(err));
  }

  struct stat st {};
  if (::fstat(fd, &st) != 0) {
    const int err = errno;
    ::close(fd);
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kIoError,
                          "MMapFile::open: fstat('%s') failed: %s", path,
                          std::strerror(err));
  }

  if (st.st_size <= 0) {
    ::close(fd);
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                          "MMapFile::open: cannot map empty file: '%s'", path);
  }

  const Size file_size = static_cast<Size>(st.st_size);
  void* mapped = ::mmap(nullptr, file_size, PROT_READ, MAP_PRIVATE, fd, 0);
  if (mapped == MAP_FAILED) {
    const int err = errno;
    ::close(fd);
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kIoError,
                          "MMapFile::open: mmap('%s', %zu bytes) failed: %s",
                          path, file_size, std::strerror(err));
  }

  // On POSIX, the file descriptor can be closed immediately after mmap succeeds
  // without invalidating the mapping.
  ::close(fd);

  MMapFile out;
  out.data_ = static_cast<const byte*>(mapped);
  out.size_ = file_size;
  out.os_file_handle_ = -1;
  out.os_map_handle_ = nullptr;

  if (prefetch) {
    out.advise(MMapAdvice::kWillNeed).ignore();
  }
  return out;
#endif
}

void MMapFile::close() noexcept {
  if (data_ == nullptr) return;

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  ::UnmapViewOfFile(static_cast<const void*>(data_));
  if (os_map_handle_ != nullptr) {
    ::CloseHandle(static_cast<HANDLE>(os_map_handle_));
  }
  if (os_file_handle_ != -1) {
    ::CloseHandle(reinterpret_cast<HANDLE>(os_file_handle_));
  }
#else
  ::munmap(const_cast<byte*>(data_), size_);
#endif

  data_ = nullptr;
  size_ = 0;
  os_file_handle_ = -1;
  os_map_handle_ = nullptr;
}

Status MMapFile::advise(MMapAdvice advice, Size offset,
                        Size length) const noexcept {
  if (!is_open()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "MMapFile::advise: file is not open");
  }
  if (offset > size_) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                         "MMapFile::advise: offset exceeds file size");
  }
  const Size eff_len = (length == 0) ? (size_ - offset) : length;
  if (eff_len > size_ - offset) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                         "MMapFile::advise: range exceeds file size");
  }
  if (eff_len == 0) return Status::ok();

#if L3_OS_POSIX
  // madvise requires page-aligned address on Linux/macOS.
  const long page_sz_raw = ::sysconf(_SC_PAGESIZE);
  const Size page_sz =
      page_sz_raw > 0 ? static_cast<Size>(page_sz_raw) : 4096u;
  const Size aligned_off = align_down(offset, page_sz);
  const Size delta = offset - aligned_off;
  const Size aligned_len = eff_len + delta;

  int posix_adv = MADV_NORMAL;
  switch (advice) {
    case MMapAdvice::kNormal:     posix_adv = MADV_NORMAL; break;
    case MMapAdvice::kSequential: posix_adv = MADV_SEQUENTIAL; break;
    case MMapAdvice::kRandom:     posix_adv = MADV_RANDOM; break;
    case MMapAdvice::kWillNeed:   posix_adv = MADV_WILLNEED; break;
    case MMapAdvice::kDontNeed:   posix_adv = MADV_DONTNEED; break;
  }

  void* addr = const_cast<byte*>(data_ + aligned_off);
  ::madvise(addr, aligned_len, posix_adv);
#else
  L3_UNUSED(advice);
#endif
  return Status::ok();
}

Result<ConstByteSpan> MMapFile::slice(Size offset,
                                      Size length) const noexcept {
  if (!is_open()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "MMapFile::slice: file is not open");
  }
  Size end = 0;
  if (!checked_add(offset, length, &end) || end > size_) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
        "MMapFile::slice: range [%zu, %zu + %zu) exceeds file size (%zu)",
        offset, offset, length, size_);
  }
  return ConstByteSpan(data_ + offset, static_cast<Index>(length));
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/core/arena.cpp ==========
// log3/core/arena.cpp
#include "log3/core/arena.h"

#include <cstdlib>
#include <memory>
#include <new>

namespace l3 {

struct Arena::Chunk {
  Chunk* next;
  Size capacity;
  Size used;
  alignas(kDefaultAlignment) byte data[1];
};

Arena::Arena(Size chunk_size, const Allocator& backing_allocator) noexcept
    : chunk_size_(chunk_size < 4096 ? 4096 : chunk_size),
      backing_allocator_(backing_allocator),
      head_(nullptr),
      total_capacity_bytes_(0),
      total_allocated_bytes_(0) {}

Arena::Arena(Arena&& other) noexcept
    : chunk_size_(other.chunk_size_),
      backing_allocator_(other.backing_allocator_),
      head_(other.head_),
      total_capacity_bytes_(other.total_capacity_bytes_),
      total_allocated_bytes_(other.total_allocated_bytes_) {
  other.head_ = nullptr;
  other.total_capacity_bytes_ = 0;
  other.total_allocated_bytes_ = 0;
}

Arena& Arena::operator=(Arena&& other) noexcept {
  if (this != &other) {
    reset();
    Chunk* curr = head_;
    while (curr != nullptr) {
      Chunk* next = curr->next;
      backing_allocator_.deallocate(curr, sizeof(Chunk) + curr->capacity,
                                    kDefaultAlignment);
      curr = next;
    }
    chunk_size_ = other.chunk_size_;
    backing_allocator_ = other.backing_allocator_;
    head_ = other.head_;
    total_capacity_bytes_ = other.total_capacity_bytes_;
    total_allocated_bytes_ = other.total_allocated_bytes_;

    other.head_ = nullptr;
    other.total_capacity_bytes_ = 0;
    other.total_allocated_bytes_ = 0;
  }
  return *this;
}

Arena::~Arena() {
  Chunk* curr = head_;
  while (curr != nullptr) {
    Chunk* next = curr->next;
    backing_allocator_.deallocate(curr, sizeof(Chunk) + curr->capacity,
                                  kDefaultAlignment);
    curr = next;
  }
}

void* Arena::allocate(Size size, Size alignment) noexcept {
  if (size == 0) return nullptr;
  L3_DCHECK(is_power_of_two(alignment));

  if (head_ != nullptr) {
    byte* raw_data = head_->data + head_->used;
    usize space = head_->capacity - head_->used;
    void* ptr = raw_data;
    if (std::align(alignment, size, ptr, space) != nullptr) {
      Size padding = static_cast<Size>(static_cast<byte*>(ptr) - raw_data);
      head_->used += padding + size;
      total_allocated_bytes_ += size;
      return ptr;
    }
  }

  Status grow_status = grow(size, alignment);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(!grow_status.is_ok())) {
    return nullptr;
  }

  byte* raw_data = head_->data + head_->used;
  usize space = head_->capacity - head_->used;
  void* ptr = raw_data;
  void* aligned_ptr = std::align(alignment, size, ptr, space);
  L3_DCHECK(aligned_ptr != nullptr);
  Size padding = static_cast<Size>(static_cast<byte*>(aligned_ptr) - raw_data);
  head_->used += padding + size;
  total_allocated_bytes_ += size;
  return aligned_ptr;
}

void Arena::reset() noexcept {
  Chunk* curr = head_;
  while (curr != nullptr) {
    curr->used = 0;
    curr = curr->next;
  }
  total_allocated_bytes_ = 0;
}

Status Arena::grow(Size min_size, Size alignment) noexcept {
  Size needed = min_size + alignment + sizeof(Chunk);
  Size alloc_cap = chunk_size_;
  if (needed > alloc_cap) {
    alloc_cap = needed;
  }

  void* mem = backing_allocator_.allocate(sizeof(Chunk) + alloc_cap, kDefaultAlignment);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(mem == nullptr)) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfMemory, "Arena: failed to allocate chunk");
  }

  auto* new_chunk = static_cast<Chunk*>(mem);
  new_chunk->next = head_;
  new_chunk->capacity = alloc_cap;
  new_chunk->used = 0;
  head_ = new_chunk;
  total_capacity_bytes_ += alloc_cap;
  return Status::ok();
}

void* Arena::allocate_trampoline(void* ctx, Size size, Size alignment) noexcept {
  return static_cast<Arena*>(ctx)->allocate(size, alignment);
}

void Arena::deallocate_trampoline(void* /*ctx*/, void* /*ptr*/, Size /*size*/,
                                  Size /*alignment*/) noexcept {
  // Arena deallocation is a no-op per individual block.
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/core/memory.cpp ==========

// src/core/memory.cpp
//
// Aligned system memory allocation, default Allocator implementation, and
// atomic memory usage statistics.
//
// Platform details:
//   * Windows : _aligned_malloc / _aligned_free (<malloc.h>)
//   * POSIX   : posix_memalign / free (<cstdlib>), available across Linux,
//               macOS, and all Android NDK API levels.

#include "log3/core/memory.h"

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdlib>

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
#include <malloc.h>
#endif

namespace l3 {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Low-level aligned system allocation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
void* alloc_aligned(Size size, Size alignment) noexcept {
  if (size == 0) return nullptr;
  L3_DCHECK(is_power_of_two(alignment));
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(!is_power_of_two(alignment))) return nullptr;

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  return ::_aligned_malloc(size, alignment);
#else
  // posix_memalign requires alignment to be a power of two AND a multiple of
  // sizeof(void*).
  const Size eff_align =
      alignment < sizeof(void*) ? sizeof(void*) : alignment;
  void* ptr = nullptr;
  if (::posix_memalign(&ptr, eff_align, size) != 0) {
    return nullptr;
  }
  return ptr;
#endif
}

void free_aligned(void* ptr) noexcept {
  if (ptr == nullptr) return;
#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  ::_aligned_free(ptr);
#else
  std::free(ptr);
#endif
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Default allocator and statistics tracking
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
namespace {

#if L3_MEMORY_TRACKING
L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic<u64> g_current_bytes{0};
L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic<u64> g_peak_bytes{0};
L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic<u64> g_total_allocations{0};
L3_ALIGNAS(kCacheLineSize) std::atomic<u64> g_total_deallocations{0};
#endif

void* default_allocate(void* /*ctx*/, Size size, Size alignment) noexcept {
  void* ptr = alloc_aligned(size, alignment);
#if L3_MEMORY_TRACKING
  if (L3_LIKELY(ptr != nullptr)) {
    const u64 prev =
        g_current_bytes.fetch_add(static_cast<u64>(size), std::memory_order_relaxed);
    const u64 curr = prev + static_cast<u64>(size);

    u64 peak = g_peak_bytes.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
    while (curr > peak &&
           !g_peak_bytes.compare_exchange_weak(
               peak, curr, std::memory_order_relaxed, std::memory_order_relaxed)) {
    }
    g_total_allocations.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
  }
#endif
  return ptr;
}

void default_deallocate(void* /*ctx*/, void* ptr, Size size,
                        Size /*alignment*/) noexcept {
  if (ptr == nullptr) return;
  free_aligned(ptr);
#if L3_MEMORY_TRACKING
  g_current_bytes.fetch_sub(static_cast<u64>(size), std::memory_order_relaxed);
  g_total_deallocations.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
#endif
}

constexpr Allocator kDefaultAllocator = {
    &default_allocate,
    &default_deallocate,
    nullptr,
    "default",
};

}  // namespace

const Allocator& default_allocator() noexcept { return kDefaultAllocator; }

MemoryStats memory_stats() noexcept {
  MemoryStats stats{};
#if L3_MEMORY_TRACKING
  stats.current_bytes = g_current_bytes.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
  stats.peak_bytes = g_peak_bytes.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
  stats.total_allocations = g_total_allocations.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
  stats.total_deallocations = g_total_deallocations.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
#endif
  return stats;
}

void reset_peak_memory() noexcept {
#if L3_MEMORY_TRACKING
  const u64 current = g_current_bytes.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
  g_peak_bytes.store(current, std::memory_order_relaxed);
#endif
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/tensor/tensor.cpp ==========
// src/tensor/tensor.cpp
//
// Implementation of Tensor creation, zero-copy view operations (reshape,
// transpose, permute, slice, squeeze, unsqueeze), and strided-to-contiguous
// deep cloning.

#include "log3/tensor/tensor.h"

namespace l3 {

namespace {

// Compute the minimum byte span required in Storage to hold all elements
// reachable by `(shape, strides)` starting at `byte_offset`, and verify it fits
// inside `storage_bytes`.
Status verify_storage_bounds(const Shape& shape, const Strides& strides,
                             DType dtype, Size byte_offset,
                             Size storage_bytes) noexcept {
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(validate_layout_for_dtype(shape, strides, dtype));

  const Index count = shape.numel();
  if (count == 0) {
    if (byte_offset > storage_bytes) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                           "Tensor byte_offset exceeds storage size");
    }
    return Status::ok();
  }

  // Compute maximum element offset: sum((dim[i] - 1) * stride[i]).
  Index max_elem_idx = 0;
  for (Index i = 0; i < shape.rank(); ++i) {
    if (shape[i] > 1 && strides[i] > 0) {
      Size term = 0;
      if (!checked_mul(static_cast<Size>(shape[i] - 1),
                       static_cast<Size>(strides[i]), &term) ||
          !checked_add(static_cast<Size>(max_elem_idx), term, &term) ||
          term >= static_cast<Size>(kMaxIndex)) {
        return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                             "tensor stride span overflow");
      }
      max_elem_idx = static_cast<Index>(term);
    }
  }

  // Total element span from element 0 through `max_elem_idx` inclusive is
  // `max_elem_idx + 1`, rounded up to whole storage blocks for sub-byte types.
  const Index span_elems = max_elem_idx + 1;
  Size needed_bytes = 0;
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(
      checked_dtype_bytes_padded(dtype, span_elems, &needed_bytes));

  Size end_byte = 0;
  if (!checked_add(byte_offset, needed_bytes, &end_byte) ||
      end_byte > storage_bytes) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
        "Tensor view requires %zu bytes (offset %zu + span %zu), but Storage "
        "has %zu bytes",
        end_byte, byte_offset, needed_bytes, storage_bytes);
  }
  return Status::ok();
}

// Recursive N-dimensional strided copy for non-contiguous full-byte tensors.
void copy_strided_recursive(const byte* src_base, byte* dst_base,
                            const Shape& shape, const Strides& src_strides,
                            Size elem_bytes, Index axis, Index src_elem_offset,
                            Index* dst_linear_idx) noexcept {
  if (axis == shape.rank()) {
    mem_copy(dst_base + static_cast<Size>(*dst_linear_idx) * elem_bytes,
             src_base + static_cast<Size>(src_elem_offset) * elem_bytes,
             elem_bytes);
    ++(*dst_linear_idx);
    return;
  }

  const Index d = shape[axis];
  const Index st = src_strides[axis];
  if (axis == shape.rank() - 1 && st == 1) {
    mem_copy(dst_base + static_cast<Size>(*dst_linear_idx) * elem_bytes,
             src_base + static_cast<Size>(src_elem_offset) * elem_bytes,
             static_cast<Size>(d) * elem_bytes);
    *dst_linear_idx += d;
    return;
  }

  for (Index i = 0; i < d; ++i) {
    copy_strided_recursive(src_base, dst_base, shape, src_strides, elem_bytes,
                           axis + 1, src_elem_offset + i * st, dst_linear_idx);
  }
}

}  // namespace

Result<Tensor> Tensor::empty(const Shape& shape, DType dtype,
                             const Allocator& allocator) noexcept {
  if (shape.empty()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "Tensor::empty: shape is empty");
  }
  const Strides strides = Strides::contiguous_for(shape);
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(validate_layout_for_dtype(shape, strides, dtype));

  Size bytes = 0;
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(checked_dtype_bytes(dtype, shape.numel(), &bytes));

  Ptr<Storage> st;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(st,
                      Storage::allocate(bytes, /*zero_init=*/false, allocator));
  return from_storage(std::move(st), shape, strides, dtype, 0, 1.0f);
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::zeros(const Shape& shape, DType dtype,
                             const Allocator& allocator) noexcept {
  if (shape.empty()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "Tensor::zeros: shape is empty");
  }
  const Strides strides = Strides::contiguous_for(shape);
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(validate_layout_for_dtype(shape, strides, dtype));

  Size bytes = 0;
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(checked_dtype_bytes(dtype, shape.numel(), &bytes));

  Ptr<Storage> st;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(st,
                      Storage::allocate(bytes, /*zero_init=*/true, allocator));
  return from_storage(std::move(st), shape, strides, dtype, 0, 1.0f);
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::full(const Shape& shape, f32 value, DType dtype,
                            const Allocator& allocator) noexcept {
  if (is_subbyte_dtype(dtype)) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kUnsupported,
        "Tensor::full does not support sub-byte DType '%s' directly",
        dtype_name(dtype));
  }
  Tensor out;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(out, empty(shape, dtype, allocator));
  const Index n = out.numel();

  switch (dtype) {
    case DType::kBool: {
      bool* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<bool>();
      const bool v = (value != 0.0f);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kFloat32: {
      f32* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<f32>();
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = value;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kFloat64: {
      f64* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<f64>();
      const f64 v = static_cast<f64>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kFloat16: {
      f16* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<f16>();
      const f16 v = f32_to_f16(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kBFloat16: {
      bf16* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<bf16>();
      const bf16 v = f32_to_bf16(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kInt8: {
      i8* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<i8>();
      const i8 v = static_cast<i8>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kUInt8: {
      u8* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<u8>();
      const u8 v = static_cast<u8>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kInt16: {
      i16* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<i16>();
      const i16 v = static_cast<i16>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kUInt16: {
      u16* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<u16>();
      const u16 v = static_cast<u16>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kInt32: {
      i32* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<i32>();
      const i32 v = static_cast<i32>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kUInt32: {
      u32* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<u32>();
      const u32 v = static_cast<u32>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kInt64: {
      i64* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<i64>();
      const i64 v = static_cast<i64>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    case DType::kUInt64: {
      u64* p = out.mutable_data_ptr<u64>();
      const u64 v = static_cast<u64>(value);
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i] = v;
      break;
    }
    default:
      return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kUnsupported,
                            "Tensor::full: unsupported DType '%s'",
                            dtype_name(dtype));
  }
  return out;
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::from_storage(Ptr<Storage> storage, const Shape& shape,
                                    const Strides& strides, DType dtype,
                                    Size byte_offset,
                                    f32 quant_scale) noexcept {
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(!storage)) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "Tensor::from_storage: storage is null");
  }
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(verify_storage_bounds(shape, strides, dtype, byte_offset,
                                           storage->size_bytes()));

  Tensor t;
  t.storage_ = std::move(storage);
  t.shape_ = shape;
  t.strides_ = strides;
  t.byte_offset_ = byte_offset;
  t.dtype_ = dtype;
  t.quant_scale_ = quant_scale;
  return t;
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::from_blob(void* data, const Shape& shape, DType dtype,
                                 bool is_mutable,
                                 const Allocator& meta_allocator) noexcept {
  if (shape.empty()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "Tensor::from_blob: shape is empty");
  }
  const Strides strides = Strides::contiguous_for(shape);
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(validate_layout_for_dtype(shape, strides, dtype));

  Size bytes = 0;
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(checked_dtype_bytes(dtype, shape.numel(), &bytes));

  Ptr<Storage> st;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(
      st, Storage::from_external(data, bytes, is_mutable, meta_allocator));
  return from_storage(std::move(st), shape, strides, dtype, 0, 1.0f);
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::clone(const Allocator& allocator) const noexcept {
  if (!is_valid()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "Tensor::clone: cannot clone invalid Tensor");
  }
  Tensor dst;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(dst, empty(shape_, dtype_, allocator));
  dst.set_quant_scale(quant_scale_);

  const Index count = numel();
  if (count == 0) return dst;

  if (is_contiguous()) {
    mem_copy(dst.raw_mutable_data(), raw_data(), dst.nbytes());
    return dst;
  }

  if (is_subbyte_dtype(dtype_)) {
    const Index blk = dtype_block_elements(dtype_);
    const Index last_dim = shape_[shape_.rank() - 1];
    const Size row_bytes = static_cast<Size>(last_dim / blk);
    const Index outer_rows = count / last_dim;

    for (Index r = 0; r < outer_rows; ++r) {
      Index rem = r;
      Index src_elem_off = 0;
      for (Index ax = shape_.rank() - 2; ax >= 0; --ax) {
        const Index coord = rem % shape_[ax];
        rem /= shape_[ax];
        src_elem_off += coord * strides_[ax];
      }
      const Size src_byte_off = static_cast<Size>(src_elem_off / blk);
      mem_copy(dst.raw_mutable_data() + static_cast<Size>(r) * row_bytes,
               raw_data() + src_byte_off, row_bytes);
    }
    return dst;
  }

  Index dst_idx = 0;
  copy_strided_recursive(raw_data(), dst.raw_mutable_data(), shape_, strides_,
                         dtype_element_size(dtype_), 0, 0, &dst_idx);
  return dst;
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::reshape(const Shape& new_shape) const noexcept {
  if (!is_valid()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "Tensor::reshape: invalid Tensor");
  }
  if (new_shape.empty()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "Tensor::reshape: new_shape cannot be empty");
  }
  if (new_shape.numel() != numel()) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kShapeMismatch,
        "Tensor::reshape: cannot reshape %lld elements into %lld elements",
        static_cast<long long>(numel()),
        static_cast<long long>(new_shape.numel()));
  }
  if (!is_contiguous()) {
    return Status::error(
        StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
        "Tensor::reshape: zero-copy reshape requires a contiguous tensor "
        "(call .contiguous() first)");
  }
  const Strides new_strides = Strides::contiguous_for(new_shape);
  return from_storage(storage_, new_shape, new_strides, dtype_, byte_offset_,
                      quant_scale_);
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::transpose(Index dim0, Index dim1) const noexcept {
  if (!is_valid()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "Tensor::transpose: invalid Tensor");
  }
  Index a0 = 0;
  Index a1 = 0;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(a0, shape_.normalize_axis(dim0));
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(a1, shape_.normalize_axis(dim1));

  Index new_dims[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  Index new_st[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  for (Index i = 0; i < shape_.rank(); ++i) {
    new_dims[i] = shape_[i];
    new_st[i] = strides_[i];
  }
  std::swap(new_dims[a0], new_dims[a1]);
  std::swap(new_st[a0], new_st[a1]);

  Shape s;
  Strides st;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(Span<const Index>(new_dims, rank())));
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(st,
                      Strides::from_strides(Span<const Index>(new_st, rank())));
  return from_storage(storage_, s, st, dtype_, byte_offset_, quant_scale_);
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::permute(Span<const Index> perm) const noexcept {
  if (!is_valid()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "Tensor::permute: invalid Tensor");
  }
  if (perm.size() != rank()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                         "Tensor::permute: permutation length != tensor rank");
  }

  bool seen[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  Index new_dims[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  Index new_st[kMaxTensorDims]{};

  for (Index i = 0; i < rank(); ++i) {
    Index ax = 0;
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(ax, shape_.normalize_axis(perm[i]));
    if (seen[ax]) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                           "Tensor::permute: duplicate axis in permutation");
    }
    seen[ax] = true;
    new_dims[i] = shape_[ax];
    new_st[i] = strides_[ax];
  }

  Shape s;
  Strides st;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(Span<const Index>(new_dims, rank())));
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(st,
                      Strides::from_strides(Span<const Index>(new_st, rank())));
  return from_storage(storage_, s, st, dtype_, byte_offset_, quant_scale_);
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::slice(Index axis, Index start,
                             Index end) const noexcept {
  if (!is_valid()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "Tensor::slice: invalid Tensor");
  }
  Index ax = 0;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(ax, shape_.normalize_axis(axis));

  const Index d = shape_[ax];
  if (start < 0) start += d;
  if (end < 0) end += d;
  if (start < 0 || end < start || end > d) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
        "Tensor::slice: invalid slice [%lld, %lld) for dimension of size %lld",
        static_cast<long long>(start), static_cast<long long>(end),
        static_cast<long long>(d));
  }

  const Index elem_delta = start * strides_[ax];
  Size byte_delta = 0;
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(checked_dtype_bytes(dtype_, elem_delta, &byte_delta));

  Index new_dims[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  for (Index i = 0; i < rank(); ++i) {
    new_dims[i] = (i == ax) ? (end - start) : shape_[i];
  }
  Shape s;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(Span<const Index>(new_dims, rank())));
  return from_storage(storage_, s, strides_, dtype_, byte_offset_ + byte_delta,
                      quant_scale_);
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::unsqueeze(Index axis) const noexcept {
  if (!is_valid()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "Tensor::unsqueeze: invalid Tensor");
  }
  if (rank() >= kMaxTensorDims) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                         "Tensor::unsqueeze: tensor already at kMaxTensorDims");
  }
  const Index new_rank = rank() + 1;
  const Index ax = axis < 0 ? (axis + new_rank) : axis;
  if (ax < 0 || ax >= new_rank) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                         "Tensor::unsqueeze: axis out of bounds");
  }

  Index new_dims[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  Index new_st[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  for (Index i = 0, src = 0; i < new_rank; ++i) {
    if (i == ax) {
      new_dims[i] = 1;
      if (src < rank()) {
        const Index d = shape_[src] > 0 ? shape_[src] : 1;
        new_st[i] = d * strides_[src];
      } else {
        new_st[i] = 1;
      }
    } else {
      new_dims[i] = shape_[src];
      new_st[i] = strides_[src];
      ++src;
    }
  }

  Shape s;
  Strides st;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s,
                      Shape::from_dims(Span<const Index>(new_dims, new_rank)));
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(
      st, Strides::from_strides(Span<const Index>(new_st, new_rank)));
  return from_storage(storage_, s, st, dtype_, byte_offset_, quant_scale_);
}

Result<Tensor> Tensor::squeeze(Index axis) const noexcept {
  if (!is_valid()) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
                         "Tensor::squeeze: invalid Tensor");
  }

  // Sentinel axis == -999 removes ALL size-1 dimensions.
  if (axis == -999) {
    if (rank() == 0) return view();
    Index new_dims[kMaxTensorDims]{};
    Index new_st[kMaxTensorDims]{};
    Index dst = 0;
    for (Index i = 0; i < rank(); ++i) {
      if (shape_[i] == 1) continue;
      new_dims[dst] = shape_[i];
      new_st[dst] = strides_[i];
      ++dst;
    }
    if (dst == 0) {
      return from_storage(storage_, Shape::scalar(), Strides(), dtype_,
                          byte_offset_, quant_scale_);
    }
    Shape s;
    Strides st;
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(Span<const Index>(new_dims, dst)));
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(st,
                        Strides::from_strides(Span<const Index>(new_st, dst)));
    return from_storage(storage_, s, st, dtype_, byte_offset_, quant_scale_);
  }

  Index ax = 0;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(ax, shape_.normalize_axis(axis));
  if (shape_[ax] != 1) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
        "Tensor::squeeze: dimension at axis %lld has size %lld (!= 1)",
        static_cast<long long>(ax), static_cast<long long>(shape_[ax]));
  }
  if (rank() == 1) {
    return from_storage(storage_, Shape::scalar(), Strides(), dtype_,
                        byte_offset_, quant_scale_);
  }

  Index new_dims[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  Index new_st[kMaxTensorDims]{};
  Index dst = 0;
  for (Index i = 0; i < rank(); ++i) {
    if (i == ax) continue;
    new_dims[dst] = shape_[i];
    new_st[dst] = strides_[i];
    ++dst;
  }

  Shape s;
  Strides st;
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(Span<const Index>(new_dims, dst)));
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(st,
                      Strides::from_strides(Span<const Index>(new_st, dst)));
  return from_storage(storage_, s, st, dtype_, byte_offset_, quant_scale_);
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./src/tensor/shape.cpp ==========
// src/tensor/shape.cpp
//
// Implementation of Shape, Strides, contiguity checks, broadcasting, and
// sub-byte layout validation.

#include "log3/tensor/shape.h"

#include <cstdio>

namespace l3 {

Result<Shape> Shape::from_dims(Span<const Index> dims) noexcept {
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(dims.size() < 0 || dims.size() > kMaxTensorDims)) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
        "Shape::from_dims: rank %lld exceeds maximum supported rank (%lld)",
        static_cast<long long>(dims.size()),
        static_cast<long long>(kMaxTensorDims));
  }

  Shape out;
  out.rank_ = dims.size();
  if (dims.empty()) {
    out.dims_[0] = 1;  // 0-D scalar
    return out;
  }

  // Always check overflow of the product of ALL non-zero dimensions even when
  // another axis is 0: `Strides::contiguous_for` multiplies all `d > 1`
