  constexpr const char& front() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    return data_[0];
  }

  constexpr const char& back() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    return data_[size_ - 1];
  }

  constexpr const_iterator begin() const noexcept { return data_; }
  constexpr const_iterator end() const noexcept { return data_ + size_; }

  // --- slicing & mutation of view --------------------------------------------
  constexpr StrView first(Index n) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(n, size_);
    return StrView(data_, n);
  }

  constexpr StrView last(Index n) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(n, size_);
    return StrView(data_ + (size_ - n), n);
  }

  // Safe substring starting at `offset` to the end of the view.
  // Clamps offset into [0, size_] so callers never trigger UB.
  constexpr StrView substr(Index offset) const noexcept {
    const Index clamped = offset < 0 ? 0 : (offset > size_ ? size_ : offset);
    return StrView(data_ + clamped, size_ - clamped);
  }

  // Safe substring [offset, offset + count). Clamps to valid range.
  constexpr StrView substr(Index offset, Index count) const noexcept {
    const Index start = offset < 0 ? 0 : (offset > size_ ? size_ : offset);
    const Index max_count = size_ - start;
    const Index len = count < 0 ? 0 : (count > max_count ? max_count : count);
    return StrView(data_ + start, len);
  }

  constexpr void remove_prefix(Index n) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(n, size_);
    data_ += n;
    size_ -= n;
  }

  constexpr void remove_suffix(Index n) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(n, size_);
    size_ -= n;
  }

  // --- comparison & predicates -----------------------------------------------
  int compare(StrView other) const noexcept {
    const Index min_len = size_ < other.size_ ? size_ : other.size_;
    if (min_len > 0) {
      const int cmp = std::memcmp(data_, other.data_, static_cast<usize>(min_len));
      if (cmp != 0) return cmp;
    }
    return size_ < other.size_ ? -1 : (size_ > other.size_ ? 1 : 0);
  }

  bool starts_with(StrView prefix) const noexcept {
    if (prefix.size_ > size_) return false;
    return prefix.size_ == 0 ||
           std::memcmp(data_, prefix.data_, static_cast<usize>(prefix.size_)) == 0;
  }

  constexpr bool starts_with(char c) const noexcept {
    return size_ > 0 && data_[0] == c;
  }

  bool ends_with(StrView suffix) const noexcept {
    if (suffix.size_ > size_) return false;
    return suffix.size_ == 0 ||
           std::memcmp(data_ + (size_ - suffix.size_), suffix.data_,
                       static_cast<usize>(suffix.size_)) == 0;
  }

  constexpr bool ends_with(char c) const noexcept {
    return size_ > 0 && data_[size_ - 1] == c;
  }

  // --- search ----------------------------------------------------------------
  constexpr Index find(char c, Index start_pos = 0) const noexcept {
    if (start_pos < 0) start_pos = 0;
    for (Index i = start_pos; i < size_; ++i) {
      if (data_[i] == c) return i;
    }
    return kNotFound;
  }

  Index find(StrView needle, Index start_pos = 0) const noexcept {
    if (start_pos < 0) start_pos = 0;
    if (needle.size_ == 0) return start_pos <= size_ ? start_pos : kNotFound;
    if (needle.size_ > size_ - start_pos) return kNotFound;

    const char first_ch = needle.data_[0];
    const Index last_start = size_ - needle.size_;
    for (Index i = start_pos; i <= last_start; ++i) {
      if (data_[i] == first_ch &&
          std::memcmp(data_ + i, needle.data_,
                      static_cast<usize>(needle.size_)) == 0) {
        return i;
      }
    }
    return kNotFound;
  }

  constexpr Index rfind(char c) const noexcept {
    for (Index i = size_ - 1; i >= 0; --i) {
      if (data_[i] == c) return i;
    }
    return kNotFound;
  }

  constexpr bool contains(char c) const noexcept {
    return find(c) != kNotFound;
  }

  bool contains(StrView needle) const noexcept {
    return find(needle) != kNotFound;
  }

  // --- whitespace trimming (ASCII whitespace: ' ', '\t', '\n', '\r', '\v', '\f')
  static constexpr bool is_ascii_space(char c) noexcept {
    return c == ' ' || c == '\t' || c == '\n' || c == '\r' || c == '\v' ||
           c == '\f';
  }

  constexpr StrView trim_left() const noexcept {
    Index i = 0;
    while (i < size_ && is_ascii_space(data_[i])) {
      ++i;
    }
    return StrView(data_ + i, size_ - i);
  }

  constexpr StrView trim_right() const noexcept {
    Index len = size_;
    while (len > 0 && is_ascii_space(data_[len - 1])) {
      --len;
    }
    return StrView(data_, len);
  }

  constexpr StrView trim() const noexcept {
    return trim_left().trim_right();
  }

 private:
  const char* data_;
  Index size_;
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Equality and relational operators
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
inline bool operator==(StrView a, StrView b) noexcept {
  if (a.size() != b.size()) return false;
  return a.size() == 0 ||
         std::memcmp(a.data(), b.data(), static_cast<usize>(a.size())) == 0;
}

inline bool operator!=(StrView a, StrView b) noexcept { return !(a == b); }
inline bool operator<(StrView a, StrView b) noexcept { return a.compare(b) < 0; }
inline bool operator<=(StrView a, StrView b) noexcept { return a.compare(b) <= 0; }
inline bool operator>(StrView a, StrView b) noexcept { return a.compare(b) > 0; }
inline bool operator>=(StrView a, StrView b) noexcept { return a.compare(b) >= 0; }

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// High-speed 64-bit hashing (replaces std::hash)
//
// Uses SplitMix64 finalizer for integers and 8-byte unaligned block mixing
// for strings/byte buffers. Guarantees never returning 0 if a caller needs a
// non-zero hash tag, though raw functions below return full 64-bit range.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Fast 64-bit integer avalanche mixer (SplitMix64).
constexpr u64 hash_u64(u64 x) noexcept {
  x ^= x >> 30;
  x *= 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
  x ^= x >> 27;
  x *= 0x94d049bb133111ebULL;
  x ^= x >> 31;
  return x;
}

// Combine two 64-bit hashes.
constexpr u64 hash_combine(u64 seed, u64 value) noexcept {
  return seed ^ (hash_u64(value) + 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL + (seed << 6) + (seed >> 2));
}

// High-speed 64-bit byte-buffer hash (processes 8 bytes per iteration).
inline u64 hash_bytes(const void* data, Size len,
                      u64 seed = 0xa0761d6478bd642fULL) noexcept {
  const auto* p = static_cast<const u8*>(data);
  u64 h = seed ^ (static_cast<u64>(len) * 0xe7037ed1a0b428dbULL);

  while (len >= 8) {
    u64 v = 0;
    std::memcpy(&v, p, sizeof(u64));
    h = hash_u64(h ^ v);
    p += 8;
    len -= 8;
  }

  if (len >= 4) {
    u32 v32 = 0;
    std::memcpy(&v32, p, sizeof(u32));
    h ^= static_cast<u64>(v32) << 32;
    p += 4;
    len -= 4;
  }

  u64 tail = 0;
  for (Size i = 0; i < len; ++i) {
    tail = (tail << 8) | static_cast<u64>(p[i]);
  }
  if (tail != 0 || len > 0) {
    h = hash_u64(h ^ tail);
  }

  return hash_u64(h);
}

inline u64 hash_str(StrView s) noexcept {
  return hash_bytes(s.data(), s.size_bytes());
}

// Default Hasher trait used by FlatMap<K, V>.
template <typename Key, typename Enable = void>
struct Hash {
  static_assert(sizeof(Key) == 0,
                "l3::Hash<Key> is not specialized for this type.");
};

template <>
struct Hash<StrView> {
  u64 operator()(StrView s) const noexcept { return hash_str(s); }
};

template <typename T>
struct Hash<T, std::enable_if_t<std::is_integral_v<T> || std::is_enum_v<T>>> {
  constexpr u64 operator()(T v) const noexcept {
    return hash_u64(static_cast<u64>(v));
  }
};

template <typename T>
struct Hash<T*> {
  u64 operator()(T* ptr) const noexcept {
    return hash_u64(reinterpret_cast< std::uintptr_t>(ptr));
  }
};

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/cpu_features.h ==========
// log3/core/cpu_features.h
//
// Runtime CPU instruction set architecture (ISA) and cache topology detection.
//
// Design notes:
//   * Detected once at startup (or on first query) in a thread-safe manner and
//     cached in a global structure, so hot-path kernel dispatch costs zero overhead
//     (just a boolean check or bitfield lookup).
//   * Supports x86-64 (SSE2, AVX, AVX2, AVX-512F, AVX-512VNNI, F16C, FMA) and
//     ARM64 (NEON, FP16 arithmetic, DotProd, I8MM for sub-byte quantization).

#pragma once

#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

struct CpuFeatures {
  // x86 / x64 features
  bool has_sse2;
  bool has_sse41;
  bool has_sse42;
  bool has_avx;
  bool has_avx2;
  bool has_fma;
  bool has_f16c;
  bool has_avx512f;
  bool has_avx512bw;
  bool has_avx512dq;
  bool has_avx512_vnni;  // Vector Neural Network Instructions (int8/int4 dp)

  // ARM / ARM64 features
  bool has_neon;
  bool has_neon_fp16;
  bool has_neon_dotprod;  // UDOT/SDOT (8-bit dot products)
  bool has_neon_i8mm;     // Matmul extension (AArch64 v8.6+)

  // Cache & topology
  int logical_cores;
  int physical_cores;
  usize l1d_cache_size;  // L1 data cache size in bytes (0 if unknown)
  usize l2_cache_size;   // L2 cache size in bytes (0 if unknown)
  usize l3_cache_size;   // L3 cache size in bytes (0 if unknown)
  usize cache_line_size; // Typically 64 bytes
};

// Retrieve the singleton CpuFeatures structure (computed once, thread-safe).
L3_API const CpuFeatures& cpu_features() noexcept;

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/status.h ==========

// log3/core/status.h
//
// Status and Result primitives used throughout the log3 library.
//
// Responsibilities:
//   * Represent successful and failed operations.
//   * Provide structured error codes and diagnostic messages.
//   * Support move-only error ownership.
//   * Provide value-or-error abstraction through Result<T>.
//   * Provide explicit error propagation macros.
//
// Compatibility:
//   * C++17
//   * No exceptions
//   * No RTTI

#pragma once

#include <new>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Status codes
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

enum class StatusCode : u8 {
  kOk = 0,
  kInvalidArgument,
  kOutOfRange,
  kShapeMismatch,
  kTypeMismatch,
  kNotFound,
  kAlreadyExists,
  kFailedPrecondition,
  kOutOfMemory,
  kUnimplemented,
  kUnsupported,
  kIoError,
  kInternal,
};

constexpr const char* status_code_name(StatusCode code) noexcept {
  switch (code) {
    case StatusCode::kOk:
      return "Ok";
    case StatusCode::kInvalidArgument:
      return "InvalidArgument";
    case StatusCode::kOutOfRange:
      return "OutOfRange";
    case StatusCode::kShapeMismatch:
      return "ShapeMismatch";
    case StatusCode::kTypeMismatch:
      return "TypeMismatch";
    case StatusCode::kNotFound:
      return "NotFound";
    case StatusCode::kAlreadyExists:
      return "AlreadyExists";
    case StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition:
      return "FailedPrecondition";
    case StatusCode::kOutOfMemory:
      return "OutOfMemory";
    case StatusCode::kUnimplemented:
      return "Unimplemented";
    case StatusCode::kUnsupported:
      return "Unsupported";
    case StatusCode::kIoError:
      return "IoError";
    case StatusCode::kInternal:
      return "Internal";
  }

  return "Unknown";
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Status
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// A successful Status owns no allocation.
// An error Status owns one internal State.
//
// Status is move-only. Moving transfers ownership and leaves the source OK.

class L3_NODISCARD Status {
public:
  struct State;

  Status() noexcept : state_(nullptr) {}

  Status(Status&& other) noexcept
      : state_(other.state_) {
    other.state_ = nullptr;
  }

  Status& operator=(Status&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
      reset();

      state_ = other.state_;
      other.state_ = nullptr;
    }

    return *this;
  }

  Status(const Status&) = delete;
  Status& operator=(const Status&) = delete;

  ~Status() noexcept {
    reset();
  }

  static Status ok() noexcept {
    return Status();
  }

  L3_API static Status error(
      StatusCode code,
      const char* message) noexcept;

  L3_API static Status errorf(
      StatusCode code,
      const char* fmt,
      ...) noexcept L3_PRINTF_FORMAT(2, 3);

  bool is_ok() const noexcept {
    return state_ == nullptr;
  }

  L3_API StatusCode code() const noexcept;

  L3_API const char* message() const noexcept;

  bool is(StatusCode expected) const noexcept {
    return code() == expected;
  }

  L3_API Status clone() const noexcept;

  // Explicitly acknowledge an intentionally ignored status.
  void ignore() const noexcept {}

private:
  void reset() noexcept {
    if (state_ != nullptr) {
      destroy(state_);
      state_ = nullptr;
    }
  }

  L3_API static void destroy(State* state) noexcept;

  State* state_;
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Result access failure
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

namespace detail {

[[noreturn]] L3_API void result_access_failed(
    const Status& status) noexcept;

}  // namespace detail

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Result<T>
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Result<T> represents either:
//
//   1. A successfully constructed T.
//   2. A non-OK Status.
//
// Invariant:
//
//   has_value_ == true  => status_ is OK and T is alive.
//   has_value_ == false => status_ is non-OK and T is not alive.
//
// Moving a value leaves a valid moved-from T in the source Result.
// Moving an error leaves a FailedPrecondition Status in the source.
//
// T requirements:
//   * Object type.
//   * Not an array, reference, const, or volatile type.
//   * Not Status.
//   * Nothrow move-constructible.
//   * Nothrow destructible.

template <typename T>
class L3_NODISCARD Result {
  static_assert(
      std::is_object<T>::value,
      "Result<T> requires an object type.");

  static_assert(
      !std::is_array<T>::value,
      "Result<T> does not support arrays.");

  static_assert(
    !std::is_const<T>::value &&
    !std::is_volatile<T>::value,
    "Result<T> does not support cv-qualified types.");

  static_assert(
      !std::is_same<T, Status>::value,
      "Result<Status> is meaningless.");

  static_assert(
      std::is_nothrow_move_constructible<T>::value,
      "Result<T> requires nothrow move construction.");

  static_assert(
      std::is_nothrow_destructible<T>::value,
      "Result<T> requires nothrow destruction.");

public:
  Result(T&& value) noexcept
      : status_(),
        has_value_(true) {
    ::new (static_cast<void*>(storage_)) T(std::move(value));
  }

  // Only allow copying when copying cannot throw.
  template <
      typename U = T,
      typename std::enable_if<
          std::is_same<U, T>::value &&
          std::is_nothrow_copy_constructible<U>::value,
          int>::type = 0>
  Result(const T& value) noexcept
      : status_(),
        has_value_(true) {
    ::new (static_cast<void*>(storage_)) T(value);
  }

  // Construct an error Result.
  // Passing OK is converted into an internal error.
  Result(Status&& status) noexcept
      : status_(std::move(status)),
        has_value_(false) {
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(status_.is_ok())) {
      status_ = Status::error(
          StatusCode::kInternal,
          "Result<T> constructed from OK Status");
    }
  }

  // Move-construct a Result.
  Result(Result&& other) noexcept
      : status_(std::move(other.status_)),
        has_value_(other.has_value_) {

    if (has_value_) {
      // Keep the source's moved-from T alive.
      ::new (static_cast<void*>(storage_)) T(
          std::move(*other.ptr()));
    } else {
      // The source must remain a valid error Result.
      other.status_ = Status::error(
          StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
          "Result was moved from");
    }
  }

  Result& operator=(Result&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
      destroy_value();

      status_ = std::move(other.status_);
      has_value_ = other.has_value_;

      if (has_value_) {
        // Source remains engaged with a valid moved-from T.
        ::new (static_cast<void*>(storage_)) T(
            std::move(*other.ptr()));
      } else {
        other.status_ = Status::error(
            StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
            "Result was moved from");
      }
    }

    return *this;
  }

  Result(const Result&) = delete;
  Result& operator=(const Result&) = delete;

  ~Result() noexcept {
    destroy_value();
  }

  bool is_ok() const noexcept {
    return has_value_;
  }

  const Status& status() const noexcept {
    return status_;
  }

  // Transfer the error status out.
  //
  // The Result remains valid after this operation.
  // Its new state is FailedPrecondition.
  Status take_status() noexcept {
    if (has_value_) {
      return Status::ok();
    }

    Status result(std::move(status_));

    status_ = Status::error(
        StatusCode::kFailedPrecondition,
        "Result status was moved out");

    return result;
  }

  T& value() & {
    check_value();
    return *ptr();
  }

  const T& value() const& {
    check_value();
    return *ptr();
  }

  T&& value() && {
    check_value();
    return std::move(*ptr());
  }

  // Move the value out.
  // The Result retains its moved-from T object.
  T take_value() {
    check_value();
    return std::move(*ptr());
  }

  T& operator*() & {
    return value();
  }

  const T& operator*() const& {
    return value();
  }

  T* operator->() {
    check_value();
    return ptr();
  }

  const T* operator->() const {
    check_value();
    return ptr();
  }

private:
  T* ptr() noexcept {
    return std::launder(
        reinterpret_cast<T*>(storage_));
  }

  const T* ptr() const noexcept {
    return std::launder(
        reinterpret_cast<const T*>(storage_));
  }

  void check_value() const {
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!has_value_)) {
      detail::result_access_failed(status_);
    }
  }

  void destroy_value() noexcept {
    if (has_value_) {
      ptr()->~T();
      has_value_ = false;
    }
  }

  Status status_;
  bool has_value_;

  alignas(alignof(T)) byte storage_[sizeof(T)];
};

}  // namespace l3

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Error propagation macros
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR:
//   Evaluates a Status expression once.
//   Returns immediately when it contains an error.
//
// L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN:
//   Evaluates a Result expression once.
//   Returns its error when no value exists.
//   Otherwise moves its value into lhs.
//
// L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN intentionally expands to multiple statements
// so declarations remain visible in the caller's scope.
//
// Always use braces when placing it inside an if/else body.

#define L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR_IMPL(status_var, expr) \
  do { \
    ::l3::Status status_var = (expr); \
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!status_var.is_ok())) { \
      return status_var; \
    } \
  } while (0)

#define L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(expr) \
  L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR_IMPL( \
      L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_status_), expr)

#define L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN_IMPL(result_var, lhs, expr) \
  auto result_var = (expr); \
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(!result_var.is_ok())) { \
    return result_var.take_status(); \
  } \
  lhs = result_var.take_value()

#define L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(lhs, expr) \
  L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN_IMPL( \
      L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_result_), lhs, expr)


========== ./include/log3/core/span.h ==========
// log3/core/span.h
//
// Span<T>: a non-owning view of a contiguous run of T (pointer + length).
// A C++17 stand-in for std::span with log3 conventions:
//
//   * size() returns Index (SIGNED, i64), like every shape/stride/offset in
//     the library. `for (Index i = 0; i < s.size(); ++i)` has no sign-compare
//     warnings and no unsigned-underflow traps. size_bytes() returns Size.
//   * Bounds are checked with L3_DCHECK (debug builds); release builds pay
//     nothing. Validate user input with Status before building a Span.
//   * Dynamic extent only (no Span<T, N>): shapes and strides are runtime
//     sized, and a fixed extent would only add template bloat.
//   * Trivially copyable, two words wide, passed by value everywhere.
//   * Never owns, never allocates, never frees. The viewed memory must outlive
//     the Span (like a raw pointer).
//
//   int a[4] = {1, 2, 3, 4};
//   Span<int> s(a);                      // from array
//   Span<const int> c = s;               // Span<T> -> Span<const T>
//   Span<int> mid = s.subspan(1, 2);     // {2, 3}
//   Span s2(ptr, count);                 // class template argument deduction
//   for (int v : s) { ... }              // iterators are plain pointers
//
// Anything with data() and size() converts (SmallVec, Vec, arrays wrappers).
// Temporaries do not: a Span of a temporary container would dangle.

#pragma once

#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

template <typename T>
class Span;

namespace detail {

template <typename X>
struct IsSpan : std::false_type {};
template <typename X>
struct IsSpan<Span<X>> : std::true_type {};

// A container C is viewable as Span<T> if c.data() yields a pointer whose
// element type converts to T by qualification only (int -> const int is fine,
// Derived -> Base is rejected: that would be slicing) and c.size() is integral.
template <typename C, typename T, typename = void>
struct IsViewableAs : std::false_type {};

template <typename C, typename T>
struct IsViewableAs<
    C, T,
    std::void_t<decltype(std::declval<C&>().data()),
                decltype(std::declval<C&>().size())>>
    : std::bool_constant<
          std::is_convertible_v<
              std::remove_pointer_t<decltype(std::declval<C&>().data())> (*)[],
              T (*)[]> &&
          std::is_integral_v<decltype(std::declval<C&>().size())> &&
          !IsSpan<std::remove_cv_t<C>>::value> {};

}  // namespace detail

template <typename T>
class Span {
  static_assert(!std::is_reference_v<T>, "Span<T&> is not allowed.");
  static_assert(!std::is_void_v<T>,
                "Span<void> is not allowed; use Span<byte> for raw memory.");

 public:
  using element_type = T;
  using value_type = std::remove_cv_t<T>;
  using pointer = T*;
  using reference = T&;
  using iterator = T*;

  // --- construction ---------------------------------------------------------
  constexpr Span() noexcept : data_(nullptr), size_(0) {}

  constexpr Span(T* data, Index size) noexcept : data_(data), size_(size) {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(size, 0);
    L3_DCHECK(size == 0 || data != nullptr);
  }

  template <usize N>
  constexpr Span(T (&array)[N]) noexcept
      : data_(array), size_(static_cast<Index>(N)) {}

  template <typename C,
            typename = std::enable_if_t<detail::IsViewableAs<C, T>::value>>
  constexpr Span(C& container) noexcept
      : data_(container.data()), size_(static_cast<Index>(container.size())) {}

  // Span<U> -> Span<T> (e.g. Span<int> -> Span<const int>).
  template <typename U,
            typename = std::enable_if_t<
                std::is_convertible_v<U (*)[], T (*)[]>>>
  constexpr Span(const Span<U>& other) noexcept
      : data_(other.data()), size_(other.size()) {}

  // --- observers ------------------------------------------------------------
  constexpr T* data() const noexcept { return data_; }
  constexpr Index size() const noexcept { return size_; }
  constexpr Size size_bytes() const noexcept {
    return static_cast<Size>(size_) * sizeof(T);
  }
  constexpr bool empty() const noexcept { return size_ == 0; }

  // --- element access -------------------------------------------------------
  constexpr T& operator[](Index i) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(i, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LT(i, size_);
    return data_[i];
  }

  constexpr T& front() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    return data_[0];
  }

  constexpr T& back() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    return data_[size_ - 1];
  }

  // --- iteration (raw pointers) ---------------------------------------------
  constexpr iterator begin() const noexcept { return data_; }
  constexpr iterator end() const noexcept { return data_ + size_; }

  // --- sub-views ------------------------------------------------------------
  // First n elements.
  constexpr Span first(Index n) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(n, size_);
    return Span(data_, n);
  }

  // Last n elements.
  constexpr Span last(Index n) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(n, size_);
    return Span(data_ + (size_ - n), n);
  }

  // Elements [offset, size()).
  constexpr Span subspan(Index offset) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(offset, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(offset, size_);
    return Span(data_ + offset, size_ - offset);
  }

  // Elements [offset, offset + count).
  constexpr Span subspan(Index offset, Index count) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(offset, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_GE(count, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(offset, size_);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(count, size_ - offset);
    return Span(data_ + offset, count);
  }

 private:
  T* data_;
  Index size_;
};

// --- class template argument deduction -----------------------------------------
template <typename T>
Span(T*, Index) -> Span<T>;

template <typename T, usize N>
Span(T (&)[N]) -> Span<T>;

template <typename C>
Span(C&) -> Span<std::remove_pointer_t<decltype(std::declval<C&>().data())>>;

// --- byte views ------------------------------------------------------------------
using ByteSpan = Span<byte>;
using ConstByteSpan = Span<const byte>;

template <typename T>
ConstByteSpan as_bytes(Span<T> s) noexcept {
  return ConstByteSpan(reinterpret_cast<const byte*>(s.data()),
                       static_cast<Index>(s.size_bytes()));
}

template <typename T>
ByteSpan as_writable_bytes(Span<T> s) noexcept {
  static_assert(!std::is_const_v<T>,
                "as_writable_bytes: cannot get writable bytes of a const Span.");
  return ByteSpan(reinterpret_cast<byte*>(s.data()),
                  static_cast<Index>(s.size_bytes()));
}

// --- comparison ------------------------------------------------------------------
// Element-wise equality (same length and a[i] == b[i] for all i).
template <typename A, typename B>
bool equal(Span<A> a, Span<B> b) noexcept {
  if (a.size() != b.size()) return false;
  for (Index i = 0; i < a.size(); ++i) {
    if (!(a[i] == b[i])) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/small_vector.h ==========
// log3/core/small_vector.h
//
// SmallVec<T, N>: a growable array that keeps its first N elements INSIDE the
// object (no heap allocation) and only touches the heap when it grows past N.
// Built for shapes and strides: a tensor with <= 8 dims never allocates.
//
//   SmallVec<Index, 8> shape;
//   L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(shape.push_back(32));
//   L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(shape.push_back(128));
//   Span<const Index> view(shape);          // converts to Span (data()/size())
//
// log3 conventions (this class is where they matter most):
//
//   * No exceptions. Every operation that may need memory returns Status
//     (kOutOfMemory, or kOutOfRange on size overflow). On failure the
//     container is left UNCHANGED (strong guarantee), so a failed push_back
//     never loses data.
//   * Move-only. Copying can allocate (and therefore fail), so there is no
//     copy constructor. Use clone() / assign() / from_span(), which report
//     errors. Moves are noexcept and never allocate.
//   * size()/capacity()/indices are signed Index, like Span and shapes.
//   * Allocator-aware: grows through log3's Allocator (default_allocator()
//     unless you pass one). The Allocator must outlive the SmallVec. A moved
//     SmallVec carries its allocator with it.
//   * T must be nothrow-move-constructible (the core never throws, so growth
//     cannot be allowed to fail halfway). Trivially copyable T (Index, f32,
//     ...) is relocated with a single memcpy.
//   * Aliasing is safe where it is cheap to be safe: v.push_back(v[0]) and
//     v.insert(i, v[j]) work even when they reallocate. assign()/append() with
//     a Span that points INTO the same vector is not supported.
//
// Bounds are checked with L3_DCHECK (debug only), like Span.

#pragma once

#include <new>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/memory.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/span.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

template <typename T, Index N>
class SmallVec {
  static_assert(N > 0, "SmallVec: inline capacity N must be at least 1.");
  static_assert(!std::is_const_v<T> && !std::is_reference_v<T>,
                "SmallVec<T>: T must be a non-const object type.");
  static_assert(std::is_nothrow_move_constructible_v<T>,
                "SmallVec<T>: T must be nothrow move constructible.");
  static_assert(std::is_nothrow_destructible_v<T>,
                "SmallVec<T>: T must be nothrow destructible.");

 public:
  using value_type = T;
  using iterator = T*;
  using const_iterator = const T*;

  static constexpr Index kInlineCapacity = N;

  // --- construction / destruction -------------------------------------------
  SmallVec() noexcept
      : data_(inline_data()), size_(0), capacity_(N), allocator_(nullptr) {}

  // `allocator` must outlive this SmallVec (and anything moved out of it).
  explicit SmallVec(const Allocator& allocator) noexcept
      : data_(inline_data()), size_(0), capacity_(N), allocator_(&allocator) {}

  SmallVec(SmallVec&& other) noexcept
      : data_(inline_data()),
        size_(0),
        capacity_(N),
        allocator_(other.allocator_) {
    take(other);
  }

  SmallVec& operator=(SmallVec&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
      clear();
      release_heap();
      allocator_ = other.allocator_;
      take(other);
    }
    return *this;
  }

  SmallVec(const SmallVec&) = delete;
  SmallVec& operator=(const SmallVec&) = delete;

  ~SmallVec() {
    clear();
    release_heap();
  }

  // Explicit, fallible copies.
  Result<SmallVec> clone() const noexcept {
    SmallVec out(allocator());
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(out.assign(span()));
    return out;
  }

  static Result<SmallVec> from_span(
      Span<const T> src, const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    SmallVec out(allocator);
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(out.assign(src));
    return out;
  }

  // --- observers --------------------------------------------------------------
  T* data() noexcept { return data_; }
  const T* data() const noexcept { return data_; }
  Index size() const noexcept { return size_; }
  Index capacity() const noexcept { return capacity_; }
  bool empty() const noexcept { return size_ == 0; }

  // True while the elements live inside the object (no heap block in use).
  bool is_inline() const noexcept { return data_ == inline_data(); }

  const Allocator& get_allocator() const noexcept { return allocator(); }

  Span<T> span() noexcept { return Span<T>(data_, size_); }
  Span<const T> span() const noexcept { return Span<const T>(data_, size_); }

  // --- element access -----------------------------------------------------------
  T& operator[](Index i) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(i, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LT(i, size_);
    return data_[i];
  }
  const T& operator[](Index i) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(i, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LT(i, size_);
    return data_[i];
  }

  T& front() noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    return data_[0];
  }
  const T& front() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    return data_[0];
  }
  T& back() noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    return data_[size_ - 1];
  }
  const T& back() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    return data_[size_ - 1];
  }

  iterator begin() noexcept { return data_; }
  iterator end() noexcept { return data_ + size_; }
  const_iterator begin() const noexcept { return data_; }
  const_iterator end() const noexcept { return data_ + size_; }

  // --- capacity -------------------------------------------------------------------
  // Make room for at least n elements (exact, like std::vector::reserve).
  Status reserve(Index n) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    if (n <= capacity_) return Status::ok();
    return grow_to(n);
  }

  // --- modifiers --------------------------------------------------------------------
  void clear() noexcept {
    destroy_range(data_, size_);
    size_ = 0;
  }

  template <typename... Args>
  Status emplace_back(Args&&... args) noexcept {
    if (L3_LIKELY(size_ < capacity_)) {
      ::new (static_cast<void*>(data_ + size_)) T(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
      ++size_;
      return Status::ok();
    }
    return emplace_back_slow(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
  }

  Status push_back(const T& value) noexcept { return emplace_back(value); }
  Status push_back(T&& value) noexcept { return emplace_back(std::move(value)); }

  void pop_back() noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GT(size_, 0);
    --size_;
    data_[size_].~T();
  }

  // Grow with value-initialized elements (zeros for numbers) or shrink.
  Status resize(Index n) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    if (n <= size_) {
      destroy_range(data_ + n, size_ - n);
      size_ = n;
      return Status::ok();
    }
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(ensure_capacity(n));
    for (Index i = size_; i < n; ++i) ::new (static_cast<void*>(data_ + i)) T();
    size_ = n;
    return Status::ok();
  }

  Status resize(Index n, const T& value) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(n, 0);
    if (n <= size_) {
      destroy_range(data_ + n, size_ - n);
      size_ = n;
      return Status::ok();
    }
    T copy(value);  // `value` may live inside this vector
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(ensure_capacity(n));
    for (Index i = size_; i < n; ++i) ::new (static_cast<void*>(data_ + i)) T(copy);
    size_ = n;
    return Status::ok();
  }

  // Replace the contents with a copy of src. src must not point into *this.
  Status assign(Span<const T> src) noexcept {
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(ensure_capacity(src.size()));  // before touching data
    clear();
    copy_construct(data_, src.data(), src.size());
    size_ = src.size();
    return Status::ok();
  }

  // Append a copy of src. src must not point into *this.
  Status append(Span<const T> src) noexcept {
    Size total = 0;
    if (!checked_add(static_cast<Size>(size_), static_cast<Size>(src.size()), &total) ||
        total > static_cast<Size>(kMaxIndex)) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange, "SmallVec: size overflow");
    }
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(ensure_capacity(static_cast<Index>(total)));
    copy_construct(data_ + size_, src.data(), src.size());
    size_ += src.size();
    return Status::ok();
  }

  // Insert value before position pos (0 <= pos <= size()). Later elements
  // shift right. `value` is taken by value so it can alias an element.
  Status insert(Index pos, T value) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(pos, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(pos, size_);
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(ensure_capacity(size_ + 1));
    if (pos == size_) {
      ::new (static_cast<void*>(data_ + size_)) T(std::move(value));
    } else {
      ::new (static_cast<void*>(data_ + size_)) T(std::move(data_[size_ - 1]));
      for (Index i = size_ - 1; i > pos; --i) data_[i] = std::move(data_[i - 1]);
      data_[pos] = std::move(value);
    }
    ++size_;
    return Status::ok();
  }

  // Remove the element at pos; later elements shift left.
  void erase(Index pos) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(pos, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LT(pos, size_);
    erase(pos, pos + 1);
  }

  // Remove elements [first, last).
  void erase(Index first, Index last) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(first, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(first, last);
    L3_DCHECK_LE(last, size_);
    const Index count = last - first;
    if (count == 0) return;
    for (Index i = last; i < size_; ++i) data_[i - count] = std::move(data_[i]);
    destroy_range(data_ + (size_ - count), count);
    size_ -= count;
  }

 private:
  static constexpr Size kAlign =
      alignof(T) > kMinObjectAlignment ? alignof(T) : kMinObjectAlignment;

  T* inline_data() noexcept { return reinterpret_cast<T*>(inline_buf_); }
  const T* inline_data() const noexcept {
    return reinterpret_cast<const T*>(inline_buf_);
  }

  const Allocator& allocator() const noexcept {
    return allocator_ != nullptr ? *allocator_ : default_allocator();
  }

  // Move other's elements into *this, which must be empty and inline.
  void take(SmallVec& other) noexcept {
    if (other.is_inline()) {
      relocate(data_, other.data_, other.size_);
      size_ = other.size_;
      other.size_ = 0;
    } else {
      data_ = other.data_;
      size_ = other.size_;
      capacity_ = other.capacity_;
      other.data_ = other.inline_data();
      other.size_ = 0;
      other.capacity_ = N;
    }
  }

  // Free the heap block (elements must already be destroyed or relocated) and
  // fall back to the inline buffer.
  void release_heap() noexcept {
    if (!is_inline()) {
      allocator().deallocate(data_, static_cast<Size>(capacity_) * sizeof(T), kAlign);
    }
    data_ = inline_data();
    capacity_ = N;
  }

  Status allocate_buffer(Index capacity, T** out) const noexcept {
    Size bytes = 0;
    if (!checked_mul(static_cast<Size>(capacity), sizeof(T), &bytes)) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange, "SmallVec: capacity overflow");
    }
    void* mem = allocator().allocate(bytes, kAlign);
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(mem == nullptr)) {
      return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kOutOfMemory,
                            "SmallVec: failed to allocate %zu bytes", bytes);
    }
    *out = static_cast<T*>(mem);
    return Status::ok();
  }

  Index next_capacity(Index min_needed) const noexcept {
    const Index doubled = capacity_ > kMaxIndex / 2 ? kMaxIndex : capacity_ * 2;
    return doubled < min_needed ? min_needed : doubled;
  }

  // Move to a bigger heap block of exactly new_cap elements (new_cap > capacity_).
  Status grow_to(Index new_cap) noexcept {
    T* buffer = nullptr;
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(allocate_buffer(new_cap, &buffer));
    relocate(buffer, data_, size_);
    release_heap();
    data_ = buffer;
    capacity_ = new_cap;
    return Status::ok();
  }

  // Amortized growth: at least double.
  Status ensure_capacity(Index min_needed) noexcept {
    if (L3_LIKELY(min_needed <= capacity_)) return Status::ok();
    return grow_to(next_capacity(min_needed));
  }

  // Slow path of emplace_back. The new element is constructed in the new
  // buffer BEFORE the old one is released, so args may refer to old elements.
  template <typename... Args>
  L3_NOINLINE Status emplace_back_slow(Args&&... args) noexcept {
    const Index new_cap = next_capacity(size_ + 1);
    T* buffer = nullptr;
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(allocate_buffer(new_cap, &buffer));
    ::new (static_cast<void*>(buffer + size_)) T(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    relocate(buffer, data_, size_);
    release_heap();
    data_ = buffer;
    capacity_ = new_cap;
    ++size_;
    return Status::ok();
  }

  // Move n elements from src to uninitialized dst, destroying the sources.
  static void relocate(T* dst, T* src, Index n) noexcept {
    if constexpr (std::is_trivially_copyable_v<T>) {
      mem_copy(dst, src, static_cast<Size>(n) * sizeof(T));
    } else {
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) {
        ::new (static_cast<void*>(dst + i)) T(std::move(src[i]));
        src[i].~T();
      }
    }
  }

  static void copy_construct(T* dst, const T* src, Index n) noexcept {
    if constexpr (std::is_trivially_copyable_v<T>) {
      mem_copy(dst, src, static_cast<Size>(n) * sizeof(T));
    } else {
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) ::new (static_cast<void*>(dst + i)) T(src[i]);
    }
  }

  static void destroy_range(T* p, Index n) noexcept {
    if constexpr (!std::is_trivially_destructible_v<T>) {
      for (Index i = 0; i < n; ++i) p[i].~T();
    } else {
      (void)p;
      (void)n;
    }
  }

  T* data_;
  Index size_;
  Index capacity_;
  const Allocator* allocator_;  // nullptr = default_allocator()
  L3_ALIGNAS(alignof(T)) byte inline_buf_[sizeof(T) * static_cast<usize>(N)];
};

// Element-wise equality; the inline capacities may differ.
template <typename T, Index N, Index M>
bool operator==(const SmallVec<T, N>& a, const SmallVec<T, M>& b) noexcept {
  return equal(a.span(), b.span());
}

template <typename T, Index N, Index M>
bool operator!=(const SmallVec<T, N>& a, const SmallVec<T, M>& b) noexcept {
  return !(a == b);
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/half.h ==========
// log3/core/half.h
//
// Half-precision floating-point conversions and utilities (f16 and bf16).
//
// Design notes:
//   * types.h defines f16 and bf16 as 2-byte storage structs so tensor headers
//     stay lightweight. All scalar conversions, constants, and batch helpers
//     live here.
//   * Pure integer bit-manipulation with round-to-nearest-even (IEEE 754
//     default). Does not depend on FPU rounding modes or flush-to-zero (FTZ)
//     control registers, guaranteeing bit-identical results across x86-64 and
//     ARM64 targets.
//   * Subnormals, signed zeros, infinities, and NaNs are handled accurately;
//     signaling NaNs are quieted on conversion.
//   * Vectorized bulk conversions (AVX2/AVX-512 F16C and ARM64 NEON FP16) live
//     in the SIMD kernel layer; the array helpers here provide the portable
//     baseline and tail-loop fallback.

#pragma once

#include <cstring>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Bit-cast helpers (compile to a single register move on GCC, Clang, and MSVC)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
L3_FORCE_INLINE u32 f32_to_bits(f32 v) noexcept {
  u32 bits = 0;
  std::memcpy(&bits, &v, sizeof(u32));
  return bits;
}

L3_FORCE_INLINE f32 f32_from_bits(u32 bits) noexcept {
  f32 v = 0.0f;
  std::memcpy(&v, &bits, sizeof(f32));
  return v;
}

L3_FORCE_INLINE u64 f64_to_bits(f64 v) noexcept {
  u64 bits = 0;
  std::memcpy(&bits, &v, sizeof(u64));
  return bits;
}

L3_FORCE_INLINE f64 f64_from_bits(u64 bits) noexcept {
  f64 v = 0.0;
  std::memcpy(&v, &bits, sizeof(f64));
  return v;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Useful constants
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
inline constexpr f16 kF16Zero = f16::from_bits(0x0000u);
inline constexpr f16 kF16NegZero = f16::from_bits(0x8000u);
inline constexpr f16 kF16One = f16::from_bits(0x3c00u);
inline constexpr f16 kF16NegOne = f16::from_bits(0xbc00u);
inline constexpr f16 kF16Max = f16::from_bits(0x7bffu);      // 65504.0
inline constexpr f16 kF16MinNorm = f16::from_bits(0x0400u);  // 2^-14
inline constexpr f16 kF16Infinity = f16::from_bits(0x7c00u);
inline constexpr f16 kF16NegInfinity = f16::from_bits(0xfc00u);
inline constexpr f16 kF16NaN = f16::from_bits(0x7e00u);

inline constexpr bf16 kBF16Zero = bf16::from_bits(0x0000u);
inline constexpr bf16 kBF16NegZero = bf16::from_bits(0x8000u);
inline constexpr bf16 kBF16One = bf16::from_bits(0x3f80u);
inline constexpr bf16 kBF16NegOne = bf16::from_bits(0xbf80u);
inline constexpr bf16 kBF16Max = bf16::from_bits(0x7f7fu);
inline constexpr bf16 kBF16MinNorm = bf16::from_bits(0x0080u);
inline constexpr bf16 kBF16Infinity = bf16::from_bits(0x7f80u);
inline constexpr bf16 kBF16NegInfinity = bf16::from_bits(0xff80u);
inline constexpr bf16 kBF16NaN = bf16::from_bits(0x7fc0u);

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Classification predicates (no <cmath> dependency)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
L3_FORCE_INLINE bool is_nan(f32 v) noexcept {
  return (f32_to_bits(v) & 0x7fffffffu) > 0x7f800000u;
}

L3_FORCE_INLINE bool is_inf(f32 v) noexcept {
  return (f32_to_bits(v) & 0x7fffffffu) == 0x7f800000u;
}

L3_FORCE_INLINE bool is_finite(f32 v) noexcept {
  return (f32_to_bits(v) & 0x7f800000u) != 0x7f800000u;
}

constexpr bool is_nan(f16 h) noexcept {
  return (h.bits & 0x7fffu) > 0x7c00u;
}

constexpr bool is_inf(f16 h) noexcept {
  return (h.bits & 0x7fffu) == 0x7c00u;
}

constexpr bool is_finite(f16 h) noexcept {
  return (h.bits & 0x7c00u) != 0x7c00u;
}

constexpr bool is_zero(f16 h) noexcept {
  return (h.bits & 0x7fffu) == 0u;
}

constexpr bool is_nan(bf16 b) noexcept {
  return (b.bits & 0x7fffu) > 0x7f80u;
}

constexpr bool is_inf(bf16 b) noexcept {
  return (b.bits & 0x7fffu) == 0x7f80u;
}

constexpr bool is_finite(bf16 b) noexcept {
  return (b.bits & 0x7f80u) != 0x7f80u;
}

constexpr bool is_zero(bf16 b) noexcept {
  return (b.bits & 0x7fffu) == 0u;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// bf16 <-> f32 conversions
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Exact: bf16 is the top 16 bits of an IEEE 754 f32.
L3_FORCE_INLINE f32 bf16_to_f32(bf16 b) noexcept {
  return f32_from_bits(static_cast<u32>(b.bits) << 16);
}

// Round-to-nearest-even. Preserves NaNs (quieting signaling NaNs).
L3_FORCE_INLINE bf16 f32_to_bf16(f32 v) noexcept {
  u32 bits = f32_to_bits(v);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY((bits & 0x7fffffffu) > 0x7f800000u)) {
    // Force quiet NaN bit so payload truncation cannot turn a NaN into Inf.
    return bf16::from_bits(static_cast<u16>((bits >> 16) | 0x0040u));
  }
  const u32 lsb = (bits >> 16) & 1u;
  bits += 0x7fffu + lsb;
  return bf16::from_bits(static_cast<u16>(bits >> 16));
}

// Fast truncation (round toward zero); useful only when NaN is impossible.
L3_FORCE_INLINE bf16 f32_to_bf16_trunc(f32 v) noexcept {
  return bf16::from_bits(static_cast<u16>(f32_to_bits(v) >> 16));
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// f16 <-> f32 conversions (IEEE 754 binary16, round-to-nearest-even)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
inline f32 f16_to_f32(f16 h) noexcept {
  const u32 w = static_cast<u32>(h.bits);
  const u32 sign = (w & 0x8000u) << 16;
  u32 exp = (w >> 10) & 0x1fu;
  u32 mant = w & 0x03ffu;

  if (L3_LIKELY(exp != 0u && exp != 31u)) {
    // Normal number: rebias exponent from 15 to 127 (+112).
    return f32_from_bits(sign | ((exp + 112u) << 23) | (mant << 13));
  }

  if (exp == 0u) {
    if (mant == 0u) return f32_from_bits(sign);
    // Subnormal: shift mantissa until the hidden bit (0x0400) is set.
    exp = 113u;
    while ((mant & 0x0400u) == 0u) {
      mant <<= 1;
      --exp;
    }
    mant &= 0x03ffu;
    return f32_from_bits(sign | (exp << 23) | (mant << 13));
  }

  // Inf or NaN (exp == 31). Quiet any signaling NaN.
  const u32 nan_quiet = mant != 0u ? 0x00400000u : 0u;
  return f32_from_bits(sign | 0x7f800000u | (mant << 13) | nan_quiet);
}

inline f16 f32_to_f16(f32 v) noexcept {
  const u32 bits = f32_to_bits(v);
  const u32 sign = (bits >> 16) & 0x8000u;
  const u32 abs_bits = bits & 0x7fffffffu;

  // NaN or Inf in f32.
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(abs_bits >= 0x7f800000u)) {
    if (abs_bits > 0x7f800000u) {
      // NaN: keep high payload bits and set the quiet bit (0x0200).
      return f16::from_bits(
          static_cast<u16>(sign | 0x7e00u | ((abs_bits >> 13) & 0x01ffu)));
    }
    return f16::from_bits(static_cast<u16>(sign | 0x7c00u));
  }

  // Overflow: >= 65520.0 (0x477fe000) rounds up to infinity under RNE.
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(abs_bits >= 0x477fe000u)) {
    return f16::from_bits(static_cast<u16>(sign | 0x7c00u));
  }

  // Normal f16 range: >= 2^-14 (0x38800000).
  if (L3_LIKELY(abs_bits >= 0x38800000u)) {
    u32 biased = abs_bits - 0x38000000u;
    const u32 lsb = (biased >> 13) & 1u;
    biased += 0x0fffu + lsb;
    return f16::from_bits(static_cast<u16>(sign | (biased >> 13)));
  }

  // Too small even for subnormal f16 (< 2^-25): rounds to signed zero.
  if (abs_bits < 0x33000000u) {
    return f16::from_bits(static_cast<u16>(sign));
  }

  // Subnormal f16 (2^-25 <= |v| < 2^-14).
  const u32 exp = abs_bits >> 23;
  const u32 mant = (abs_bits & 0x007fffffu) | 0x00800000u;
  const u32 shift = 126u - exp;  // in [14, 24]
  const u32 lsb = (mant >> shift) & 1u;
  const u32 round_bias = (1u << (shift - 1u)) - 1u + lsb;
  const u16 sub = static_cast<u16>((mant + round_bias) >> shift);
  return f16::from_bits(static_cast<u16>(sign | sub));
}

// Direct f16 <-> bf16 goes through f32.
L3_FORCE_INLINE bf16 f16_to_bf16(f16 h) noexcept {
  return f32_to_bf16(f16_to_f32(h));
}

L3_FORCE_INLINE f16 bf16_to_f16(bf16 b) noexcept {
  return f32_to_f16(bf16_to_f32(b));
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Contiguous buffer conversions (portable baseline)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
inline void convert_f32_to_f16(const f32* L3_RESTRICT src,
                               f16* L3_RESTRICT dst, Index count) noexcept {
  L3_DCHECK_GE(count, 0);
  L3_DCHECK(count == 0 || (src != nullptr && dst != nullptr));
  for (Index i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    dst[i] = f32_to_f16(src[i]);
  }
}

inline void convert_f16_to_f32(const f16* L3_RESTRICT src,
                               f32* L3_RESTRICT dst, Index count) noexcept {
  L3_DCHECK_GE(count, 0);
  L3_DCHECK(count == 0 || (src != nullptr && dst != nullptr));
  for (Index i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    dst[i] = f16_to_f32(src[i]);
  }
}

inline void convert_f32_to_bf16(const f32* L3_RESTRICT src,
                                bf16* L3_RESTRICT dst, Index count) noexcept {
  L3_DCHECK_GE(count, 0);
  L3_DCHECK(count == 0 || (src != nullptr && dst != nullptr));
  for (Index i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    dst[i] = f32_to_bf16(src[i]);
  }
}

inline void convert_bf16_to_f32(const bf16* L3_RESTRICT src,
                                f32* L3_RESTRICT dst, Index count) noexcept {
  L3_DCHECK_GE(count, 0);
  L3_DCHECK(count == 0 || (src != nullptr && dst != nullptr));
  for (Index i = 0; i < count; ++i) {
    dst[i] = bf16_to_f32(src[i]);
  }
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/flat_map.h ==========
// log3/core/flat_map.h
//
// FlatMap<K, V, Hasher>: high-performance, open-addressed flat hash table with
// linear probing, metadata control bytes, and backward-shift deletion.
//
// Why not std::unordered_map:
//   * std::unordered_map allocates a separate heap node for EVERY inserted
//     element, destroying CPU cache locality and fragmenting the heap.
//   * FlatMap stores all control bytes and key-value slots in a single
//     contiguous allocation from log3's Allocator (or Arena!).
//   * Uses a compact 1-byte control tag per slot:
//       - 0x00        : kEmpty (slot has never been occupied)
//       - 0x80..0xFF  : Occupied (low 7 bits store the top 7 bits of the 64-bit
//                       hash as a fast fingerprint, avoiding 99% of full key
//                       comparisons during probing)
//   * Backward-shift deletion eliminates tombstones completely, so lookup
//     performance never degrades over long-running inference/training sessions
//     with frequent insertions and erasures.
//   * Move-only, no exceptions, returns Status (kOutOfMemory / kOutOfRange) on
//     every fallible operation, leaving the map unchanged on failure.
//
// Usage:
//   FlatMap<StrView, Index> tensor_index;
//   L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(tensor_index.put("model.layers.0.attn.wq.weight", 0));
//   if (const Index* idx = tensor_index.find("model.layers.0.attn.wq.weight")) {
//     ...
//   }

#pragma once

#include <new>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/memory.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/str_view.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

template <typename K, typename V, typename Hasher = Hash<K>>
class FlatMap {
  static_assert(!std::is_const_v<K> && !std::is_reference_v<K>,
                "FlatMap: K must be a non-const object type.");
  static_assert(!std::is_const_v<V> && !std::is_reference_v<V>,
                "FlatMap: V must be a non-const object type.");
  static_assert(std::is_nothrow_move_constructible_v<K> &&
                    std::is_nothrow_move_constructible_v<V>,
                "FlatMap: K and V must be nothrow move-constructible.");
  static_assert(std::is_nothrow_destructible_v<K> &&
                    std::is_nothrow_destructible_v<V>,
                "FlatMap: K and V must be nothrow destructible.");

 public:
  struct Entry {
    K key;
    V value;
  };

  // Forward iterator over occupied entries.
  template <bool IsConst>
  class IteratorImpl {
   public:
    using EntryType = std::conditional_t<IsConst, const Entry, Entry>;
    using CtrlPtr = const u8*;
    using SlotPtr = EntryType*;

    constexpr IteratorImpl() noexcept
        : ctrl_(nullptr), slots_(nullptr), index_(0), capacity_(0) {}

    constexpr IteratorImpl(CtrlPtr ctrl, SlotPtr slots, Index index,
                           Index capacity) noexcept
        : ctrl_(ctrl), slots_(slots), index_(index), capacity_(capacity) {
      advance_to_occupied();
    }

    EntryType& operator*() const noexcept {
      L3_DCHECK_LT(index_, capacity_);
      return slots_[index_];
    }

    EntryType* operator->() const noexcept {
      L3_DCHECK_LT(index_, capacity_);
      return &slots_[index_];
    }

    IteratorImpl& operator++() noexcept {
      ++index_;
      advance_to_occupied();
      return *this;
    }

    IteratorImpl operator++(int) noexcept {
      IteratorImpl tmp = *this;
      ++(*this);
      return tmp;
    }

    friend bool operator==(const IteratorImpl& a,
                           const IteratorImpl& b) noexcept {
      return a.slots_ == b.slots_ && a.index_ == b.index_;
    }

    friend bool operator!=(const IteratorImpl& a,
                           const IteratorImpl& b) noexcept {
      return !(a == b);
    }

   private:
    constexpr void advance_to_occupied() noexcept {
      while (index_ < capacity_ && (ctrl_[index_] & kOccupiedBit) == 0) {
        ++index_;
      }
    }

    CtrlPtr ctrl_;
    SlotPtr slots_;
    Index index_;
    Index capacity_;
  };

  using iterator = IteratorImpl<false>;
  using const_iterator = IteratorImpl<true>;

  // --- construction / destruction --------------------------------------------
  FlatMap() noexcept
      : ctrl_(nullptr),
        slots_(nullptr),
        size_(0),
        capacity_(0),
        allocator_(nullptr) {}

  explicit FlatMap(const Allocator& allocator) noexcept
      : ctrl_(nullptr),
        slots_(nullptr),
        size_(0),
        capacity_(0),
        allocator_(&allocator) {}

  FlatMap(FlatMap&& other) noexcept
      : ctrl_(other.ctrl_),
        slots_(other.slots_),
        size_(other.size_),
        capacity_(other.capacity_),
        allocator_(other.allocator_) {
    other.ctrl_ = nullptr;
    other.slots_ = nullptr;
    other.size_ = 0;
    other.capacity_ = 0;
  }

  FlatMap& operator=(FlatMap&& other) noexcept {
    if (this != &other) {
      clear();
      free_table();
      ctrl_ = other.ctrl_;
      slots_ = other.slots_;
      size_ = other.size_;
      capacity_ = other.capacity_;
      allocator_ = other.allocator_;

      other.ctrl_ = nullptr;
      other.slots_ = nullptr;
      other.size_ = 0;
      other.capacity_ = 0;
    }
    return *this;
  }

  FlatMap(const FlatMap&) = delete;
  FlatMap& operator=(const FlatMap&) = delete;

  ~FlatMap() {
    clear();
    free_table();
  }

  // --- observers -------------------------------------------------------------
  Index size() const noexcept { return size_; }
  Index capacity() const noexcept { return capacity_; }
  bool empty() const noexcept { return size_ == 0; }
  const Allocator& get_allocator() const noexcept { return allocator(); }

  iterator begin() noexcept { return iterator(ctrl_, slots_, 0, capacity_); }
  iterator end() noexcept {
    return iterator(ctrl_, slots_, capacity_, capacity_);
  }
  const_iterator begin() const noexcept {
    return const_iterator(ctrl_, slots_, 0, capacity_);
  }
  const_iterator end() const noexcept {
    return const_iterator(ctrl_, slots_, capacity_, capacity_);
  }

  // --- capacity management ---------------------------------------------------
  // Pre-allocate space for at least `min_entries` without rehashing.
  // Keeps load factor <= 75% (capacity >= ceil(min_entries * 4 / 3)).
  Status reserve(Index min_entries) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(min_entries, 0);
    if (min_entries <= max_load(capacity_)) return Status::ok();

    Index target_cap = kMinCapacity;
    while (max_load(target_cap) < min_entries) {
      if (target_cap > kMaxIndex / 2) {
        return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                             "FlatMap: capacity overflow");
      }
      target_cap *= 2;
    }
    return rehash(target_cap);
  }

  void clear() noexcept {
    if (size_ == 0 || ctrl_ == nullptr) return;
    for (Index i = 0; i < capacity_; ++i) {
      if ((ctrl_[i] & kOccupiedBit) != 0) {
        slots_[i].~Entry();
        ctrl_[i] = kEmptyTag;
      }
    }
    size_ = 0;
  }

  // --- lookup ----------------------------------------------------------------
  V* find(const K& key) noexcept {
    const Index idx = find_slot_index(key);
    return idx >= 0 ? &slots_[idx].value : nullptr;
  }

  const V* find(const K& key) const noexcept {
    const Index idx = find_slot_index(key);
    return idx >= 0 ? &slots_[idx].value : nullptr;
  }

  bool contains(const K& key) const noexcept {
    return find_slot_index(key) >= 0;
  }

  // --- insertion / update ----------------------------------------------------
  // Insert or overwrite `key` with `value`.
  // If `out_inserted` is non-null, sets *out_inserted = true if a new key was
  // inserted, or false if an existing key was overwritten.
  template <typename KeyArg, typename ValArg>
  Status put(KeyArg&& key, ValArg&& value,
             bool* out_inserted = nullptr) noexcept {
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(ensure_room_for_one());

    const u64 h = Hasher{}(key);
    const u8 tag = make_tag(h);
    const Index mask = capacity_ - 1;
    Index idx = static_cast<Index>(h) & mask;

    for (;;) {
      const u8 c = ctrl_[idx];
      if (c == kEmptyTag) {
        ::new (static_cast<void*>(&slots_[idx]))
            Entry{K(std::forward<KeyArg>(key)), V(std::forward<ValArg>(value))};
        ctrl_[idx] = tag;
        ++size_;
        if (out_inserted != nullptr) *out_inserted = true;
        return Status::ok();
      }
      if (c == tag && slots_[idx].key == key) {
        slots_[idx].value = std::forward<ValArg>(value);
        if (out_inserted != nullptr) *out_inserted = false;
        return Status::ok();
      }
      idx = (idx + 1) & mask;
    }
  }

  // Insert `(key, value)` ONLY if `key` is not already in the map.
  // Returns a pointer to the value in the map via `out_value` (if non-null)
  // and sets `*out_inserted` (if non-null) to true when newly inserted.
  template <typename KeyArg, typename ValArg>
  Status try_insert(KeyArg&& key, ValArg&& value, V** out_value = nullptr,
                    bool* out_inserted = nullptr) noexcept {
    L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(ensure_room_for_one());

    const u64 h = Hasher{}(key);
    const u8 tag = make_tag(h);
    const Index mask = capacity_ - 1;
    Index idx = static_cast<Index>(h) & mask;

    for (;;) {
      const u8 c = ctrl_[idx];
      if (c == kEmptyTag) {
        ::new (static_cast<void*>(&slots_[idx]))
            Entry{K(std::forward<KeyArg>(key)), V(std::forward<ValArg>(value))};
        ctrl_[idx] = tag;
        ++size_;
        if (out_value != nullptr) *out_value = &slots_[idx].value;
        if (out_inserted != nullptr) *out_inserted = true;
        return Status::ok();
      }
      if (c == tag && slots_[idx].key == key) {
        if (out_value != nullptr) *out_value = &slots_[idx].value;
        if (out_inserted != nullptr) *out_inserted = false;
        return Status::ok();
      }
      idx = (idx + 1) & mask;
    }
  }

  // --- erasure (backward-shift deletion, zero tombstones) --------------------
  // Removes `key` if present and returns true; returns false if not found.
  bool erase(const K& key) noexcept {
    const Index found = find_slot_index(key);
    if (found < 0) return false;

    const Index mask = capacity_ - 1;
    Index hole = found;
    Index scan = (hole + 1) & mask;

    // Shift subsequent entries back if their ideal home bucket is at or before
    // `hole` (cyclically modulo capacity_).
    while (ctrl_[scan] != kEmptyTag) {
      const u64 ideal_hash = Hasher{}(slots_[scan].key);
      const Index ideal = static_cast<Index>(ideal_hash) & mask;

      // Distance from `ideal` to `scan` vs distance from `ideal` to `hole`.
      const Index dist_scan = (scan - ideal) & mask;
      const Index dist_hole = (hole - ideal) & mask;

      if (dist_hole <= dist_scan) {
        slots_[hole] = std::move(slots_[scan]);
        ctrl_[hole] = ctrl_[scan];
        hole = scan;
      }
      scan = (scan + 1) & mask;
    }

    slots_[hole].~Entry();
    ctrl_[hole] = kEmptyTag;
    --size_;
    return true;
  }

 private:
  static constexpr u8 kEmptyTag = 0x00u;
  static constexpr u8 kOccupiedBit = 0x80u;
  static constexpr Index kMinCapacity = 16;
  static constexpr Size kBlockAlign =
      alignof(Entry) > kMinObjectAlignment ? alignof(Entry) : kMinObjectAlignment;

  static constexpr u8 make_tag(u64 hash) noexcept {
    return static_cast<u8>((hash >> 57) | kOccupiedBit);
  }

  // 75% max load factor: capacity * 3 / 4.
  static constexpr Index max_load(Index cap) noexcept {
    return cap - (cap >> 2);
  }

  const Allocator& allocator() const noexcept {
    return allocator_ != nullptr ? *allocator_ : default_allocator();
  }

  // Compute total byte size and slot offset for a combined (ctrl + slots) block.
  static bool compute_layout(Index cap, Size* out_slots_offset,
                             Size* out_total_bytes) noexcept {
    const Size ctrl_bytes = static_cast<Size>(cap);
    const Size slots_offset = align_up(ctrl_bytes, alignof(Entry));
    Size slots_bytes = 0;
    if (!checked_mul(static_cast<Size>(cap), sizeof(Entry), &slots_bytes)) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!checked_add(slots_offset, slots_bytes, out_total_bytes)) {
      return false;
    }
    *out_slots_offset = slots_offset;
    return true;
  }

  void free_table() noexcept {
    if (ctrl_ != nullptr) {
      Size slots_offset = 0;
      Size total_bytes = 0;
      compute_layout(capacity_, &slots_offset, &total_bytes);
      allocator().deallocate(ctrl_, total_bytes, kBlockAlign);
      ctrl_ = nullptr;
      slots_ = nullptr;
      capacity_ = 0;
    }
  }

  Status ensure_room_for_one() noexcept {
    if (L3_LIKELY(size_ + 1 <= max_load(capacity_))) {
      return Status::ok();
    }
    const Index new_cap = capacity_ == 0 ? kMinCapacity : capacity_ * 2;
    return rehash(new_cap);
  }

  Status rehash(Index new_cap) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(is_power_of_two(static_cast<Size>(new_cap)));

    Size slots_offset = 0;
    Size total_bytes = 0;
    if (!compute_layout(new_cap, &slots_offset, &total_bytes)) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kOutOfRange, "FlatMap: layout overflow");
    }

    void* raw = allocator().allocate(total_bytes, kBlockAlign);
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(raw == nullptr)) {
      return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kOutOfMemory,
                            "FlatMap: failed to allocate %zu bytes",
                            total_bytes);
    }

    auto* new_ctrl = static_cast<u8*>(raw);
    auto* new_slots =
        reinterpret_cast<Entry*>(static_cast<byte*>(raw) + slots_offset);
    mem_zero(new_ctrl, static_cast<Size>(new_cap));

    const Index mask = new_cap - 1;
    for (Index i = 0; i < capacity_; ++i) {
      if ((ctrl_[i] & kOccupiedBit) != 0) {
        const u64 h = Hasher{}(slots_[i].key);
        const u8 tag = make_tag(h);
        Index dst = static_cast<Index>(h) & mask;
        while (new_ctrl[dst] != kEmptyTag) {
          dst = (dst + 1) & mask;
        }
        ::new (static_cast<void*>(&new_slots[dst])) Entry(std::move(slots_[i]));
        new_ctrl[dst] = tag;
        slots_[i].~Entry();
      }
    }

    free_table();
    ctrl_ = new_ctrl;
    slots_ = new_slots;
    capacity_ = new_cap;
    return Status::ok();
  }

  Index find_slot_index(const K& key) const noexcept {
    if (size_ == 0) return -1;
    const u64 h = Hasher{}(key);
    const u8 tag = make_tag(h);
    const Index mask = capacity_ - 1;
    Index idx = static_cast<Index>(h) & mask;

    for (;;) {
      const u8 c = ctrl_[idx];
      if (c == kEmptyTag) return -1;
      if (c == tag && slots_[idx].key == key) return idx;
      idx = (idx + 1) & mask;
    }
  }

  u8* ctrl_;
  Entry* slots_;
  Index size_;
  Index capacity_;
  const Allocator* allocator_;
};

// Convenience alias for string-keyed maps (tensor registries, token tables).
template <typename V>
using StrMap = FlatMap<StrView, V>;

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/arena.h ==========
// log3/core/arena.h
#pragma once

#include "log3/core/memory.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

class L3_API Arena {
public:
  explicit Arena(Size chunk_size = 65536,
                 const Allocator& backing_allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  Arena(const Arena&) = delete;
  Arena& operator=(const Arena&) = delete;

  Arena(Arena&& other) noexcept;
  Arena& operator=(Arena&& other) noexcept;

  ~Arena();

  void* allocate(Size size, Size alignment = kDefaultAlignment) noexcept;
  void reset() noexcept;

  Size capacity_bytes() const noexcept { return total_capacity_bytes_; }
  Size allocated_bytes() const noexcept { return total_allocated_bytes_; }

  Allocator allocator() const noexcept {
    return Allocator{&Arena::allocate_trampoline, &Arena::deallocate_trampoline,
                     const_cast<Arena*>(this), "Arena"};
  }

private:
  struct Chunk;

  static void* allocate_trampoline(void* ctx, Size size, Size alignment) noexcept;
  static void deallocate_trampoline(void* ctx, void* ptr, Size size,
                                    Size alignment) noexcept;

  Status grow(Size min_size, Size alignment) noexcept;

  Size chunk_size_;
  Allocator backing_allocator_;
  Chunk* head_;
  Size total_capacity_bytes_;
  Size total_allocated_bytes_;
};

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/assert.h ==========
// log3/core/assert.h
//
// Invariant checking. Built on log.h (fatal log + abort) and status.h.
//
// Two tiers, deliberately simple:
//
//   L3_CHECK*   Always on, in debug AND release. For conditions whose
//               violation means a bug or an unrecoverable state (corrupt
//               graph, impossible shape after validation, ...). On failure:
//               log a FATAL message with file:line and the failed expression,
//               then abort. Cheap enough to leave on: the failure path is
//               out-of-line and cold, the hot path is one predictable branch.
//
//   L3_DCHECK*  Debug builds only (L3_DEBUG). Use inside hot kernels and
//               inner loops. In release the condition is NOT evaluated and no
//               code is generated, but it is still type-checked, so it cannot
//               silently rot.
//
// What these are NOT for: validating user input. Bad arguments coming from a
// caller (wrong shape, wrong dtype, ...) are recoverable and must be reported
// with Status / Result<T>, never with CHECK.
//
//   L3_CHECK(cond)                     L3_CHECK_MSG(cond, fmt, ...)
//   L3_CHECK_EQ/NE/LT/LE/GT/GE(a, b)   prints both values on failure
//   L3_CHECK_OK(status_expr)           prints the Status code and message
//   L3_PANIC(fmt, ...)                 unconditional fatal error
//   L3_NOT_IMPLEMENTED()               unconditional fatal "not implemented"
//   L3_DCHECK_UNREACHABLE()            debug: panic, release: optimizer hint
//
// Comparison checks (L3_CHECK_EQ etc.) accept bool, integers, enums, floating
// point, pointers and nullptr. Mixed signed/unsigned integer comparisons are
// done correctly (-1 is never equal to UINT_MAX), which std only offers from
// C++20 (std::cmp_equal); we provide it for C++17.
//
// Tip: set -ffile-prefix-map=<src_dir>=. (GCC/Clang) or /pathmap (MSVC) so
// __FILE__ does not embed your build machine's absolute paths in the binary.

#pragma once

#include <type_traits>

#include "log3/core/log.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {
namespace detail {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Value capture for failure messages
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
struct CheckValue {
  enum class Kind : u8 { kBool, kSigned, kUnsigned, kFloat, kPointer };

  Kind kind;
  union {
    i64 i;
    u64 u;
    f64 f;
    const void* p;
  };
};

template <typename T>
inline constexpr bool kAlwaysFalse = false;

template <typename T>
CheckValue make_check_value(const T& v) noexcept {
  CheckValue out{};
  if constexpr (std::is_same_v<T, bool>) {
    out.kind = CheckValue::Kind::kBool;
    out.u = v ? 1u : 0u;
  } else if constexpr (std::is_enum_v<T>) {
    return make_check_value(static_cast<std::underlying_type_t<T>>(v));
  } else if constexpr (std::is_integral_v<T>) {
    if constexpr (std::is_signed_v<T>) {
      out.kind = CheckValue::Kind::kSigned;
      out.i = static_cast<i64>(v);
    } else {
      out.kind = CheckValue::Kind::kUnsigned;
      out.u = static_cast<u64>(v);
    }
  } else if constexpr (std::is_floating_point_v<T>) {
    out.kind = CheckValue::Kind::kFloat;
    out.f = static_cast<f64>(v);
  } else if constexpr (std::is_pointer_v<T>) {
    out.kind = CheckValue::Kind::kPointer;
    out.p = static_cast<const void*>(v);
  } else if constexpr (std::is_null_pointer_v<T>) {
    out.kind = CheckValue::Kind::kPointer;
    out.p = nullptr;
  } else {
    static_assert(kAlwaysFalse<T>,
                  "L3_CHECK_<op> supports bool, integer, enum, floating point, "
                  "pointer and nullptr operands only.");
  }
  return out;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Safe comparisons (mixed signed/unsigned integers handled correctly)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
template <typename A, typename B>
inline constexpr bool kMixedSignInts =
    std::is_integral_v<A> && std::is_integral_v<B> &&
    !std::is_same_v<A, bool> && !std::is_same_v<B, bool> &&
    (std::is_signed_v<A> != std::is_signed_v<B>);

template <typename A, typename B>
constexpr bool cmp_eq(const A& a, const B& b) noexcept {
  if constexpr (kMixedSignInts<A, B>) {
    if constexpr (std::is_signed_v<A>) {
      return a >= 0 && static_cast<std::make_unsigned_t<A>>(a) == b;
    } else {
      return b >= 0 && a == static_cast<std::make_unsigned_t<B>>(b);
    }
  } else {
    return a == b;
  }
}

template <typename A, typename B>
constexpr bool cmp_ne(const A& a, const B& b) noexcept {
  return !cmp_eq(a, b);
}

template <typename A, typename B>
constexpr bool cmp_lt(const A& a, const B& b) noexcept {
  if constexpr (kMixedSignInts<A, B>) {
    if constexpr (std::is_signed_v<A>) {
      return a < 0 || static_cast<std::make_unsigned_t<A>>(a) < b;
    } else {
      return b >= 0 && a < static_cast<std::make_unsigned_t<B>>(b);
    }
  } else {
    return a < b;
  }
}

template <typename A, typename B>
constexpr bool cmp_gt(const A& a, const B& b) noexcept {
  return cmp_lt(b, a);
}

// For floating point, <= and >= must stay false when either side is NaN, so
// they are not written as !(a > b). Only the integer path uses negation.
template <typename A, typename B>
constexpr bool cmp_le(const A& a, const B& b) noexcept {
  if constexpr (kMixedSignInts<A, B>) {
    return !cmp_lt(b, a);
  } else {
    return a <= b;
  }
}

template <typename A, typename B>
constexpr bool cmp_ge(const A& a, const B& b) noexcept {
  if constexpr (kMixedSignInts<A, B>) {
    return !cmp_lt(a, b);
  } else {
    return a >= b;
  }
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Failure paths: out-of-line, cold, never return. Defined in
// src/core/assert.cpp. They log at FATAL level and call fatal_abort().
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
[[noreturn]] L3_COLD L3_API void check_failed(const char* file, int line,
                                              const char* func,
                                              const char* expr) noexcept;

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD L3_API void check_failed_msg(const char* file, int line,
                                                  const char* func,
                                                  const char* expr,
                                                  const char* fmt, ...) noexcept
    L3_PRINTF_FORMAT(5, 6);

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD L3_API void check_op_failed(const char* file, int line,
                                                 const char* func,
                                                 const char* expr,
                                                 CheckValue lhs,
                                                 CheckValue rhs) noexcept;

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD L3_API void check_status_failed(const char* file, int line,
                                                     const char* func,
                                                     const char* expr,
                                                     const Status& status) noexcept;

[[noreturn]] L3_COLD L3_API void panic_msg(const char* file, int line,
                                           const char* func,
                                           const char* fmt, ...) noexcept
    L3_PRINTF_FORMAT(4, 5);

}  // namespace detail
}  // namespace l3

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// L3_CHECK family (always on)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
#define L3_CHECK(cond)                                                      \
  do {                                                                      \
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!(cond))) {                                             \
      ::l3::detail::check_failed(__FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, #cond);      \
    }                                                                       \
  } while (0)

// fmt is required; extra arguments are printf-style.
#define L3_CHECK_MSG(cond, ...)                                             \
  do {                                                                      \
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!(cond))) {                                             \
      ::l3::detail::check_failed_msg(__FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, #cond,   \
                                     __VA_ARGS__);                          \
    }                                                                       \
  } while (0)

#define L3_CHECK_OP_IMPL(cmp_fn, expr_text, a, b)                           \
  do {                                                                      \
    const auto& L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_lhs_) = (a);                              \
    const auto& L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_rhs_) = (b);                              \
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!::l3::detail::cmp_fn(L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_lhs_),          \
                                          L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_rhs_)))) {      \
      ::l3::detail::check_op_failed(                                        \
          __FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, expr_text,                          \
          ::l3::detail::make_check_value(L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_lhs_)),          \
          ::l3::detail::make_check_value(L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_rhs_)));         \
    }                                                                       \
  } while (0)

#define L3_CHECK_EQ(a, b) L3_CHECK_OP_IMPL(cmp_eq, #a " == " #b, a, b)
#define L3_CHECK_NE(a, b) L3_CHECK_OP_IMPL(cmp_ne, #a " != " #b, a, b)
#define L3_CHECK_LT(a, b) L3_CHECK_OP_IMPL(cmp_lt, #a " < " #b, a, b)
#define L3_CHECK_LE(a, b) L3_CHECK_OP_IMPL(cmp_le, #a " <= " #b, a, b)
#define L3_CHECK_GT(a, b) L3_CHECK_OP_IMPL(cmp_gt, #a " > " #b, a, b)
#define L3_CHECK_GE(a, b) L3_CHECK_OP_IMPL(cmp_ge, #a " >= " #b, a, b)

// expr may be a Status temporary or a const Status& (e.g. result.status()).
#define L3_CHECK_OK(expr)                                                   \
  do {                                                                      \
    const ::l3::Status& L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_cs_) = (expr);                    \
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_cs_).is_ok())) {                     \
      ::l3::detail::check_status_failed(__FILE__, __LINE__, __func__,       \
                                        #expr, L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_cs_));     \
    }                                                                       \
  } while (0)

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// L3_DCHECK family (debug only). In release the branch is dead: nothing is
// evaluated, nothing is emitted, but the code is still type-checked.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
#define L3_DCHECK(cond)         do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK(cond); } } while (0)
#define L3_DCHECK_MSG(cond, ...) \
  do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK_MSG(cond, __VA_ARGS__); } } while (0)

#define L3_DCHECK_EQ(a, b) do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK_EQ(a, b); } } while (0)
#define L3_DCHECK_NE(a, b) do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK_NE(a, b); } } while (0)
#define L3_DCHECK_LT(a, b) do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK_LT(a, b); } } while (0)
#define L3_DCHECK_LE(a, b) do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK_LE(a, b); } } while (0)
#define L3_DCHECK_GT(a, b) do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK_GT(a, b); } } while (0)
#define L3_DCHECK_GE(a, b) do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK_GE(a, b); } } while (0)
#define L3_DCHECK_OK(expr) do { if (L3_DEBUG) { L3_CHECK_OK(expr); } } while (0)

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Unconditional fatal errors
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
#define L3_PANIC(...) \
  ::l3::detail::panic_msg(__FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, __VA_ARGS__)

#define L3_NOT_IMPLEMENTED() \
  ::l3::detail::panic_msg(__FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, "not implemented")

// Debug: panic if reached. Release: tell the optimizer it cannot happen.
#if L3_DEBUG
#define L3_DCHECK_UNREACHABLE() \
  ::l3::detail::panic_msg(__FILE__, __LINE__, __func__, "unreachable code reached")
#else
#define L3_DCHECK_UNREACHABLE() L3_UNREACHABLE()
#endif


========== ./include/log3/core/scope_guard.h ==========
// log3/core/scope_guard.h
//
// Zero-overhead RAII scope exit guard (`ScopeGuard` and `L3_DEFER`).
//
// Why `L3_DEFER` is essential in a Status-based codebase:
//   * Because log3 disables exceptions and propagates errors explicitly via
//     `L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(...)`, functions that acquire temporary buffers, OS
