//     handles, or locks may have 5-10 early return points.
//   * `L3_DEFER { cleanup(); };` guarantees that `cleanup()` executes on every
//     return path unless explicitly `.dismiss()`-ed on the success path.
//   * Zero heap allocation, inlined directly into the caller's stack frame.
//
// Usage:
//   void* scratch = alloc_aligned(4096);
//   L3_DEFER { free_aligned(scratch); };
//   L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(step_one(scratch));
//   L3_RETURN_IF_ERROR(step_two(scratch));

#pragma once

#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

#include "log3/core/platform.h"

namespace l3 {

template <typename F>
class ScopeGuard {
 public:
  explicit constexpr ScopeGuard(F&& fn) noexcept
      : fn_(std::forward<F>(fn)), active_(true) {}

  ScopeGuard(ScopeGuard&& other) noexcept
      : fn_(std::move(other.fn_)), active_(other.active_) {
    other.active_ = false;
  }

  ScopeGuard(const ScopeGuard&) = delete;
  ScopeGuard& operator=(const ScopeGuard&) = delete;
  ScopeGuard& operator=(ScopeGuard&&) = delete;

  ~ScopeGuard() noexcept {
    if (active_) {
      fn_();
    }
  }

  // Cancel the guard so the cleanup action is NOT run when leaving scope.
  constexpr void dismiss() noexcept { active_ = false; }

 private:
  F fn_;
  bool active_;
};

namespace detail {

struct DeferHelper {
  template <typename F>
  constexpr ScopeGuard<std::decay_t<F>> operator+(F&& fn) const noexcept {
    return ScopeGuard<std::decay_t<F>>(std::forward<F>(fn));
  }
};

}  // namespace detail

}  // namespace l3

// Execute a block unconditionally when the enclosing scope exits:
//   L3_DEFER { close_handle(h); };
#define L3_DEFER                                                        \
  auto L3_UNIQUE_NAME(l3_defer_guard_) = ::l3::detail::DeferHelper{} + \
                                         [&]() noexcept


========== ./include/log3/core/timer.h ==========
// log3/core/timer.h
//
// High-resolution monotonic clock, Stopwatch, and bandwidth/FLOP/s calculators
// for profiling BitNet kernels, model loading, and token/s generation speed.
//
// Why not <chrono>:
//   * Avoids pulling in <chrono> and <ratio> template machinery across every
//     translation unit.
//   * Uses direct OS monotonic hardware counters (`CLOCK_MONOTONIC` on
//     Linux/Android/macOS, `QueryPerformanceCounter` on Windows) that never
//     jump backward on NTP clock adjustments.
//   * Built-in helpers for effective memory bandwidth (GB/s), compute
//     throughput (GOPS / GFLOPS), and tokens/sec.
//
// Compatibility:
//   * C++17, header-only, no exceptions, no RTTI.

#pragma once

#include <ctime>

#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
#include <windows.h>
#endif

namespace l3 {

// Monotonic timestamp in nanoseconds (for measuring elapsed intervals).
inline u64 monotonic_now_ns() noexcept {
#if L3_OS_WINDOWS
  static const LARGE_INTEGER s_freq = []() {
    LARGE_INTEGER f{};
    ::QueryPerformanceFrequency(&f);
    return f;
  }();
  LARGE_INTEGER counter{};
  ::QueryPerformanceCounter(&counter);
  if (s_freq.QuadPart <= 0) return 0;
  // Avoid u64 overflow by splitting quotient and remainder.
  const u64 whole_secs =
      static_cast<u64>(counter.QuadPart) / static_cast<u64>(s_freq.QuadPart);
  const u64 rem_ticks =
      static_cast<u64>(counter.QuadPart) % static_cast<u64>(s_freq.QuadPart);
  return whole_secs * 1000000000ULL +
         (rem_ticks * 1000000000ULL) / static_cast<u64>(s_freq.QuadPart);
#else
  struct timespec ts {};
  ::clock_gettime(CLOCK_MONOTONIC, &ts);
  return static_cast<u64>(ts.tv_sec) * 1000000000ULL +
         static_cast<u64>(ts.tv_nsec);
#endif
}

// Lightweight stopwatch for kernel and layer benchmarking.
class Stopwatch {
 public:
  Stopwatch() noexcept : start_ns_(monotonic_now_ns()) {}

  void reset() noexcept { start_ns_ = monotonic_now_ns(); }

  u64 elapsed_ns() const noexcept {
    const u64 now = monotonic_now_ns();
    return now >= start_ns_ ? (now - start_ns_) : 0ULL;
  }

  f64 elapsed_us() const noexcept {
    return static_cast<f64>(elapsed_ns()) * 1e-3;
  }

  f64 elapsed_ms() const noexcept {
    return static_cast<f64>(elapsed_ns()) * 1e-6;
  }

  f64 elapsed_seconds() const noexcept {
    return static_cast<f64>(elapsed_ns()) * 1e-9;
  }

 private:
  u64 start_ns_;
};

// Compute effective memory bandwidth in Gigabytes/sec (10^9 B/s) given bytes
// transferred and elapsed nanoseconds.
constexpr f64 compute_gbps(Size bytes_transferred, u64 elapsed_ns) noexcept {
  return elapsed_ns > 0
             ? static_cast<f64>(bytes_transferred) / static_cast<f64>(elapsed_ns)
             : 0.0;
}

// Compute effective Giga-Operations/sec (GOPS / GFLOPS, 10^9 ops/s) given
// operation count and elapsed nanoseconds.
constexpr f64 compute_gops(u64 total_ops, u64 elapsed_ns) noexcept {
  return elapsed_ns > 0
             ? static_cast<f64>(total_ops) / static_cast<f64>(elapsed_ns)
             : 0.0;
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/ptr.h ==========
// log3/core/ptr.h
//
// Intrusive reference counting: RefCounted (base class) + Ptr<T> (smart
// pointer). Tensor Storage and autograd graph Nodes are owned through Ptr<T>.
//
// Why not std::shared_ptr:
//   * One allocation, no separate control block. The count lives inside the
//     object, next to its data (better cache behavior).
//   * Ptr<T> is exactly one pointer wide. shared_ptr is two.
//   * You can make a Ptr from a raw pointer (e.g. `Ptr<Node>(this)`) at any
//     time, because the count is in the object. No enable_shared_from_this.
//   * Allocation goes through log3's Allocator (arena / pool / tracked), and
//     frees with the exact size via sized deallocation.
//   * No weak-count bookkeeping, no RTTI, no exceptions, no virtual dtor.
//
// How destruction works without a virtual destructor: make_ptr<T> knows the
// exact type T, so it records a tiny function "destroy this T" and the
// allocator in the object header. When the last Ptr goes away, that function
// runs ~T() and frees sizeof(T) bytes. So Ptr<Base> pointing at a Derived
// object destroys the Derived correctly and frees the right number of bytes,
// even if Base has no virtual destructor.
//
// Object header cost: 3 pointers (24 bytes on 64-bit): count + destroy
// function + allocator.
//
// Thread safety: the count is atomic. Copying/destroying Ptrs to the same
// object from several threads is safe. The pointed-to object itself is not
// synchronized.
//
// Rules for classes managed by Ptr:
//   * derive publicly from RefCounted
//   * create them only with make_ptr<T>() / make_ptr_in<T>()
//     (never on the stack, never with new/delete)
//   * the Allocator passed to make_ptr_in must outlive every object created
//     from it (the object stores a pointer to it). default_allocator() always
//     does.
//   * reference cycles leak (A holds Ptr<B>, B holds Ptr<A>). Break cycles in
//     the design; a weak pointer can be added later if needed.

#pragma once

#include <atomic>
#include <type_traits>
#include <utility>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/memory.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

class RefCounted;

namespace detail {
struct RefCountedAccess;
}  // namespace detail

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// RefCounted
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class RefCounted {
 public:
  using DestroyFn = void (*)(RefCounted* self) noexcept;

  RefCounted(const RefCounted&) = delete;
  RefCounted& operator=(const RefCounted&) = delete;

  // Current number of Ptrs. Informational under concurrency.
  u32 use_count() const noexcept {
    return count_.load(std::memory_order_relaxed);
  }

  // True if exactly one Ptr owns this object (e.g. safe to mutate in place /
  // copy-on-write). The acquire load pairs with other threads' releases.
  bool is_unique() const noexcept {
    return count_.load(std::memory_order_acquire) == 1;
  }

  void add_ref() const noexcept {
    count_.fetch_add(1, std::memory_order_relaxed);
  }

  // Drops one reference; destroys the object when it was the last one.
  void release() const noexcept {
#if L3_TSAN
    const u32 old = count_.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_acq_rel);
    L3_DCHECK(old > 0);
    if (old == 1) destroy_fn_(const_cast<RefCounted*>(this));
#else
    // Release on the decrement, acquire fence only on the final one: cheapest
    // correct pattern (the same one libstdc++/libc++ shared_ptr use).
    const u32 old = count_.fetch_sub(1, std::memory_order_release);
    L3_DCHECK(old > 0);
    if (old == 1) {
      std::atomic_thread_fence(std::memory_order_acquire);
      destroy_fn_(const_cast<RefCounted*>(this));
    }
#endif
  }

 protected:
  RefCounted() noexcept : count_(0), destroy_fn_(nullptr), allocator_(nullptr) {}
  ~RefCounted() = default;  // non-virtual and protected: no delete via base

 private:
  friend struct detail::RefCountedAccess;

  mutable std::atomic<u32> count_;
  DestroyFn destroy_fn_;
  const Allocator* allocator_;
};

static_assert(std::atomic<u32>::is_always_lock_free,
              "log3: atomic<u32> must be lock-free.");
static_assert(sizeof(RefCounted) == 3 * sizeof(void*),
              "log3: RefCounted header must stay 3 pointers wide.");

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Ptr<T>
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
template <typename T>
class Ptr {
 public:
  using element_type = T;

  constexpr Ptr() noexcept : ptr_(nullptr) {}
  constexpr Ptr(std::nullptr_t) noexcept : ptr_(nullptr) {}

  // Share an existing object: increments its count. This is how you get a Ptr
  // back from a raw pointer or from `this`.
  explicit Ptr(T* raw) noexcept : ptr_(raw) {
    static_assert(std::is_convertible_v<T*, const RefCounted*>,
                  "Ptr<T>: T must derive publicly from RefCounted.");
    if (ptr_ != nullptr) ptr_->add_ref();
  }

  Ptr(const Ptr& other) noexcept : ptr_(other.ptr_) {
    if (ptr_ != nullptr) ptr_->add_ref();
  }

  Ptr(Ptr&& other) noexcept : ptr_(other.ptr_) { other.ptr_ = nullptr; }

  // Ptr<Derived> -> Ptr<Base>
  template <typename U,
            typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_convertible_v<U*, T*>>>
  Ptr(const Ptr<U>& other) noexcept : ptr_(other.get()) {
    if (ptr_ != nullptr) ptr_->add_ref();
  }

  template <typename U,
            typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_convertible_v<U*, T*>>>
  Ptr(Ptr<U>&& other) noexcept : ptr_(other.detach()) {}

  ~Ptr() {
    static_assert(std::is_convertible_v<T*, const RefCounted*>,
                  "Ptr<T>: T must derive publicly from RefCounted.");
    if (ptr_ != nullptr) ptr_->release();
  }

  Ptr& operator=(const Ptr& other) noexcept {
    Ptr(other).swap(*this);
    return *this;
  }

  Ptr& operator=(Ptr&& other) noexcept {
    Ptr(std::move(other)).swap(*this);
    return *this;
  }

  Ptr& operator=(std::nullptr_t) noexcept {
    reset();
    return *this;
  }

  // Take ownership of a pointer whose reference was already counted (does NOT
  // increment). The counterpart of detach().
  static Ptr adopt(T* raw) noexcept {
    Ptr p;
    p.ptr_ = raw;
    return p;
  }

  // Give up ownership WITHOUT decrementing; the caller now owns one reference.
  T* detach() noexcept {
    T* p = ptr_;
    ptr_ = nullptr;
    return p;
  }

  void reset() noexcept { Ptr().swap(*this); }

  void swap(Ptr& other) noexcept {
    T* tmp = ptr_;
    ptr_ = other.ptr_;
    other.ptr_ = tmp;
  }

  T* get() const noexcept { return ptr_; }

  T& operator*() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(ptr_ != nullptr);
    return *ptr_;
  }

  T* operator->() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(ptr_ != nullptr);
    return ptr_;
  }

  explicit operator bool() const noexcept { return ptr_ != nullptr; }

  u32 use_count() const noexcept { return ptr_ != nullptr ? ptr_->use_count() : 0; }

 private:
  T* ptr_;
};

static_assert(sizeof(Ptr<RefCounted>) == sizeof(void*),
              "log3: Ptr<T> must be exactly one pointer wide.");

// --- comparisons ---------------------------------------------------------------
template <typename A, typename B>
bool operator==(const Ptr<A>& a, const Ptr<B>& b) noexcept {
  return a.get() == b.get();
}
template <typename A, typename B>
bool operator!=(const Ptr<A>& a, const Ptr<B>& b) noexcept {
  return a.get() != b.get();
}
template <typename A>
bool operator==(const Ptr<A>& a, std::nullptr_t) noexcept {
  return a.get() == nullptr;
}
template <typename A>
bool operator!=(const Ptr<A>& a, std::nullptr_t) noexcept {
  return a.get() != nullptr;
}
template <typename A>
bool operator==(std::nullptr_t, const Ptr<A>& a) noexcept {
  return a.get() == nullptr;
}
template <typename A>
bool operator!=(std::nullptr_t, const Ptr<A>& a) noexcept {
  return a.get() != nullptr;
}

// Downcast (Ptr<Base> -> Ptr<Derived>). No RTTI: the caller guarantees the
// dynamic type. Increments the count.
template <typename T, typename U>
Ptr<T> static_ptr_cast(const Ptr<U>& p) noexcept {
  return Ptr<T>(static_cast<T*>(p.get()));
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Creation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
namespace detail {

struct RefCountedAccess {
  static void bind(RefCounted* self, RefCounted::DestroyFn fn,
                   const Allocator* allocator) noexcept {
    self->destroy_fn_ = fn;
    self->allocator_ = allocator;
  }

  template <typename T>
  static void destroy_impl(RefCounted* self) noexcept {
    constexpr Size kAlign =
        alignof(T) > kMinObjectAlignment ? alignof(T) : kMinObjectAlignment;
    T* obj = static_cast<T*>(self);
    const Allocator* allocator = self->allocator_;  // read before ~T()
    obj->~T();
    allocator->deallocate(obj, sizeof(T), kAlign);
  }
};

}  // namespace detail

// Allocate and construct a T from `allocator`, wrapped in a Ptr (count = 1).
// On allocation failure returns a kOutOfMemory Status.
// T's constructor must not throw (the core is built without exceptions).
template <typename T, typename... Args>
Result<Ptr<T>> make_ptr_in(const Allocator& allocator, Args&&... args) noexcept {
  static_assert(std::is_convertible_v<T*, RefCounted*>,
                "make_ptr: T must derive publicly from RefCounted.");
  static_assert(!std::is_abstract_v<T>, "make_ptr: T must not be abstract.");
  constexpr Size kAlign =
      alignof(T) > kMinObjectAlignment ? alignof(T) : kMinObjectAlignment;

  void* mem = allocator.allocate(sizeof(T), kAlign);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(mem == nullptr)) {
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kOutOfMemory,
                          "make_ptr: failed to allocate %zu bytes (allocator: %s)",
                          sizeof(T),
                          allocator.name != nullptr ? allocator.name : "?");
  }
  T* obj = ::new (mem) T(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
  detail::RefCountedAccess::bind(obj, &detail::RefCountedAccess::destroy_impl<T>,
                                 &allocator);
  return Ptr<T>(obj);
}

// Same, using default_allocator().
template <typename T, typename... Args>
Result<Ptr<T>> make_ptr(Args&&... args) noexcept {
  return make_ptr_in<T>(default_allocator(), std::forward<Args>(args)...);
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/core/dtype.h ==========
// log3/core/dtype.h
//
// Data type descriptors, compile-time type traits, sub-byte packing helpers,
// and overflow-checked byte-size math for scalar and quantized tensors.
//
// Unified block model:
//   Every DType is described by (block_elements, block_bytes):
//     * Scalar types (f32, bf16, i8, ...) have block_elements == 1 and
//       block_bytes == sizeof(T).
//     * Sub-byte packed types have block_elements > 1 and block_bytes == 1:
//         - kTernary2 ("t2", BitNet b1.58 {-1, 0, +1}): 4 elements per 1 byte
//         - kInt4     ("i4", signed 4-bit [-8, 7])    : 2 elements per 1 byte
//         - kUInt4    ("u4", unsigned 4-bit [0, 15])  : 2 elements per 1 byte
//
//   Byte layout of kTernary2 ("t2") within one byte (4 weights w0..w3):
//     +---------+---------+---------+---------+
//     | bits 7:6| bits 5:4| bits 3:2| bits 1:0|
//     |   w3    |   w2    |   w1    |   w0    |
//     +---------+---------+---------+---------+
//     Encoding is biased by +1 (code = w + 1):
//       0b00 (0) -> -1
//       0b01 (1) ->  0
//       0b10 (2) -> +1
//       0b11 (3) -> reserved
//     Unpacking is a branchless mask-and-subtract: w = (code & 0x3) - 1.
//
//   Byte layout of kInt4 / kUInt4 within one byte (2 weights w0, w1):
//     +-------------------+-------------------+
//     |     bits 7:4      |     bits 3:0      |
//     |        w1         |        w0         |
//     +-------------------+-------------------+
//     kUInt4 stores [0, 15] directly; kInt4 uses biased +8 encoding
//     (code = w + 8, so w = (code & 0xF) - 8) for branchless SIMD unpacking.

#pragma once

#include <cstring>
#include <type_traits>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/memory.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DType enumeration
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
enum class DType : u8 {
  kBool = 0,
  kInt8,
  kUInt8,
  kInt16,
  kUInt16,
  kInt32,
  kUInt32,
  kInt64,
  kUInt64,
  kFloat16,
  kBFloat16,
  kFloat32,
  kFloat64,
  // Sub-byte packed quantization types:
  kTernary2,  // 2-bit BitNet b1.58 ternary {-1, 0, +1}, 4 elements per byte
  kInt4,      // 4-bit signed integer [-8, 7], 2 elements per byte
  kUInt4,     // 4-bit unsigned integer [0, 15], 2 elements per byte
};

inline constexpr usize kNumDTypes = static_cast<usize>(DType::kUInt4) + 1;

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// DTypeInfo metadata table (constexpr, zero runtime cost)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
struct DTypeInfo {
  DType dtype;
  const char* name;     // short canonical name ("f32", "bf16", "t2", ...)
  u8 bits_per_element;  // 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, or 64
  u8 block_elements;    // elements per indivisible storage block (1, 2, or 4)
  u8 block_bytes;       // bytes per indivisible storage block (1, 2, 4, or 8)
  bool is_float;        // f16, bf16, f32, f64
  bool is_integer;      // i8..i64, u8..u64
  bool is_signed;       // signed integers, floats, t2, i4
  bool is_subbyte;      // t2, i4, u4 (block_elements > 1)
};

namespace detail {

inline constexpr DTypeInfo kDTypeTable[kNumDTypes] = {
    // dtype             name    bits  blk_el blk_B  flt    int    sgn    sub
    {DType::kBool,      "bool",   8,    1,     1,   false, false, false, false},
    {DType::kInt8,      "i8",     8,    1,     1,   false, true,  true,  false},
    {DType::kUInt8,     "u8",     8,    1,     1,   false, true,  false, false},
    {DType::kInt16,     "i16",   16,    1,     2,   false, true,  true,  false},
    {DType::kUInt16,    "u16",   16,    1,     2,   false, true,  false, false},
    {DType::kInt32,     "i32",   32,    1,     4,   false, true,  true,  false},
    {DType::kUInt32,    "u32",   32,    1,     4,   false, true,  false, false},
    {DType::kInt64,     "i64",   64,    1,     8,   false, true,  true,  false},
    {DType::kUInt64,    "u64",   64,    1,     8,   false, true,  false, false},
    {DType::kFloat16,   "f16",   16,    1,     2,   true,  false, true,  false},
    {DType::kBFloat16,  "bf16",  16,    1,     2,   true,  false, true,  false},
    {DType::kFloat32,   "f32",   32,    1,     4,   true,  false, true,  false},
    {DType::kFloat64,   "f64",   64,    1,     8,   true,  false, true,  false},
    {DType::kTernary2,  "t2",     2,    4,     1,   false, false, true,  true },
    {DType::kInt4,      "i4",     4,    2,     1,   false, false, true,  true },
    {DType::kUInt4,     "u4",     4,    2,     1,   false, false, false, true },
};

}  // namespace detail

constexpr bool is_valid_dtype(DType dt) noexcept {
  return static_cast<usize>(dt) < kNumDTypes;
}

constexpr const DTypeInfo& dtype_info(DType dt) noexcept {
  L3_DCHECK(is_valid_dtype(dt));
  return detail::kDTypeTable[static_cast<usize>(dt)];
}

constexpr const char* dtype_name(DType dt) noexcept {
  return is_valid_dtype(dt) ? detail::kDTypeTable[static_cast<usize>(dt)].name
                            : "unknown";
}

constexpr u8 dtype_bits(DType dt) noexcept {
  return dtype_info(dt).bits_per_element;
}

constexpr Index dtype_block_elements(DType dt) noexcept {
  return static_cast<Index>(dtype_info(dt).block_elements);
}

constexpr Size dtype_block_bytes(DType dt) noexcept {
  return static_cast<Size>(dtype_info(dt).block_bytes);
}

// Element size in bytes for non-sub-byte types. Asserts in debug builds if
// called on a sub-byte type (use dtype_block_bytes / checked_dtype_bytes).
constexpr Size dtype_element_size(DType dt) noexcept {
  L3_DCHECK(!dtype_info(dt).is_subbyte);
  return static_cast<Size>(dtype_info(dt).block_bytes);
}

constexpr bool is_float_dtype(DType dt) noexcept {
  return dtype_info(dt).is_float;
}

constexpr bool is_integer_dtype(DType dt) noexcept {
  return dtype_info(dt).is_integer;
}

constexpr bool is_signed_dtype(DType dt) noexcept {
  return dtype_info(dt).is_signed;
}

constexpr bool is_subbyte_dtype(DType dt) noexcept {
  return dtype_info(dt).is_subbyte;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Compile-time mapping from C++ scalar types to DType
//   Sub-byte types (t2, i4, u4) have no 1-to-1 C++ scalar type, so DTypeOf<T>
//   is intentionally defined only for full-byte scalar types.
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
template <typename T>
struct DTypeTraits {
  static constexpr bool kSupported = false;
};

#define L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(cpp_type, enum_val)        \
  template <>                                            \
  struct DTypeTraits<cpp_type> {                         \
    static constexpr bool kSupported = true;             \
    static constexpr DType kValue = DType::enum_val;     \
  }

L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(bool, kBool);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(i8, kInt8);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(u8, kUInt8);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(i16, kInt16);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(u16, kUInt16);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(i32, kInt32);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(u32, kUInt32);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(i64, kInt64);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(u64, kUInt64);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(f16, kFloat16);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(bf16, kBFloat16);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(f32, kFloat32);
L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT(f64, kFloat64);

#undef L3_DEFINE_DTYPE_TRAIT

template <typename T>
constexpr DType dtype_of() noexcept {
  using CleanT = std::remove_cv_t<T>;
  static_assert(DTypeTraits<CleanT>::kSupported,
                "dtype_of<T>: T is not a registered log3 scalar DType.");
  return DTypeTraits<CleanT>::kValue;
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Overflow-checked storage size calculation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Computes the exact byte count for `numel` elements of type `dt`.
// Fails with:
//   * kInvalidArgument if `numel < 0` or `numel` is not divisible by
//     `dtype_block_elements(dt)` (e.g. 5 elements of kTernary2).
//   * kOutOfRange if the byte count overflows `Size`.
inline Status checked_dtype_bytes(DType dt, Index numel, Size* out_bytes) noexcept {
  L3_DCHECK(out_bytes != nullptr);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(!is_valid_dtype(dt))) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument, "invalid DType");
  }
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(numel < 0)) {
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                          "negative element count: %lld",
                          static_cast<long long>(numel));
  }
  const DTypeInfo& info = dtype_info(dt);
  const Index blk_elems = static_cast<Index>(info.block_elements);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(numel % blk_elems != 0)) {
    return Status::errorf(
        StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
        "element count (%lld) for DType %s must be a multiple of %lld",
        static_cast<long long>(numel), info.name,
        static_cast<long long>(blk_elems));
  }
  const Size blocks = static_cast<Size>(numel / blk_elems);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(!checked_mul(blocks, static_cast<Size>(info.block_bytes),
                               out_bytes))) {
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                          "byte size overflow for %lld elements of %s",
                          static_cast<long long>(numel), info.name);
  }
  return Status::ok();
}

// Same as checked_dtype_bytes, but rounds `numel` UP to the nearest whole
// storage block for sub-byte types (useful when packing tail elements).
inline Status checked_dtype_bytes_padded(DType dt, Index numel,
                                         Size* out_bytes) noexcept {
  L3_DCHECK(out_bytes != nullptr);
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(!is_valid_dtype(dt))) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument, "invalid DType");
  }
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(numel < 0)) {
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                          "negative element count: %lld",
                          static_cast<long long>(numel));
  }
  const DTypeInfo& info = dtype_info(dt);
  const Size blocks =
      div_ceil(static_cast<Size>(numel), static_cast<Size>(info.block_elements));
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(!checked_mul(blocks, static_cast<Size>(info.block_bytes),
                               out_bytes))) {
    return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
                          "byte size overflow for %lld elements of %s",
                          static_cast<long long>(numel), info.name);
  }
  return Status::ok();
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Name parsing (accepts canonical short names and common aliases)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
inline Result<DType> dtype_from_name(const char* name) noexcept {
  if (L3_UNLIKELY(name == nullptr)) {
    return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument, "dtype name is null");
  }
  for (usize i = 0; i < kNumDTypes; ++i) {
    if (std::strcmp(name, detail::kDTypeTable[i].name) == 0) {
      return static_cast<DType>(detail::kDTypeTable[i].dtype);
    }
  }
  // Common full-name aliases (for Python bindings / model config files).
  if (std::strcmp(name, "float32") == 0 || std::strcmp(name, "float") == 0) {
    return DType::kFloat32;
  }
  if (std::strcmp(name, "float16") == 0 || std::strcmp(name, "half") == 0) {
    return DType::kFloat16;
  }
  if (std::strcmp(name, "bfloat16") == 0) return DType::kBFloat16;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "float64") == 0 || std::strcmp(name, "double") == 0) {
    return DType::kFloat64;
  }
  if (std::strcmp(name, "int8") == 0) return DType::kInt8;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "uint8") == 0) return DType::kUInt8;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "int16") == 0) return DType::kInt16;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "uint16") == 0) return DType::kUInt16;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "int32") == 0) return DType::kInt32;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "uint32") == 0) return DType::kUInt32;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "int64") == 0) return DType::kInt64;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "uint64") == 0) return DType::kUInt64;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "ternary2") == 0 || std::strcmp(name, "i2_s") == 0 ||
      std::strcmp(name, "bitnet158") == 0) {
    return DType::kTernary2;
  }
  if (std::strcmp(name, "int4") == 0) return DType::kInt4;
  if (std::strcmp(name, "uint4") == 0) return DType::kUInt4;

  return Status::errorf(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                        "unknown DType name: '%s'", name);
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// BitNet kTernary2 ("t2") and 4-bit ("i4" / "u4") scalar pack/unpack helpers
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Clamp an integer to {-1, 0, +1} and encode as a 2-bit biased value {0, 1, 2}.
constexpr u8 encode_t2(i8 v) noexcept {
  const i8 clamped = v < -1 ? -1 : (v > 1 ? 1 : v);
  return static_cast<u8>(clamped + 1);
}

// Decode a 2-bit biased code {0, 1, 2} back to {-1, 0, +1}.
constexpr i8 decode_t2(u8 code) noexcept {
  return static_cast<i8>(static_cast<int>(code & 0x03u) - 1);
}

// Pack 4 ternary values {-1, 0, +1} into a single byte (w0 in bits 1:0,
// w1 in bits 3:2, w2 in bits 5:4, w3 in bits 7:6).
constexpr u8 pack_t2_x4(i8 w0, i8 w1, i8 w2, i8 w3) noexcept {
  return static_cast<u8>(encode_t2(w0) |
                         (encode_t2(w1) << 2) |
                         (encode_t2(w2) << 4) |
                         (encode_t2(w3) << 6));
}

// Unpack a single kTernary2 byte into 4 signed i8 values in {-1, 0, +1}.
inline void unpack_t2_x4(u8 packed, i8 out[4]) noexcept {
  out[0] = decode_t2(packed);
  out[1] = decode_t2(static_cast<u8>(packed >> 2));
  out[2] = decode_t2(static_cast<u8>(packed >> 4));
  out[3] = decode_t2(static_cast<u8>(packed >> 6));
}

// Pack 2 signed 4-bit integers in [-8, 7] into one byte (biased by +8).
constexpr u8 pack_i4_x2(i8 w0, i8 w1) noexcept {
  const i8 c0 = w0 < -8 ? -8 : (w0 > 7 ? 7 : w0);
  const i8 c1 = w1 < -8 ? -8 : (w1 > 7 ? 7 : w1);
  const u8 b0 = static_cast<u8>(c0 + 8) & 0x0fu;
  const u8 b1 = static_cast<u8>(c1 + 8) & 0x0fu;
  return static_cast<u8>(b0 | (b1 << 4));
}

inline void unpack_i4_x2(u8 packed, i8 out[2]) noexcept {
  out[0] = static_cast<i8>(static_cast<int>(packed & 0x0fu) - 8);
  out[1] = static_cast<i8>(static_cast<int>((packed >> 4) & 0x0fu) - 8);
}

// Pack 2 unsigned 4-bit integers in [0, 15] into one byte.
constexpr u8 pack_u4_x2(u8 w0, u8 w1) noexcept {
  const u8 b0 = (w0 > 15u ? 15u : w0) & 0x0fu;
  const u8 b1 = (w1 > 15u ? 15u : w1) & 0x0fu;
  return static_cast<u8>(b0 | (b1 << 4));
}

inline void unpack_u4_x2(u8 packed, u8 out[2]) noexcept {
  out[0] = packed & 0x0fu;
  out[1] = (packed >> 4) & 0x0fu;
}

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/tensor/shape.h ==========
// log3/tensor/shape.h
//
// Shape and Strides descriptors for N-dimensional tensors (up to kMaxTensorDims
// = 8 dimensions, stored completely inline with zero heap allocation).
//
// Why fixed inline storage (`kMaxTensorDims = 8`) instead of std::vector:
//   * Every modern deep learning workload (LLMs, BitNet, ViTs, CNNs, GNNs) uses
//     1D to 6D tensors. Capping tensor rank at 8 lets Shape and Strides live in
//     72 bytes on the stack/inside Tensor: TriviallyCopyable, passed by value,
//     zero heap allocations, zero destructor overhead, and 100% cache-friendly.
//   * Strides are expressed in ELEMENTS (not bytes) so sub-byte DTypes
//     (kTernary2 = 4 elems/byte, kInt4 = 2 elems/byte) and full-byte DTypes
//     share the exact same indexing math! For sub-byte tensors, the innermost
//     dimension (`dims.back()`) must be a multiple of `dtype_block_elements(dt)`
//     and contiguous (`strides.back() == 1`).
//
// Compatibility:
//   * C++17, no exceptions, no RTTI, zero heap allocations.

#pragma once

#include <cstring>
#include <initializer_list>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/dtype.h"
#include "log3/core/memory.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/span.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

// Maximum supported tensor rank (dimensions).
inline constexpr Index kMaxTensorDims = 8;

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Shape
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API Shape {
 public:
  // Default constructor creates an uninitialized / empty Shape (`rank() == 0`
  // and `numel() == 0`, so `empty() == true`). Use `Shape::scalar()` for a 0-D
  // scalar tensor with 1 element, or `Shape::from_dims({n})` for a 1-D vector.
  constexpr Shape() noexcept : dims_{0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, rank_(0) {}

  // Checked creation from a Span of dimensions.
  // Fails with:
  //   * kInvalidArgument if rank < 0, rank > kMaxTensorDims, or any dim < 0.
  //   * kOutOfRange if the product of dimensions or any sub-product overflows
  //     Index (guarantees `Strides::contiguous_for` can never overflow).
  static Result<Shape> from_dims(Span<const Index> dims) noexcept;

  static Result<Shape> from_dims(std::initializer_list<Index> dims) noexcept {
    return from_dims(
        Span<const Index>(dims.begin(), static_cast<Index>(dims.size())));
  }

  // Create a 0-D scalar shape (rank = 0, numel = 1).
  // Distinguished internally by setting dims_[0] = 1 while rank_ = 0.
  static constexpr Shape scalar() noexcept {
    Shape s;
    s.dims_[0] = 1;
    s.rank_ = 0;
    return s;
  }

  // --- observers -------------------------------------------------------------
  constexpr Index rank() const noexcept { return rank_; }
  constexpr Index ndim() const noexcept { return rank_; }
  constexpr bool is_scalar() const noexcept {
    return rank_ == 0 && dims_[0] == 1;
  }
  constexpr bool empty() const noexcept {
    return rank_ == 0 && dims_[0] == 0;
  }

  constexpr const Index* data() const noexcept { return dims_; }
  constexpr Span<const Index> dims() const noexcept {
    return Span<const Index>(dims_, rank_);
  }

  constexpr Index dim(Index axis) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(axis, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LT(axis, rank_);
    return dims_[axis];
  }

  constexpr Index operator[](Index axis) const noexcept { return dim(axis); }

  // Total number of elements across all dimensions.
  constexpr Index numel() const noexcept {
    if (rank_ == 0) return dims_[0];  // 0 for default empty, 1 for scalar()
    Index prod = 1;
    for (Index i = 0; i < rank_; ++i) {
      prod *= dims_[i];
    }
    return prod;
  }

  // Normalize a possibly-negative axis in [-rank, rank - 1] into [0, rank - 1].
  Result<Index> normalize_axis(Index axis) const noexcept {
    const Index actual = axis < 0 ? (axis + rank_) : axis;
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(actual < 0 || actual >= rank_)) {
      return Status::errorf(
          StatusCode::kOutOfRange,
          "Shape::normalize_axis: axis %lld out of range for rank %lld",
          static_cast<long long>(axis), static_cast<long long>(rank_));
    }
    return actual;
  }

  // Format human-readable shape string into `buf` (e.g., "(2, 512, 768)").
  // Returns the actual number of characters written into `buf` (excluding the
  // null terminator, never exceeding `buf_size - 1`).
  usize to_string(char* buf, usize buf_size) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index dims_[kMaxTensorDims];
  Index rank_;
};

constexpr bool operator==(const Shape& a, const Shape& b) noexcept {
  if (a.rank() != b.rank()) return false;
  if (a.rank() == 0) return a.numel() == b.numel();
  for (Index i = 0; i < a.rank(); ++i) {
    if (a[i] != b[i]) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

constexpr bool operator!=(const Shape& a, const Shape& b) noexcept {
  return !(a == b);
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Strides (expressed in elements, signed to support negative/zero strides)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API Strides {
 public:
  constexpr Strides() noexcept : strides_{0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, rank_(0) {}

  // Compute standard row-major (C-contiguous) strides for `shape`.
  // Convention: stride[i] = product of shape[i+1 .. rank-1] (with 0-sized
  // dimensions treated as 1 for stride propagation so outer axes stay
  // monotonically ordered even on 0-element slices).
  static Strides contiguous_for(const Shape& shape) noexcept;

  static Result<Strides> from_strides(Span<const Index> strides) noexcept;

  static Result<Strides> from_strides(
      std::initializer_list<Index> strides) noexcept {
    return from_strides(
        Span<const Index>(strides.begin(), static_cast<Index>(strides.size())));
  }

  constexpr Index rank() const noexcept { return rank_; }
  constexpr const Index* data() const noexcept { return strides_; }
  constexpr Span<const Index> values() const noexcept {
    return Span<const Index>(strides_, rank_);
  }

  constexpr Index operator[](Index axis) const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(axis, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LT(axis, rank_);
    return strides_[axis];
  }

  constexpr Index& operator[](Index axis) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_GE(axis, 0);
    L3_DCHECK_LT(axis, rank_);
    return strides_[axis];
  }

 private:
  Index strides_[kMaxTensorDims];
  Index rank_;
};

constexpr bool operator==(const Strides& a, const Strides& b) noexcept {
  if (a.rank() != b.rank()) return false;
  for (Index i = 0; i < a.rank(); ++i) {
    if (a[i] != b[i]) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

constexpr bool operator!=(const Strides& a, const Strides& b) noexcept {
  return !(a == b);
}

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Layout, contiguity, broadcasting, and sub-byte block validation
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Returns true if `(shape, strides)` represents a dense, row-major contiguous
// layout with no gaps between elements.
L3_API bool is_contiguous_layout(const Shape& shape,
                                 const Strides& strides) noexcept;

// Validates that `(shape, strides)` is legal for `dtype`:
//   * Rejects negative strides on all axes.
//   * For sub-byte packed DTypes (`kTernary2`, `kInt4`, `kUInt4`), the last
//     dimension must be a multiple of `dtype_block_elements(dtype)` (even when
//     an outer dimension is 0), the innermost stride must be 1, and all outer
//     strides must be multiples of `dtype_block_elements(dtype)`.
L3_API Status validate_layout_for_dtype(const Shape& shape,
                                        const Strides& strides,
                                        DType dtype) noexcept;

// NumPy / PyTorch broadcast rule: computes the broadcast output shape of `a`
// and `b`, or returns `kShapeMismatch` if incompatible.
L3_API Result<Shape> broadcast_shapes(const Shape& a, const Shape& b) noexcept;

// Computes the effective broadcast strides (setting stride = 0 along axes where
// `src_shape` had size 1 and was stretched to `target_shape[axis] != 1`).
L3_API Result<Strides> broadcast_strides(const Shape& src_shape,
                                         const Strides& src_strides,
                                         const Shape& target_shape) noexcept;

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/tensor/storage.h ==========
// log3/tensor/storage.h
//
// Storage: intrusive reference-counted byte buffer backing one or more Tensors.
//
// Three ownership modes (unified under a single branchless data pointer):
//   1. kOwnedAllocator : Allocated from a `l3::Allocator` (default_allocator()
//                        or an `Arena`), aligned to `kDefaultAlignment` (64 B).
//                        Freed automatically when the last `Ptr<Storage>`
//                        releases.
//   2. kSharedMMap     : Zero-copy slice inside a shared `RefCounted` MMapFile
//                        holder (`MMapHolder`), keeping the OS file mapping
//                        alive for as long as any Tensor views its weights!
//   3. kExternalBorrowed: Non-owning view of caller-managed memory (e.g., a
//                        NumPy/PyTorch tensor buffer passed across the C API or
//                        a stack scratch buffer).
//
// Note on Allocator storage inside `Storage`:
//   `Storage` stores its `data_allocator_` by VALUE (not by pointer) because
//   `l3::Allocator` is a trivially copyable 32-byte struct (`Arena::allocator()`
//   returns a temporary `Allocator` by value). Only the `RefCounted` object
//   header itself is allocated via `default_allocator()`, which has static
//   program-wide lifetime, while the tensor payload buffer `data_` is allocated
//   and freed through `data_allocator_`.
//
// Compatibility:
//   * C++17, header-only, no exceptions, no RTTI.

#pragma once

#include <utility>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/memory.h"
#include "log3/core/mmap.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/ptr.h"
#include "log3/core/span.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"

namespace l3 {

// Intrusive RefCounted wrapper around MMapFile so multiple Tensor Storages can
// share sub-slices of the same memory-mapped model file safely.
class MMapHolder final : public RefCounted {
 public:
  explicit MMapHolder(MMapFile&& file) noexcept : file_(std::move(file)) {}

  const MMapFile& file() const noexcept { return file_; }
  MMapFile& file() noexcept { return file_; }

 private:
  MMapFile file_;
};

class Storage final : public RefCounted {
 public:
  enum class Kind : u8 {
    kOwnedAllocator = 0,
    kSharedMMap = 1,
    kExternalBorrowed = 2,
  };

  // --- factory methods -------------------------------------------------------

  // Allocate `size_bytes` of 64-byte-aligned memory from `allocator`.
  // If `zero_init` is true, zeros the buffer.
  static Result<Ptr<Storage>> allocate(
      Size size_bytes, bool zero_init = false,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!allocator.is_valid())) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                           "Storage::allocate: allocator is invalid");
    }

    byte* raw = nullptr;
    if (size_bytes > 0) {
      raw = static_cast<byte*>(
          allocator.allocate(size_bytes, kDefaultAlignment));
      if (L3_UNLIKELY(raw == nullptr)) {
        return Status::errorf(
            StatusCode::kOutOfMemory,
            "Storage::allocate: failed to allocate %zu bytes from '%s'",
            size_bytes, allocator.name != nullptr ? allocator.name : "?");
      }
      if (zero_init) {
        mem_zero(raw, size_bytes);
      }
    }

    // Allocate the small Storage control object via default_allocator() (which
    // has static lifetime, satisfying RefCounted's requirement that its header
    // allocator pointer outlive the object even when `allocator` was a
    // temporary struct returned by `arena.allocator()`).
    auto res = make_ptr_in<Storage>(default_allocator(), raw, size_bytes,
                                    Kind::kOwnedAllocator, /*mutable=*/true,
                                    &allocator, Ptr<MMapHolder>());
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!res.is_ok())) {
      if (raw != nullptr) {
        allocator.deallocate(raw, size_bytes, kDefaultAlignment);
      }
      return res.take_status();
    }
    return res;
  }

  // Create a read-only zero-copy Storage backed by a slice `[offset, offset +
  // length)` of a shared `MMapHolder`.
  static Result<Ptr<Storage>> from_mmap(
      const Ptr<MMapHolder>& holder, Size offset, Size length,
      const Allocator& meta_allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(!holder || !holder->file().is_open())) {
      return Status::error(StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
                           "Storage::from_mmap: MMapHolder is null or closed");
    }
    ConstByteSpan slice;
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(slice, holder->file().slice(offset, length));

    return make_ptr_in<Storage>(
        default_allocator(), const_cast<byte*>(slice.data()), length,
        Kind::kSharedMMap, /*mutable=*/false, &meta_allocator, holder);
  }

  // Wrap an external mutable buffer without taking ownership.
  // The caller guarantees `data` outlives all Tensors referencing this Storage.
  static Result<Ptr<Storage>> from_external(
      void* data, Size size_bytes, bool is_mutable = true,
      const Allocator& meta_allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    if (L3_UNLIKELY(size_bytes > 0 && data == nullptr)) {
      return Status::error(
          StatusCode::kInvalidArgument,
          "Storage::from_external: data is null with non-zero size");
    }
    return make_ptr_in<Storage>(
        default_allocator(), static_cast<byte*>(data), size_bytes,
        Kind::kExternalBorrowed, is_mutable, &meta_allocator,
        Ptr<MMapHolder>());
  }

  // Wrap an external read-only buffer without taking ownership.
  static Result<Ptr<Storage>> from_external_const(
      const void* data, Size size_bytes,
      const Allocator& meta_allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    return from_external(const_cast<void*>(data), size_bytes,
                         /*is_mutable=*/false, meta_allocator);
  }

  // Public constructor for `make_ptr_in<Storage>` (prefer static factories).
  // Copies `*data_allocator` by value so temporary Allocator wrappers (such as
  // `arena.allocator()`) never dangle!
  Storage(byte* data, Size size_bytes, Kind kind, bool is_mutable,
          const Allocator* data_allocator, Ptr<MMapHolder> mmap_holder) noexcept
      : data_(data),
        size_bytes_(size_bytes),
        data_allocator_(data_allocator != nullptr ? *data_allocator
                                                  : default_allocator()),
        mmap_holder_(std::move(mmap_holder)),
        kind_(kind),
        is_mutable_(is_mutable) {}

  ~Storage() noexcept {
    if (kind_ == Kind::kOwnedAllocator && data_ != nullptr &&
        data_allocator_.is_valid()) {
      data_allocator_.deallocate(data_, size_bytes_, kDefaultAlignment);
      data_ = nullptr;
    }
  }

  // --- observers -------------------------------------------------------------
  const byte* data() const noexcept { return data_; }

  byte* mutable_data() noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(is_mutable_);
    return data_;
  }

  Size size_bytes() const noexcept { return size_bytes_; }
  Kind kind() const noexcept { return kind_; }
  bool is_mutable() const noexcept { return is_mutable_; }

  ConstByteSpan bytes() const noexcept {
    return ConstByteSpan(data_, static_cast<Index>(size_bytes_));
  }

  ByteSpan writable_bytes() noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(is_mutable_);
    return ByteSpan(data_, static_cast<Index>(size_bytes_));
  }

 private:
  byte* data_;
  Size size_bytes_;
  Allocator data_allocator_;  // Stored by value (32B) so temporary Allocators never dangle
  Ptr<MMapHolder> mmap_holder_;
  Kind kind_;
  bool is_mutable_;
};

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/tensor/tensor.h ==========
// log3/tensor/tensor.h
//
// Tensor: lightweight multi-dimensional array view over a ref-counted Storage,
// with first-class support for scalar DTypes (f32, f16, bf16, i8, i32, ...) and
// BitNet sub-byte quantized DTypes (kTernary2 "t2", kInt4 "i4", kUInt4 "u4").
//
// Design principles:
//   * Copying a Tensor (via `.view()` or move) never copies underlying data:
//     multiple Tensors share `Ptr<Storage>` with independent `(shape, strides,
//     byte_offset)`.
//   * Move-only by default (matching `SmallVec` and `Status`) so accidental
//     deep vs shallow copy confusion is impossible: call `.view()` for an
//     explicit zero-copy alias (O(1) ref-count bump) or `.clone()` for an
//     explicit deep contiguous copy.
//   * Built-in quantization scale metadata (`quant_scale()`): BitNet b1.58 and
//     INT4 weight tensors carry a per-tensor scalar scale (`f32`), so linear
//     layers and GEMM kernels have everything they need inside one Tensor.
//   * Zero exceptions, zero RTTI, 64-byte SIMD-aligned allocations by default.

#pragma once

#include <initializer_list>
#include <utility>

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/dtype.h"
#include "log3/core/half.h"
#include "log3/core/memory.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/ptr.h"
#include "log3/core/span.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"
#include "log3/tensor/shape.h"
#include "log3/tensor/storage.h"

namespace l3 {

class L3_API Tensor {
 public:
  // Default constructor creates an empty/uninitialized Tensor (`!is_valid()`).
  Tensor() noexcept
      : storage_(),
        shape_(),
        strides_(),
        byte_offset_(0),
        dtype_(DType::kFloat32),
        quant_scale_(1.0f) {}

  Tensor(Tensor&&) noexcept = default;
  Tensor& operator=(Tensor&&) noexcept = default;

  // Explicit `.view()` or `.clone()` instead of implicit copy constructor.
  Tensor(const Tensor&) = delete;
  Tensor& operator=(const Tensor&) = delete;

  ~Tensor() noexcept = default;

  // --- factory methods -------------------------------------------------------

  // Allocate a new contiguous uninitialized Tensor of `shape` and `dtype`.
  static Result<Tensor> empty(
      const Shape& shape, DType dtype = DType::kFloat32,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  static Result<Tensor> empty(
      std::initializer_list<Index> dims, DType dtype = DType::kFloat32,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    Shape s;
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(dims));
    return empty(s, dtype, allocator);
  }

  // Allocate a new contiguous zero-initialized Tensor of `shape` and `dtype`.
  static Result<Tensor> zeros(
      const Shape& shape, DType dtype = DType::kFloat32,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  static Result<Tensor> zeros(
      std::initializer_list<Index> dims, DType dtype = DType::kFloat32,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    Shape s;
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(dims));
    return zeros(s, dtype, allocator);
  }

  // Allocate a contiguous Tensor and fill all elements with `value` (supports
  // all non-sub-byte DTypes: bool, i8..i64, u8..u64, f16, bf16, f32, f64).
  static Result<Tensor> full(
      const Shape& shape, f32 value, DType dtype = DType::kFloat32,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  static Result<Tensor> full(
      std::initializer_list<Index> dims, f32 value,
      DType dtype = DType::kFloat32,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    Shape s;
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(dims));
    return full(s, value, dtype, allocator);
  }

  // Construct a Tensor view over an existing Storage with explicit shape,
  // strides, and byte offset. Validates bounds and sub-byte alignment.
  static Result<Tensor> from_storage(Ptr<Storage> storage, const Shape& shape,
                                     const Strides& strides, DType dtype,
                                     Size byte_offset = 0,
                                     f32 quant_scale = 1.0f) noexcept;

  // Wrap caller-owned contiguous memory as a zero-copy Tensor (no allocation).
  static Result<Tensor> from_blob(
      void* data, const Shape& shape, DType dtype = DType::kFloat32,
      bool is_mutable = true,
      const Allocator& meta_allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  static Result<Tensor> from_blob_const(
      const void* data, const Shape& shape, DType dtype = DType::kFloat32,
      const Allocator& meta_allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept {
    return from_blob(const_cast<void*>(data), shape, dtype, /*is_mutable=*/false,
                     meta_allocator);
  }

  // --- explicit copy / clone -------------------------------------------------

  // O(1) shallow copy: shares the underlying `Storage` with identical shape,
  // strides, byte_offset, and quant_scale.
  Tensor view() const noexcept {
    Tensor out;
    out.storage_ = storage_;
    out.shape_ = shape_;
    out.strides_ = strides_;
    out.byte_offset_ = byte_offset_;
    out.dtype_ = dtype_;
    out.quant_scale_ = quant_scale_;
    return out;
  }

  // Deep copy into a newly allocated contiguous Tensor. Works for both
  // contiguous and non-contiguous (strided/transposed/sliced) tensors.
  Result<Tensor> clone(
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) const noexcept;

  // If this Tensor is already contiguous, returns `.view()` in O(1); otherwise
  // returns `.clone(allocator)`.
  Result<Tensor> contiguous(
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) const noexcept {
    if (is_contiguous()) return view();
    return clone(allocator);
  }

  // --- metadata observers ----------------------------------------------------
  bool is_valid() const noexcept {
    return storage_ != nullptr && !shape_.empty();
  }
  DType dtype() const noexcept { return dtype_; }
  const DTypeInfo& dtype_meta() const noexcept { return dtype_info(dtype_); }

  const Shape& shape() const noexcept { return shape_; }
  const Strides& strides() const noexcept { return strides_; }
  Index rank() const noexcept { return shape_.rank(); }
  Index ndim() const noexcept { return shape_.rank(); }
  Index dim(Index axis) const noexcept { return shape_.dim(axis); }
  Index stride(Index axis) const noexcept { return strides_[axis]; }
  Index numel() const noexcept { return shape_.numel(); }
  Size byte_offset() const noexcept { return byte_offset_; }

  bool is_contiguous() const noexcept {
    return is_contiguous_layout(shape_, strides_);
  }
  bool is_mutable() const noexcept {
    return storage_ != nullptr && storage_->is_mutable();
  }
  bool is_quantized() const noexcept { return is_subbyte_dtype(dtype_); }

  // Byte size of the logical elements (valid when contiguous).
  Size nbytes() const noexcept {
    Size out = 0;
    const Status st = checked_dtype_bytes(dtype_, shape_.numel(), &out);
    L3_DCHECK(st.is_ok());
    L3_UNUSED(st);
    return out;
  }

  f32 quant_scale() const noexcept { return quant_scale_; }
  void set_quant_scale(f32 scale) noexcept { quant_scale_ = scale; }

  const Ptr<Storage>& storage() const noexcept { return storage_; }

  // --- raw & typed data pointers ---------------------------------------------
  const byte* raw_data() const noexcept {
    if (storage_ == nullptr || storage_->data() == nullptr) return nullptr;
    return storage_->data() + byte_offset_;
  }

  byte* raw_mutable_data() noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(is_mutable());
    if (storage_ == nullptr || storage_->mutable_data() == nullptr) {
      return nullptr;
    }
    return storage_->mutable_data() + byte_offset_;
  }

  template <typename T>
  const T* data_ptr() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_EQ(dtype_, dtype_of<T>());
    return reinterpret_cast<const T*>(raw_data());
  }

  template <typename T>
  T* mutable_data_ptr() noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK_EQ(dtype_, dtype_of<T>());
    L3_DCHECK(is_mutable());
    return reinterpret_cast<T*>(raw_mutable_data());
  }

  // Contiguous typed Span accessors (asserts contiguity and DType match in debug).
  template <typename T>
  Span<const T> span() const noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(is_contiguous());
    return Span<const T>(data_ptr<T>(), numel());
  }

  template <typename T>
  Span<T> mutable_span() noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(is_contiguous());
    return Span<T>(mutable_data_ptr<T>(), numel());
  }

  // --- zero-copy view transformations ----------------------------------------

  // Reshape a contiguous tensor to `new_shape` without copying data.
  // Fails with kShapeMismatch if `new_shape.numel() != numel()`, or
  // kFailedPrecondition if the tensor is non-contiguous.
  Result<Tensor> reshape(const Shape& new_shape) const noexcept;

  Result<Tensor> reshape(std::initializer_list<Index> new_dims) const noexcept {
    Shape s;
    L3_ASSIGN_OR_RETURN(s, Shape::from_dims(new_dims));
    return reshape(s);
  }

  // Swap two axes (`dim0` and `dim1`, negative indices supported) in O(1).
  Result<Tensor> transpose(Index dim0, Index dim1) const noexcept;

  // Permute all axes according to `perm` (length must equal `rank()`).
  Result<Tensor> permute(Span<const Index> perm) const noexcept;

  // Slice along `axis` in `[start, end)` with step 1 in O(1).
  Result<Tensor> slice(Index axis, Index start, Index end) const noexcept;

  // Insert a size-1 dimension at `axis` (in `[-rank - 1, rank]`).
  Result<Tensor> unsqueeze(Index axis) const noexcept;

  // Remove a size-1 dimension at `axis` (or remove ALL size-1 dimensions when
  // `axis == -999`).
  Result<Tensor> squeeze(Index axis = -999) const noexcept;

 private:
  Ptr<Storage> storage_;
  Shape shape_;
  Strides strides_;
  Size byte_offset_;
  DType dtype_;
  f32 quant_scale_;
};

}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/nn/transformer.h ==========
// log3/nn/transformer.h
//
// Complete BitNet b1.58 Transformer model architecture:
//   * BitNetFeedForward : SwiGLU FFN with BitLinear w1 (gate), w2 (down), w3 (up)
//   * TransformerBlock  : Pre-RMSNorm -> GQA Attention -> Residual -> Pre-RMSNorm -> FFN -> Residual
//   * BitNetTransformer : Embedding -> N Blocks -> Final RMSNorm -> LM Head -> Token Generation
//
// Compatibility:
//   * C++17, no exceptions, no RTTI.

#pragma once

#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"
#include "log3/nn/attention.h"
#include "log3/nn/layers.h"
#include "log3/nn/module.h"
#include "log3/tensor/tensor.h"

namespace l3 {
namespace nn {

struct TransformerConfig {
  Index vocab_size = 32000;
  Index dim = 512;
  Index hidden_dim = 1376;
  Index num_layers = 4;
  Index num_heads = 8;
  Index num_kv_heads = 4;
  Index max_seq_len = 2048;
  f32 norm_eps = 1e-5f;
  f32 rope_theta = 10000.0f;
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// BitNet SwiGLU Feed-Forward Network (FFN)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API BitNetFeedForward final : public Module {
 public:
  BitNetFeedForward() noexcept : dim_(0), hidden_dim_(0) {}

  static Result<BitNetFeedForward> create(
      Index dim, Index hidden_dim,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  BitLinear& w1() noexcept { return w1_; }  // gate: dim -> hidden_dim
  BitLinear& w2() noexcept { return w2_; }  // down: hidden_dim -> dim
  BitLinear& w3() noexcept { return w3_; }  // up:   dim -> hidden_dim

  // Forward pass: y = w2(silu(w1(x)) * w3(x))
  Status forward(const Tensor& x, Tensor* out,
                 ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index dim_;
  Index hidden_dim_;
  BitLinear w1_;
  BitLinear w2_;
  BitLinear w3_;
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// TransformerBlock
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API TransformerBlock final : public Module {
 public:
  TransformerBlock() noexcept : layer_id_(0) {}

  static Result<TransformerBlock> create(
      Index layer_id, const TransformerConfig& cfg,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  RMSNorm& attn_norm() noexcept { return attn_norm_; }
  GroupedQueryAttention& attn() noexcept { return attn_; }
  RMSNorm& ffn_norm() noexcept { return ffn_norm_; }
  BitNetFeedForward& ffn() noexcept { return ffn_; }

  Status forward(const Tensor& x, Tensor* out, KVCache* cache = nullptr,
                 ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index layer_id_;
  RMSNorm attn_norm_;
  GroupedQueryAttention attn_;
  RMSNorm ffn_norm_;
  BitNetFeedForward ffn_;
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// BitNetTransformer: End-to-end 1.58-bit Language Model
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API BitNetTransformer final : public Module {
 public:
  BitNetTransformer() noexcept {}

  static Result<BitNetTransformer> create(
      const TransformerConfig& cfg,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  const TransformerConfig& config() const noexcept { return cfg_; }
  Embedding& tok_embeddings() noexcept { return tok_embeddings_; }
  TransformerBlock& block(Index i) noexcept { return blocks_[i]; }
  RMSNorm& norm() noexcept { return norm_; }
  BitLinear& output() noexcept { return output_; }

  // Forward pass: token sequence -> next token logits (shape: seq_len, vocab_size)
  Status forward(Span<const Index> tokens, Tensor* logits,
                 Span<KVCache> caches = {},
                 ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) const noexcept;

  // Single-token generation step: given prompt tokens, generates `max_new_tokens`
  Status generate(Span<const Index> prompt, Index max_new_tokens,
                  SmallVec<Index, 128>* out_tokens, f32 temperature = 0.0f,
                  ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) const noexcept;

 private:
  TransformerConfig cfg_;
  Embedding tok_embeddings_;
  SmallVec<TransformerBlock, 32> blocks_;
  RMSNorm norm_;
  BitLinear output_;
};

}  // namespace nn
}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/nn/attention.h ==========
// log3/nn/attention.h
//
// High-performance BitNet Grouped-Query Attention (GQA / MHA / MQA) with
// zero-copy pre-allocated KV-Cache.
//
// Key Features:
//   * Supports Multi-Head Attention (num_kv_heads == num_q_heads), Multi-Query
//     Attention (num_kv_heads == 1), and Grouped-Query Attention (GQA).
//   * Projections (wq, wk, wv, wo) are BitLinear b1.58 layers (pure integer
//     additions/subtractions during GEMV/GEMM).
//   * Dynamic KVCache manages past key-value states token-by-token with zero
//     reallocations during autoregressive decoding.
//
// Compatibility:
//   * C++17, no exceptions, no RTTI.

#pragma once

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"
#include "log3/nn/layers.h"
#include "log3/nn/module.h"
#include "log3/tensor/tensor.h"

namespace l3 {
namespace nn {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// KVCache: circular / sliding sequence buffer for Key and Value activations
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API KVCache {
 public:
  KVCache() noexcept : max_seq_len_(0), num_kv_heads_(0), head_dim_(0), cur_len_(0) {}

  static Result<KVCache> create(
      Index max_seq_len, Index num_kv_heads, Index head_dim,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  void reset() noexcept { cur_len_ = 0; }

  Index current_length() const noexcept { return cur_len_; }
  Index max_length() const noexcept { return max_seq_len_; }
  Index num_kv_heads() const noexcept { return num_kv_heads_; }
  Index head_dim() const noexcept { return head_dim_; }

  Tensor& key_cache() noexcept { return k_cache_; }
  const Tensor& key_cache() const noexcept { return k_cache_; }
  Tensor& value_cache() noexcept { return v_cache_; }
  const Tensor& value_cache() const noexcept { return v_cache_; }

  // Append new key/value tokens of shape `(new_tokens, num_kv_heads, head_dim)`.
  Status append(const Tensor& new_k, const Tensor& new_v) noexcept;

 private:
  Index max_seq_len_;
  Index num_kv_heads_;
  Index head_dim_;
  Index cur_len_;
  Tensor k_cache_;  // shape: (max_seq_len, num_kv_heads, head_dim)
  Tensor v_cache_;  // shape: (max_seq_len, num_kv_heads, head_dim)
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// GroupedQueryAttention (GQA) with BitLinear Projections
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API GroupedQueryAttention final : public Module {
 public:
  GroupedQueryAttention() noexcept
      : dim_(0), num_heads_(0), num_kv_heads_(0), head_dim_(0) {}

  static Result<GroupedQueryAttention> create(
      Index dim, Index num_heads, Index num_kv_heads,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  Index dim() const noexcept { return dim_; }
  Index num_heads() const noexcept { return num_heads_; }
  Index num_kv_heads() const noexcept { return num_kv_heads_; }
  Index head_dim() const noexcept { return head_dim_; }

  BitLinear& wq() noexcept { return wq_; }
  BitLinear& wk() noexcept { return wk_; }
  BitLinear& wv() noexcept { return wv_; }
  BitLinear& wo() noexcept { return wo_; }

  RotaryEmbedding& rope() noexcept { return rope_; }

  // Forward pass:
  //   x      : (seq_len, dim)
  //   cache  : optional KVCache for autoregressive generation
  //   out    : (seq_len, dim)
  Status forward(const Tensor& x, Tensor* out, KVCache* cache = nullptr,
                 ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index dim_;
  Index num_heads_;
  Index num_kv_heads_;
  Index head_dim_;

  BitLinear wq_;  // (dim) -> (num_heads * head_dim)
  BitLinear wk_;  // (dim) -> (num_kv_heads * head_dim)
  BitLinear wv_;  // (dim) -> (num_kv_heads * head_dim)
  BitLinear wo_;  // (num_heads * head_dim) -> (dim)
  RotaryEmbedding rope_;
};

}  // namespace nn
}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/nn/layers.h ==========
// log3/nn/layers.h
//
// Core neural network layers:
//   * RMSNorm        : Root Mean Square Layer Normalization (Llama/BitNet)
//   * BitLinear      : 1.58-bit ternary linear projection layer
//   * Linear         : Standard FP32/FP16 dense layer
//   * Embedding      : Token lookup table
//   * RotaryEmbedding: Rotary Position Embeddings (RoPE) cache & operator
//
// Compatibility:
//   * C++17, no exceptions, no RTTI.

#pragma once

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"
#include "log3/kernels/cpu_kernels.h"
#include "log3/nn/module.h"
#include "log3/tensor/tensor.h"

namespace l3 {
namespace nn {

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// RMSNorm Layer
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API RMSNorm final : public Module {
 public:
  RMSNorm() noexcept : dim_(0), eps_(1e-5f) {}

  static Result<RMSNorm> create(
      Index dim, f32 eps = 1e-5f,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  Index dim() const noexcept { return dim_; }
  f32 eps() const noexcept { return eps_; }
  Tensor& weight() noexcept { return weight_; }
  const Tensor& weight() const noexcept { return weight_; }

  // Forward pass: y = rmsnorm(x, weight, eps).
  // x can be 2D (B, D) or 3D (B, S, D).
  Status forward(const Tensor& x, Tensor* out) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index dim_;
  f32 eps_;
  Tensor weight_;
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// BitLinear Layer (BitNet b1.58 ternary weights {-1, 0, +1})
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API BitLinear final : public Module {
 public:
  BitLinear() noexcept : in_features_(0), out_features_(0) {}

  static Result<BitLinear> create(
      Index in_features, Index out_features,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  Index in_features() const noexcept { return in_features_; }
  Index out_features() const noexcept { return out_features_; }
  Tensor& weight() noexcept { return weight_; }
  const Tensor& weight() const noexcept { return weight_; }

  // Load FP32 weights and quantize them into BitNet b1.58 format in-place:
  Status load_weights_from_f32(const Tensor& w_f32) noexcept;

  // Forward pass: out = x @ weight^T.
  // Supports 2D (B, in_feat) and 3D (B, S, in_feat) activations.
  Status forward(const Tensor& x, Tensor* out,
                 ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index in_features_;
  Index out_features_;
  Tensor weight_;  // DType::kTernary2, shape: (out_features, in_features)
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Linear Layer (Standard FP32 dense layer)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API Linear final : public Module {
 public:
  Linear() noexcept : in_features_(0), out_features_(0), has_bias_(false) {}

  static Result<Linear> create(
      Index in_features, Index out_features, bool has_bias = false,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  Index in_features() const noexcept { return in_features_; }
  Index out_features() const noexcept { return out_features_; }
  Tensor& weight() noexcept { return weight_; }
  const Tensor& weight() const noexcept { return weight_; }
  Tensor& bias() noexcept { return bias_; }
  const Tensor& bias() const noexcept { return bias_; }

  Status forward(const Tensor& x, Tensor* out,
                 ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index in_features_;
  Index out_features_;
  bool has_bias_;
  Tensor weight_;  // shape: (out_features, in_features)
  Tensor bias_;    // shape: (out_features)
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Embedding Layer (Token lookup table)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API Embedding final : public Module {
 public:
  Embedding() noexcept : vocab_size_(0), embedding_dim_(0) {}

  static Result<Embedding> create(
      Index vocab_size, Index embedding_dim,
      const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

  Index vocab_size() const noexcept { return vocab_size_; }
  Index embedding_dim() const noexcept { return embedding_dim_; }
  Tensor& weight() noexcept { return weight_; }
  const Tensor& weight() const noexcept { return weight_; }

  // Forward lookup: input token ids (Index array of length `seq_len`) ->
  // `out` of shape `(seq_len, embedding_dim)`.
  Status forward(Span<const Index> tokens, Tensor* out) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index vocab_size_;
  Index embedding_dim_;
  Tensor weight_;  // shape: (vocab_size, embedding_dim)
};

// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Rotary Embedding (RoPE)
// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
class L3_API RotaryEmbedding final : public Module {
 public:
  RotaryEmbedding() noexcept : head_dim_(0), theta_base_(10000.0f) {}

  static Result<RotaryEmbedding> create(Index head_dim,
                                        f32 theta_base = 10000.0f) noexcept;

  Index head_dim() const noexcept { return head_dim_; }

  // In-place rotary embedding applied to Q or K of shape (seq_len, num_heads, head_dim)
  // starting at sequence position `start_pos`.
  Status apply(Tensor* qk, Index start_pos = 0) const noexcept;

 private:
  Index head_dim_;
  f32 theta_base_;
};

}  // namespace nn
}  // namespace l3


========== ./include/log3/nn/module.h ==========
// log3/nn/module.h
#pragma once

#include "log3/core/assert.h"
#include "log3/core/fn_ref.h"
#include "log3/core/platform.h"
#include "log3/core/small_vector.h"
#include "log3/core/status.h"
#include "log3/core/str_view.h"
#include "log3/core/types.h"
#include "log3/tensor/tensor.h"

namespace l3 {
namespace nn {

struct NamedTensor {
  const char* name;
  Tensor tensor;
  bool is_param;
};

class L3_API Module {
 public:
  Module() noexcept : training_(false) {}
  virtual ~Module() noexcept = default;

  Module(const Module&) = delete;
  Module& operator=(const Module&) = delete;

  Module(Module&&) noexcept = default;
  Module& operator=(Module&&) noexcept = default;

  bool is_training() const noexcept { return training_; }
  virtual void train(bool mode = true) noexcept { training_ = mode; }
  void eval() noexcept { train(false); }

  void register_parameter(const char* name, const Tensor& tensor) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(name != nullptr);
    named_tensors_.push_back(NamedTensor{name, tensor.view(), true}).ignore();
  }

  void register_buffer(const char* name, const Tensor& tensor) noexcept {
    L3_DCHECK(name != nullptr);
    named_tensors_.push_back(NamedTensor{name, tensor.view(), false}).ignore();
  }

  void for_each_parameter(
      FnRef<void(StrView name, Tensor& param)> visitor) noexcept {
    for (Index i = 0; i < named_tensors_.size(); ++i) {
      if (named_tensors_[i].is_param && named_tensors_[i].tensor.is_valid()) {
        visitor(StrView(named_tensors_[i].name), named_tensors_[i].tensor);
      }
    }
  }

  void for_each_parameter(
      FnRef<void(StrView name, const Tensor& param)> visitor) const noexcept {
    for (Index i = 0; i < named_tensors_.size(); ++i) {
      if (named_tensors_[i].is_param && named_tensors_[i].tensor.is_valid()) {
        visitor(StrView(named_tensors_[i].name), named_tensors_[i].tensor);
      }
    }
  }

  Index num_parameters() const noexcept {
    Index total = 0;
    for (Index i = 0; i < named_tensors_.size(); ++i) {
      if (named_tensors_[i].is_param && named_tensors_[i].tensor.is_valid()) {
        total += named_tensors_[i].tensor.numel();
      }
    }
    return total;
  }

 protected:
  SmallVec<NamedTensor, 16> named_tensors_;
  bool training_;
};

}  // namespace nn
}  // namespace l3

                                   u8* L3_RESTRICT dst_packed, Index count,

                                     f32* L3_RESTRICT dst, Index count,

    const Tensor& weights_f32,
    const Allocator& allocator = default_allocator()) noexcept;

                                      i8* L3_RESTRICT dst, Index count,
                                      f32* out_scale) noexcept;

L3_API i32 bitnet_dot_t2_i8(const u8* L3_RESTRICT w_packed,
                            const i8* L3_RESTRICT x_i8, Index count) noexcept;

L3_API f32 bitnet_dot_t2_f32(const u8* L3_RESTRICT w_packed,
                             const f32* L3_RESTRICT x_f32, Index count,
                             f32 w_scale) noexcept;

    const Tensor& x, const Tensor& w_t2, Tensor* out,
    ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) noexcept;

                   Index count) noexcept;

                    Index count) noexcept;

                        Index count, f32 eps = 1e-5f) noexcept;

                             f32 theta_base = 10000.0f) noexcept;

                         ThreadPool& pool = default_thread_pool()) noexcept;

}  // namespace kernels
